# Source Generated with Decompyle++
# File: detector.pyc (Python 3.11)

import glob
import json
import os
import threading
import time
import cv2
import numpy as np
AGF_TOOLS_DIR = os.path.expanduser(os.path.join('~', 'AGF'))
BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
SPRITE_ASSIGN_DIRS = (os.path.join(AGF_TOOLS_DIR, 'assignments'), os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'assets', 'sprite'))
FIELD_H = 651
CENTER = np.array([
    512,
    340], dtype = np.float32)
X_SCALE = 0.75
GATE_X = (40, 984)
GATE_Y = (40, 614)
CLICK_FRAMES = 10
COOLDOWN_LOOPS = CLICK_FRAMES - 1
SEARCH_EXPAND_FRAMES = 10
SPRITE_AVOID2_DELAY_SEC = 8.0

class SearchWindow:
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.age = 0
        self.was_main = False

    
    def update(self, is_main):
        if not is_main or not self.was_main:
            self.reset()
        self.was_main = is_main

    
    def advance(self, n=1.0):
        # n = elapsed time in 0.1 s units, so the search window grows at the same wall-clock
        # speed whatever the loop rate is (it was 1 per loop when the loop ran at 10 Hz)
        self.age = min(SEARCH_EXPAND_FRAMES, self.age + n)

    
    def reset(self):
        self.age = 0

    
    def scale(self):
        return 0.5 + 0.5 * self.age / SEARCH_EXPAND_FRAMES

    scale = property(scale)

AVOID_OVERLAP = 0.8
(FIRE_R_MIN, FIRE_RB_DIFF, FIRE_G_MIN) = (200, 90, 80)
(FIRE_SEED_R, FIRE_SEED_G, FIRE_SEED_B_MAX) = (245, 200, 190)
FIRE_ROI_DEFAULT = (0.5, 0.8, 28)
FIRE_BLOB_MIN = 40
FIRE_LOW_INTACT = 0.45

def fire_ambient(frame_bgr, box):
    (H, W) = frame_bgr.shape[:2]
    (x1, y1, x2, y2) = (int(v) for v in box)
    (w, h) = (x2 - x1, y2 - y1)
    patches = ((x1, y1 - int(h * 1.2), x2, y1 - int(h * 0.85)), (x1, y2 + int(h * 0.15), x2, y2 + int(h * 0.5)), (x1 - int(w * 1.0), y1 + h // 4, x1 - int(w * 0.2), y2), (x2 + int(w * 0.2), y1 + h // 4, x2 + int(w * 1.0), y2))
    fiery = valid = 0
    for px0, py0, px1, py1 in patches:
        (px0, py0) = (max(0, px0), max(0, py0))
        (px1, py1) = (min(W, px1), min(H, py1))
        if px1 - px0 < 8 or py1 - py0 < 6:
            continue
        valid += 1
        if fire_pixels(frame_bgr[py0:py1, px0:px1]) >= 16:
            fiery += 1
    return valid > 0 and fiery >= -(-valid * 3 // 4)

FIRE_REGION_TOP = 0.4
FIRE_REGION_PAD_X = 6
FIRE_REGION_PAD_B = 8

def fire_region(box, rect = None):
    (x1, y1, x2, y2) = (int(v) for v in box)
    if rect is None:
        return (max(0, x1 - FIRE_REGION_PAD_X), y1 + int((y2 - y1) * FIRE_REGION_TOP), x2 + FIRE_REGION_PAD_X, y2 + FIRE_REGION_PAD_B)
    (w, h) = (x2 - x1, y2 - y1)
    (rx0, ry0, rx1, ry1) = rect
    return (x1 + int(w * rx0), y1 + int(h * ry0), x1 + int(w * rx1), y1 + int(h * ry1))


def fire_pixels(roi_bgr):
    if roi_bgr.size == 0:
        return 0
    b = roi_bgr[:, :, 0].astype(np.int16)
    g = roi_bgr[:, :, 1].astype(np.int16)
    r = roi_bgr[:, :, 2].astype(np.int16)
    orange = (r > FIRE_R_MIN) & (r - b > FIRE_RB_DIFF) & (g > FIRE_G_MIN)
    core = (r > FIRE_SEED_R) & (g > FIRE_SEED_G) & (b < FIRE_SEED_B_MAX)
    if not core.any():
        return 0
    m8 = cv2.morphologyEx((orange | core).astype(np.uint8), cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, np.ones((3, 3), np.uint8))
    (n, lab) = cv2.connectedComponents(m8)
    if n <= 1:
        return 0
    valid = np.unique(lab[core])
    valid = valid[valid > 0]
    if len(valid) == 0:
        return 0
    return int(np.bincount(lab.ravel())[valid].max())


def roi_in_box(frame_bgr, box, roi = None, rect = None):
    (x0, y0, x1, y1) = fire_region(box, rect)
    return frame_bgr[max(0, y0):max(0, y1), max(0, x0):max(0, x1)]


def _subtract_sprite_alpha(observed, reference):
    obs = observed.astype(np.float32)
    src = reference[:, :, :3].astype(np.float32)
    alpha = reference[:, :, 3:4].astype(np.float32) / 255.0
    remaining = np.maximum(1.0 - alpha, 0.00392156862745098)
    blended = (alpha[:, :, 0] > 0) & (alpha[:, :, 0] < 1) & np.all((obs >= alpha * src - 3) & (obs <= alpha * src + 255 * (1 - alpha) + 3), axis = 2)
    low = (obs - alpha * src - 3) / remaining
    high = ((obs - alpha * src) + 3) / remaining
    background = np.stack((high[:, :, 0], low[:, :, 1], low[:, :, 2]), axis = 2)
    filtered = observed.copy()
    filtered[blended] = np.clip(background, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8)[blended]
    same = (alpha[:, :, 0] > 0) & (np.abs(obs - src).max(axis = 2) <= 3)
    filtered[same] = 0
    return filtered


def _fire_roi_without_sprite(frame_bgr, box, rect, sprite_bgra, sprite_alpha = False):
    crop = roi_in_box(frame_bgr, box, rect = rect)
    (bx0, by0, bx1, by1) = (int(v) for v in box)
    if crop.size == 0 or sprite_bgra.shape != (by1 - by0, bx1 - bx0, 4):
        return crop
    (rx0, ry0, _rx1, _ry1) = fire_region(box, rect)
    (rx0, ry0) = (max(0, rx0), max(0, ry0))
    (x0, y0) = (max(rx0, bx0), max(ry0, by0))
    x1 = min(rx0 + crop.shape[1], bx1)
    y1 = min(ry0 + crop.shape[0], by1)
    if x1 <= x0 or y1 <= y0:
        return crop
    reference = sprite_bgra[y0 - by0:y1 - by0, x0 - bx0:x1 - bx0]
    observed = crop[y0 - ry0:y1 - ry0, x0 - rx0:x1 - rx0]
    if sprite_alpha:
        filtered = crop.copy()
        filtered[y0 - ry0:y1 - ry0, x0 - rx0:x1 - rx0] = _subtract_sprite_alpha(observed, reference)
        return filtered
    same = (reference[:, :, 3] > 200) & (np.abs(observed.astype(np.int16) - reference[:, :, :3].astype(np.int16)).max(axis = 2) <= 3)
    if not same.any():
        return crop
    filtered = crop.copy()
    filtered[y0 - ry0:y1 - ry0, x0 - rx0:x1 - rx0][same] = 0
    return filtered


def box_has_fire(frame_bgr, box, roi = None, rect = None, sprite_bgra = None, sprite_alpha = False):
    crop = roi_in_box(frame_bgr, box, rect = rect) if sprite_bgra is None else _fire_roi_without_sprite(frame_bgr, box, rect, sprite_bgra, sprite_alpha)
    return fire_pixels(crop) >= FIRE_BLOB_MIN

DIGIT_ROI = (490, 225, 560, 335)
DIGIT_MATCH_MIN = 0.6
DUMMY_NEAR_DIST = 90.0
DUMMY_STACK_STOP = 6
DUMMY_RESET_LOOPS = 20
DIGIT_DIR = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), 'assets', 'digits')
_digit_tpl = None
_digit_dump_at = 0.0
DIGIT_DUMP_KEEP = 200
_digit_prune_busy = False

def _remove_files(paths):
    global _digit_prune_busy
    for f in paths:
        try:
            os.remove(f)
        except Exception:
            continue
    _digit_prune_busy = False


def _prune_digit_dumps(d):
    global _digit_prune_busy
    if _digit_prune_busy:
        return None
    
    try:
        files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(d, 'd_*.png')))
    except Exception:
        return None

    old = files[:-DIGIT_DUMP_KEEP] if len(files) > DIGIT_DUMP_KEEP else []
    if not old:
        return None
    if len(old) > 50:
        _digit_prune_busy = True
        threading.Thread(target = _remove_files, args = (old,), daemon = True).start()
        return None
    _remove_files(old)


def _digit_mask(roi_bgr):
    b = roi_bgr[:, :, 0].astype(np.int16)
    g = roi_bgr[:, :, 1].astype(np.int16)
    r = roi_bgr[:, :, 2].astype(np.int16)
    return ((r >= 190) & (g >= 90) & (g <= 210) & (b <= 130)).astype(np.uint8)


def _digit_templates():
    global _digit_tpl
    if _digit_tpl is None:
        _digit_tpl = { }
        for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(DIGIT_DIR, '*.png')):
            name = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(p))[0]
            img = cv2.imread(p, cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
            if img is not None and len(name) == 1 and name.isdigit():
                _digit_tpl[name] = (img > 127).astype(np.float32)
    return _digit_tpl


def read_top_number(frame_bgr, dump_dir = None):
    global _digit_dump_at
    tpl = _digit_templates()
    if not tpl:
        return None
    (x0, y0, x1, y1) = DIGIT_ROI
    if frame_bgr.shape[0] < y1 or frame_bgr.shape[1] < x1:
        return None
    roi = frame_bgr[y0:y1, x0:x1]
    mask = _digit_mask(roi)
    (n, lab, stats, _) = cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(mask, 8)
    keep = np.zeros_like(mask)
    for i in range(1, n):
        a = int(stats[i, cv2.CC_STAT_AREA])
        h = int(stats[i, cv2.CC_STAT_HEIGHT])
        if 25 <= a <= 500 and h <= 50:
            keep[lab == i] = 1
    if int(keep.sum()) < 60:
        return None
    m = keep.astype(np.float32)
    (best, score) = (None, 0.0)
    for name, t in tpl.items():
        s = float(cv2.matchTemplate(m, t, cv2.TM_CCOEFF_NORMED).max())
        if s > score:
            (best, score) = (name, s)
    if score >= DIGIT_MATCH_MIN:
        return best
    if dump_dir and time.time() - _digit_dump_at >= 5.0:
        _digit_dump_at = time.time()
        
        try:
            os.makedirs(dump_dir, exist_ok = True)
            cv2.imwrite(os.path.join(dump_dir, time.strftime('d_%Y%m%d_%H%M%S.png')), roi)
            _prune_digit_dumps(dump_dir)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return None


BOX_TL_BGR = (89, 193, 216)
BOX_TR_BGR = (84, 191, 215)
BOX_BL_BGR = (83, 193, 219)
BOX_BR_BGR = (86, 193, 215)
BOX_TOL = 10
MOB_PIXEL_BGR = (255, 255, 88)
MOB_PIXEL_TOL = 20

def _find_corner_pattern(frame_bgr, tl, tr, bl, br, tol):
    img = frame_bgr[:FIELD_H].astype(np.int16)
    (TL, TR) = (img[:-1, :-1], img[:-1, 1:])
    (BL, BR) = (img[1:, :-1], img[1:, 1:])
    mask = (np.abs(TL - tl) <= tol).all(2) & (np.abs(TR - tr) <= tol).all(2) & (np.abs(BL - bl) <= tol).all(2) & (np.abs(BR - br) <= tol).all(2)
    (ys, xs) = np.nonzero(mask)
    if xs.size == 0:
        return np.empty((0, 2), dtype = np.float32)
    return np.stack([
        xs,
        ys], axis = 1).astype(np.float32)


def find_boxes(frame_bgr):
    return _find_corner_pattern(frame_bgr, BOX_TL_BGR, BOX_TR_BGR, BOX_BL_BGR, BOX_BR_BGR, BOX_TOL)


def find_pixel_mobs(frame_bgr):
    return _find_corner_pattern(frame_bgr, MOB_PIXEL_BGR, MOB_PIXEL_BGR, MOB_PIXEL_BGR, MOB_PIXEL_BGR, MOB_PIXEL_TOL)


def _overlap_frac(mob, avoids):
    (mx1, my1, mx2, my2) = mob
    area = max(1.0, (mx2 - mx1) * (my2 - my1))
    best = 0.0
    for ax1, ay1, ax2, ay2 in avoids:
        iw = min(mx2, ax2) - max(mx1, ax1)
        ih = min(my2, ay2) - max(my1, ay1)
        if iw > 0 and ih > 0:
            best = max(best, iw * ih / area)
    return best


class MobDetector:
    
    def __init__(self, model_path, conf = 0.8, classes = ('mob',), imgsz = None, avoid_conf = None):
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.conf = conf
        self.avoid_conf = conf if avoid_conf is None else avoid_conf
        self.classes = set(classes)
        self.imgsz = imgsz
        self.model = None
        self.names = None
        self.ready = False

    
    def preload(self):
        from ultralytics import YOLO
        self.model = YOLO(self.model_path)
        self.names = self.model.names
        if self.imgsz is None:
            
            try:
                self.imgsz = int(self.model.ckpt['train_args']['imgsz'])
            except Exception:
                self.imgsz = 512

        dummy = np.zeros((self.imgsz, self.imgsz, 3), dtype = np.uint8)
        self.model.predict(source = dummy, imgsz = self.imgsz, conf = self.conf, verbose = False)
        self.ready = True

    
    def detect(self, frame_bgr, conf = None, fire_roi = None, with_conf = False, trust_avoid = None, fire_rect = None):
        empty = np.empty((0, 2), dtype = np.float32)
        empty_cf = np.empty((0,), dtype = np.float32)
        if not self.ready:
            return (empty, empty_cf) if with_conf else empty
        trust = fire_roi is None if trust_avoid is None else bool(trust_avoid)
        mob_th = self.conf if conf is None else conf
        avoid_th = self.avoid_conf if conf is None else conf
        rgb = cv2.cvtColor(frame_bgr[:FIELD_H], cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
        r = self.model.predict(source = rgb, imgsz = self.imgsz, conf = min(mob_th, avoid_th), verbose = False)[0]
        (cands, avoids, avoid2s) = ([], [], [])
        for box, cls, cf in zip(r.boxes.xyxy, r.boxes.cls, r.boxes.conf):
            name = self.names[int(cls)]
            xy = box.tolist()
            if name in self.classes:
                if float(cf) >= mob_th:
                    cands.append((xy, float(cf)))
                continue
            if name == 'avoid2':
                if float(cf) >= avoid_th:
                    avoid2s.append(xy)
                continue
            if name.startswith('avoid') and float(cf) >= avoid_th:
                if trust:
                    avoids.append(xy)
                    continue
                cands.append((xy, float(cf)))
        excl = avoid2s + avoids
        (out, out_cf) = ([], [])
        for box, cf in cands:
            (x1, y1, x2, y2) = box
            if excl and _overlap_frac((x1, y1, x2, y2), excl) >= AVOID_OVERLAP:
                continue
            (cx, cy) = ((x1 + x2) / 2.0, (y1 + y2) / 2.0)
            if fire_roi is not None and box_has_fire(frame_bgr, box, fire_roi, rect = fire_rect):
                continue
            out.append([
                cx,
                cy])
            out_cf.append(cf)
        coords = np.array(out, dtype=np.float32) if out else empty
        if with_conf:
            return (coords, np.array(out_cf, dtype=np.float32) if out_cf else empty_cf)
        return coords



class SpriteDetector:
    _FIRE_SPRITE_EXCLUDE = frozenset({
        'ghostjy_m',
        'ghostjy_n',
        'ghostpm_m',
        'ghostpm_n',
        'jiphaeng_m',
        'jiphaeng_n',
        'master_pil_m',
        'master_pil_n',
        'lord_kumchun_m',
        'lord_kumchun_n',
        'lord_shinnong_m',
        'lord_shinnong_n'})
    _FIRE_ALPHA_EXCLUDE = frozenset({
        'jiphaeng_n',
        'jiphaeng_m'})
    
    def __init__(self, agf_names):
        self.agf_names = list(agf_names)
        self.matcher = None
        self.frame_cls = { }
        self.names = {
            0: 'mob' }
        self.ready = False
        self._fire_sprites = { }

    
    @staticmethod
    def _load_assign(stem):
        for d in SPRITE_ASSIGN_DIRS:
            try:
                with open(os.path.join(d, stem + '.json'), encoding='utf-8') as fp:
                    return json.load(fp)['classes']
            except Exception:
                continue
        return None

    
    def preload(self):
        import sprite_match
        paths = sprite_match.resolve_agf_paths(self.agf_names)
        fire_sprites = { }
        skip = set()
        for p in paths:
            stem = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(p))[0]
            cls = self._load_assign(stem)
            if cls is None:
                continue
            self.frame_cls[stem] = { i: c for i, c in enumerate(cls) if c != 'mob' }
            skip |= { (stem, i) for i, c in enumerate(cls) if c in ('other', 'skip') }
        bg_dir = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'snapshots')
        bg_counts = None
        if not glob.glob(os.path.join(bg_dir, '*.png')):
            bg_dir = None
            hist = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'assets', 'sprite', 'bg_hist.npz')
            
            try:
                import numpy as _np
                z = _np.load(hist)
                bg_counts = dict(zip(z['colors'].tolist(), z['counts'].tolist()))
            except Exception:
                pass

        self.matcher = sprite_match.SpriteMatcher(paths, bg_dir=bg_dir, bg_counts=bg_counts, skip_frames=skip, log=lambda *a, **k: None)
        for p in paths:
            stem = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(p))[0]
            if stem not in self._FIRE_SPRITE_EXCLUDE:
                continue
            source = sprite_match.AgfFile(p)
            for i in range(source.nframes):
                if (stem, i) not in skip:
                    fire_sprites[stem, i] = sprite_match._trim(source.frame(i))
        self._fire_sprites = fire_sprites
        self.ready = True

    
    def fire_reference(self, tag):
        (stem, fr) = tag.rsplit(':', 1)
        mirror = fr.endswith('M')
        idx = int(fr[:-1]) if mirror else int(fr)
        reference = self._fire_sprites.get((stem, idx))
        return reference[:, ::-1] if reference is not None and mirror else reference

    
    def has_fire(self, frame_bgr, box, tag, roi = None, rect = None):
        return box_has_fire(frame_bgr, box, roi, rect = rect, sprite_bgra = self.fire_reference(tag), sprite_alpha = tag.rsplit(':', 1)[0] in self._FIRE_ALPHA_EXCLUDE)

    
    def detect(self, frame_bgr, conf = None, fire_roi = None, with_conf = False, trust_avoid = None, fire_rect = None, *, allow_avoid2 = False):
        empty = np.empty((0, 2), dtype = np.float32)
        empty_cf = np.empty((0,), dtype = np.float32)
        if not self.ready:
            return (empty, empty_cf) if with_conf else empty
        (cands, excl) = ([], [])
        for x, y, w, h, sc, tag, lowf in self.matcher.detect(frame_bgr):
            (stem, fr) = tag.rsplit(':', 1)
            mirror = fr.endswith('M')
            idx = int(fr[:-1]) if mirror else int(fr)
            cls = self.frame_cls.get(stem, { }).get(idx)
            box = (x, y, x + w, y + h)
            if cls == 'avoid2' and mirror and allow_avoid2:
                excl.append(box)
                continue
            if cls in ('other', 'skip'):
                continue
            cands.append((box, sc, lowf, tag))
        (out, out_cf) = ([], [])
        for box, cf, lowf, tag in cands:
            if excl and _overlap_frac(box, excl) >= AVOID_OVERLAP:
                continue
            if fire_roi is not None and self.has_fire(frame_bgr, box, tag, fire_roi, rect = fire_rect):
                # Fire ring at the mob's feet = another player/party is already fighting it.
                # With a tuned fire_rect that is decisive. (The decompiled condition used to
                # skip such a mob only when the fire was 'ambient', so engaged mobs were kept:
                # live 2026-11-19, 3 of 3 ringed 도사_귀 flagged, 2 of them still clicked.)
                if fire_rect or lowf < FIRE_LOW_INTACT or fire_ambient(frame_bgr, box):
                    continue
            out.append([
                (box[0] + box[2]) / 2.0,
                (box[1] + box[3]) / 2.0])
            out_cf.append(cf)
        coords = np.array(out, dtype=np.float32) if out else empty
        if with_conf:
            return (coords, np.array(out_cf, dtype=np.float32) if out_cf else empty_cf)
        return coords



def corrected_dist(coords):
    dx = (coords[:, 0] - CENTER[0]) * X_SCALE
    dy = coords[:, 1] - CENTER[1]
    return np.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)


# Screen areas that are HUD, never ground: a click there opens a window or presses a button
# (the 용병 portrait column opens that merc's 가방/status window, which freezes the macro).
UI_BLOCK_RECTS = (
    (0, 0, 100, 525),        # 용병 portraits + their HP bars
    (0, 0, 350, 90),         # top-left stat box (shown on hover) and portrait 1
    (100, 0, 665, 52),       # top toolbar icons
    (820, 0, 1024, 205),     # minimap
    (955, 355, 1024, 650),   # right-hand button column
    (0, 555, 335, 700),      # Alt+1 / Alt+2 quick slots
    (0, 640, 1024, 768),     # bottom HUD
)


def ui_blocked(x, y) -> bool:
    return any(x0 <= x < x1 and y0 <= y < y1 for x0, y0, x1, y1 in UI_BLOCK_RECTS)


def drop_ui_points(coords):
    """Remove detections that sit on the HUD so the next-nearest real mob is picked instead."""
    if coords is None or coords.shape[0] == 0:
        return coords
    keep = np.array([not ui_blocked(float(c[0]), float(c[1])) for c in coords], dtype=bool)
    return coords[keep]


class DummyGuard:
    """Decides when a mob that keeps being re-clicked is a dummy (cannot be attacked / unreachable).

    The old rule stopped the whole macro after 6 re-clicks with no digit read, which is about 6 s:
    a mob that is simply being walked to, or a digit the reader missed, ended the run (a fight often
    started seconds later). Now a mob must stay unanswered for MIN_SECONDS of real time AND
    MIN_CLICKS re-clicks. Then that spot is skipped for BLACKLIST_SEC and hunting goes on; only
    repeated blacklisting with no fight in between (ESCALATE within ESCALATE_WINDOW) stops it.
    Any fight start / digit read calls progress() and clears everything.
    """
    NEAR = DUMMY_NEAR_DIST
    MIN_CLICKS = DUMMY_STACK_STOP
    MIN_SECONDS = 15.0
    STALE_SECONDS = 3.0
    BLACKLIST_SEC = 45.0
    BLACKLIST_RADIUS = 45.0
    ESCALATE = 3
    ESCALATE_WINDOW = 180.0
    # On a crowded map (many players standing on the mobs) most clicks land on a mob that cannot be
    # attacked, so several spots get skipped in a row. The rule therefore never stops the macro: once
    # enough spots are skipped it clears the whole list and starts over, which keeps it clicking until
    # the countdown actually starts. A real disconnect is caught by the separate no-fight clock.
    RESET_AFTER = 4

    def __init__(self):
        self.spots = {}      # rounded (x, y) -> [clicks, first_t]
        self.last_key = None
        self.last_t = None
        self.black = []      # (x, y, expires)
        self.events = []     # times of blacklisting with no progress in between

    @property
    def clicks(self):
        """Re-clicks on the spot clicked last: the first click on a new spot is not a re-click."""
        v = self.spots.get(self.last_key)
        return v[0] - 1 if v else 0

    @property
    def first_t(self):
        v = self.spots.get(self.last_key)
        return v[1] if v else None

    def progress(self):
        """A fight started or the countdown digit was seen: the clicks worked."""
        self.spots = {}
        self.last_key = None
        self.last_t = None
        self.events = []

    def click(self, x, y, dist, now):
        """Register a mob click. Returns 'ok', 'suspect' (n-th unanswered re-click), 'skip' (skip this
        spot and keep hunting) or 'skip_all' (every skipped spot was cleared - retry everything).

        The counter is kept PER SPOT, not against the previous click: on a crowded map the nearest
        mob changes every time, and one shared counter that reset on every change never reached the
        limit, so nothing was ever skipped and the no-fight clock stopped the run.
        """
        if self.last_t is not None and now - self.last_t > self.STALE_SECONDS:
            self.spots = {}
            self.last_key = None
        self.last_t = now
        key = (int(round(x / 25.0)), int(round(y / 25.0)))
        self.last_key = key
        spot = self.spots.setdefault(key, [0, now])
        spot[0] += 1
        if spot[0] < self.MIN_CLICKS or now - spot[1] < self.MIN_SECONDS:
            return 'suspect' if spot[0] > 1 else 'ok'
        self.black.append((x, y, now + self.BLACKLIST_SEC))
        self.events = [t for t in self.events if now - t < self.ESCALATE_WINDOW] + [now]
        del self.spots[key]
        if len(self.events) >= self.ESCALATE and len(self.black) >= self.RESET_AFTER:
            self.black = []
            self.events = []
            self.spots = {}
            return 'skip_all'
        return 'skip'

    def filter(self, coords, now):
        """Drop detections on a skipped spot."""
        self.black = [b for b in self.black if b[2] > now]
        if coords is None or not self.black or coords.shape[0] == 0:
            return coords
        keep = np.ones(coords.shape[0], dtype=bool)
        for bx, by, _ in self.black:
            keep &= np.hypot(coords[:, 0] - bx, coords[:, 1] - by) > self.BLACKLIST_RADIUS
        return coords[keep]


def nearest_in_gate(coords, scale = 1.0, center = None):
    coords = drop_ui_points(coords)
    if coords is None or coords.shape[0] == 0:
        return None
    gx = (GATE_X[0] + GATE_X[1]) / 2.0
    gy = (GATE_Y[0] + GATE_Y[1]) / 2.0
    if center is not None and scale < 1.0:
        (gx, gy) = center
    hx = ((GATE_X[1] - GATE_X[0]) / 2.0) * scale
    hy = ((GATE_Y[1] - GATE_Y[0]) / 2.0) * scale
    mask = (coords[:, 0] >= max(GATE_X[0], gx - hx)) & (coords[:, 0] <= min(GATE_X[1], gx + hx)) & (coords[:, 1] >= max(GATE_Y[0], gy - hy)) & (coords[:, 1] <= min(GATE_Y[1], gy + hy))
    if not mask.any():
        return None
    d = corrected_dist(coords)
    real = np.flatnonzero(mask)[np.argmin(d[mask])]
    return (float(coords[real, 0]), float(coords[real, 1]), float(d[real]))


def click_cooldown(dist):
    return COOLDOWN_LOOPS

import ctypes
import ctypes.wintypes
import glob
import os
import time
import cv2
import numpy as np
from agf import AgfFile

try:
    ctypes.windll
    ctypes.WinDLL
except AttributeError:
    class _StubDLL:
        def __getattr__(self, name):
            return lambda *a, **k: 0
    class _StubLib:
        def __getattr__(self, name):
            return _StubDLL()
    ctypes.windll = _StubLib()
    ctypes.WinDLL = lambda *a, **k: _StubDLL()

(FIELD_W, FIELD_H) = (1024, 651)
MAX_MATCH_PX = 20000
CASCADE_K = 6
CASCADE_PREMIN = 1
DEFAULT_GAME_DIR = 'C:\\AKInteractive\\Gersang'

def find_game_dir():
    
    try:
        import win32gui
        import win32process
        found = []
        
        def _exe(pid):
            h = ctypes.windll.kernel32.OpenProcess(4096, False, pid)
            if not h:
                return ''
            
            try:
                buf = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(ctypes.wintypes.MAX_PATH)
                size = ctypes.wintypes.DWORD(ctypes.wintypes.MAX_PATH)
                ok = ctypes.windll.kernel32.QueryFullProcessImageNameW(h, 0, buf, ctypes.byref(size))
                ctypes.windll.kernel32.CloseHandle(h)
                return buf.value if ok else ''
            except:
                ctypes.windll.kernel32.CloseHandle(h)


        
        def _enum(hwnd, _):
            if not win32gui.IsWindowVisible(hwnd) or win32gui.GetWindowTextLength(hwnd) == 0:   # decompile dropped the not
                return None
            (_, pid) = win32process.GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd)
            p = _exe(pid)
            if p.lower().endswith('\\gersang.exe'):
                found.append(os.path.dirname(p))
                return None

        win32gui.EnumWindows(_enum, None)
        if found:
            return found[0]
    except Exception:
        pass

    if os.path.isdir(DEFAULT_GAME_DIR):
        return DEFAULT_GAME_DIR
    return ''


def resolve_agf_paths(names, game_dir = None):
    game_dir = game_dir or find_game_dir()
    if not game_dir:
        raise FileNotFoundError('거상 설치 폴더를 찾을 수 없습니다 (게임 실행 필요)')
    quick = [
        os.path.join(game_dir, 'Assets', 'Textures', 'Characters'),
        os.path.join(game_dir, 'Assets', 'Textures', 'Skills')]
    paths = []
    deep = None
    for name in names:
        stem = name.lower()
        hit = ''
        for d in quick:
            for ext in ('.AGF', '.agf'):
                p = os.path.join(d, name + ext)
                if os.path.isfile(p):
                    hit = p
                    break
            if hit:
                break
        if not hit:
            if deep is None:
                deep = { os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(p))[0].lower(): p for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(game_dir, '**', '*.agf'), recursive = True) }
            hit = deep.get(stem, '')
        if not hit:
            raise FileNotFoundError(f'''AGF 없음: {name} (게임 폴더 {game_dir})''')
        paths.append(hit)
    return paths


def _trim(s):
    a = s[:, :, 3]
    (ys, xs) = np.where(a > 0)
    if len(xs) == 0:
        return s
    return s[ys.min():ys.max() + 1, xs.min():xs.max() + 1]


def _enc(b, g, r):
    return b.astype(np.uint32) | g.astype(np.uint32) << 8 | r.astype(np.uint32) << 16


def _bg_color_counts(bg_dir, log):
    counts = { }
    files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(bg_dir, '*.png'))) if bg_dir else []
    for p in files:
        im = cv2.imread(p)
        if im is None:
            continue
        if im.shape[:2] == (768, 1024):
            im = im[:FIELD_H]
        e = _enc(im[:, :, 0], im[:, :, 1], im[:, :, 2]).ravel()
        (u, c) = np.unique(e, return_counts = True)
        for k, v in zip(u.tolist(), c.tolist()):
            counts[k] = counts.get(k, 0) + v
    if files:
        log(f'''[*] 배경 코퍼스 {len(files)}장, 고유색 {len(counts)}개''')
    return counts


class SpriteMatcher:
    
    def __init__(self, agf_paths, bg_dir=None, mirror=True, n_anchors=3, n_probes=28,
                 probe_tol=3, accept=0.6, min_bits=55.0, min_frac=0.3,
                 skip_frames=None, bg_counts=None, log=print):
        self.probe_tol = int(probe_tol)
        self.accept = float(accept)
        self.min_bits = float(min_bits)
        self.min_frac = float(min_frac)
        self.log = log
        t0 = time.time()
        if bg_counts is None:
            bg_counts = _bg_color_counts(bg_dir, log) if bg_dir else { }
        self._bg_total = max(1, sum(bg_counts.values()))
        self._bgq = np.zeros((32, 32, 32), np.float64)
        if bg_counts:
            ks = np.fromiter(bg_counts.keys(), np.int64, len(bg_counts))
            vs = np.fromiter(bg_counts.values(), np.float64, len(bg_counts))
            np.add.at(self._bgq, ((ks & 255) >> 3, (ks >> 8 & 255) >> 3, (ks >> 16 & 255) >> 3), vs)
        skip_frames = skip_frames or set()
        tpl = []
        for path in agf_paths:
            stem = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(path))[0]
            a = AgfFile(path)
            for i in range(a.nframes):
                if (stem, i) in skip_frames:
                    continue
                s = _trim(a.frame(i))
                variants = [
                    (s, f'''{stem}:{i}''')]
                if mirror:
                    variants.append((s[:, ::-1].copy(), f'''{stem}:{i}M'''))
                for im, tag in variants:
                    t = self._build_one(im, tag, bg_counts, n_anchors, n_probes)
                    if t is not None:
                        tpl.append(t)
        if not tpl:
            raise ValueError('유효한 템플릿이 없습니다')
        self.tpl = tpl
        self.w = np.array([ t['w'] for t in tpl ], np.int32)
        self.h = np.array([ t['h'] for t in tpl ], np.int32)
        (colors, items) = ([], [])
        for ti, t in enumerate(tpl):
            seen = set()
            for c, ax, ay in t['anchors']:
                (b, g, r) = (c & 255, c >> 8 & 255, c >> 16 & 255)
                for d in (0, -1, 1):
                    c2 = min(255, max(0, b + d)) | min(255, max(0, g + d)) << 8 | min(255, max(0, r + d)) << 16
                    if (c2, ax, ay) in seen:
                        continue
                    seen.add((c2, ax, ay))
                    colors.append(c2)
                    items.append((ti, ax, ay))
        colors = np.array(colors, np.uint32)
        items = np.array(items, np.int32)
        order = np.argsort(colors, kind = 'stable')
        (colors, items) = (colors[order], items[order])
        (uniq, start) = np.unique(colors, return_index = True)
        self.a_colors = uniq
        self.a_start = np.append(start, len(colors)).astype(np.int64)
        self.a_t = np.ascontiguousarray(items[:, 0])
        self.a_ax = np.ascontiguousarray(items[:, 1])
        self.a_ay = np.ascontiguousarray(items[:, 2])
        self.lutR = np.zeros(256, bool)
        self.lutR[uniq >> 16 & 255] = True
        log(f'''[*] 매처 빌드 {time.time() - t0:.1f}s — 템플릿 {len(tpl)}개, 앵커색 {len(uniq)}개, R-LUT 밀도 {self.lutR.mean() * 100:.0f}%''')
        return None

    
    def _cube_counts(self, b, g, r):
        t = self.probe_tol
        lo = [ np.clip(v - t, 0, 255).astype(np.int64) >> 3 for v in (b, g, r) ]
        hi = [ np.clip(v + t, 0, 255).astype(np.int64) >> 3 for v in (b, g, r) ]
        tot = np.zeros(len(lo[0]), np.float64)
        for ib in (0, 1):
            bb = hi[0] if ib else lo[0]
            mb = (ib == 0) | (hi[0] != lo[0])
            for ig in (0, 1):
                gg = hi[1] if ig else lo[1]
                mg = mb & ((ig == 0) | (hi[1] != lo[1]))
                for ir in (0, 1):
                    rr = hi[2] if ir else lo[2]
                    m = mg & ((ir == 0) | (hi[2] != lo[2]))
                    tot[m] += self._bgq[bb[m], gg[m], rr[m]]
        return tot

    
    def _build_one(self, s, tag, bg_counts, n_anchors, n_probes):
        (h, w) = s.shape[:2]
        if h < 25 or w < 20:
            return None
        interior = cv2.erode((s[:, :, 3] > 200).astype(np.uint8), np.ones((3, 3), np.uint8)).astype(bool)
        (ys, xs) = np.nonzero(interior)
        if len(xs) < 150:
            return None
        b, g, r = (s[ys, xs, 0].astype(np.int32), s[ys, xs, 1].astype(np.int32), s[ys, xs, 2].astype(np.int32))
        colors = _enc(s[ys, xs, 0], s[ys, xs, 1], s[ys, xs, 2])
        val = np.maximum(np.maximum(b, g), r)
        sat = (val - np.minimum(np.minimum(b, g), r)).astype(np.float64)
        distinct = (val >= 60) & (sat >= 25)
        if int(distinct.sum()) < 10:
            return None
        bgc = self._cube_counts(b, g, r)
        score = np.log2(bgc + 1.0) * 512.0 - sat
        min_d = max(6.0, (w * w + h * h) ** 0.5 / 4)
        anchors = []
        order = np.argsort(score)
        order = order[distinct[order]]
        top = ys < h * 0.4
        top_order = order[top[order]]
        if len(top_order):
            j = top_order[0]
            anchors.append((int(colors[j]), int(xs[j]), int(ys[j])))
        for j in order:
            if len(anchors) >= n_anchors:
                break
            if all(((xs[j] - ax) ** 2 + (ys[j] - ay) ** 2 >= min_d * min_d for _c, ax, ay in anchors)):
                anchors.append((int(colors[j]), int(xs[j]), int(ys[j])))
        if not anchors:
            return None
        di = np.nonzero(distinct)[0]
        n_d = min(n_probes // 2, len(di))
        sel_d = di[np.linspace(0, len(di) - 1, n_d).astype(np.int64)]
        sel_u = np.linspace(0, len(xs) - 1, min(n_probes - n_d, len(xs))).astype(np.int64)
        sel = np.unique(np.concatenate([
            sel_d,
            sel_u]))
        py, px = (ys[sel], xs[sel])
        p = (self._cube_counts(b[sel], g[sel], r[sel]) + 1.0) / self._bg_total
        wgt = np.clip(-np.log2(p), 1.0, 14.0).astype(np.float32)
        return {
            'w': w,
            'h': h,
            'tag': tag,
            'anchors': anchors,
            'px': px.astype(np.int32),
            'py': py.astype(np.int32),
            'pcol': s[py, px, :3].astype(np.int16),
            'wgt': wgt }

    
    def detect(self, frame_bgr, timings = None):
        pk = self._ensure_torch()
        if pk is not False:
            
            try:
                return self._detect_torch(frame_bgr, pk, timings)
            except Exception as e:
                self._torchpk = False
                self.log(f'''[매처] torch detect 실패 → numpy 폴백: {e!r}''')
            except:
                raise
                raise

        return self._detect_numpy(frame_bgr, timings)

    
    def _detect_numpy(self, frame_bgr, timings = None):
        f = frame_bgr[:FIELD_H, :FIELD_W]
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        m = self.lutR[f[:, :, 2]]
        (ys, xs) = np.nonzero(m)
        if len(xs):
            enc = _enc(f[ys, xs, 0], f[ys, xs, 1], f[ys, xs, 2])
            idx = np.searchsorted(self.a_colors, enc)
            idx_c = np.minimum(idx, len(self.a_colors) - 1)
            ok = self.a_colors[idx_c] == enc
            ys, xs, cid = (ys[ok], xs[ok], idx_c[ok])
            if len(xs) > MAX_MATCH_PX:
                sub = np.linspace(0, len(xs) - 1, MAX_MATCH_PX).astype(np.int64)
                ys, xs, cid = (ys[sub], xs[sub], cid[sub])
        else:
            cid = np.empty(0, np.int64)
        t1 = time.perf_counter()
        if len(cid):
            cnt = (self.a_start[cid + 1] - self.a_start[cid]).astype(np.int64)
            tot = int(cnt.sum())
            first = self.a_start[cid]
            cum = np.cumsum(cnt)
            item = np.repeat(first, cnt) + (np.arange(tot) - np.repeat(cum - cnt, cnt))
            ti = self.a_t[item]
            ox = np.repeat(xs, cnt) - self.a_ax[item]
            oy = np.repeat(ys, cnt) - self.a_ay[item]
            inb = (ox >= 0) & (oy >= 0) & (ox + self.w[ti] <= FIELD_W) & (oy + self.h[ti] <= FIELD_H)
            ti, ox, oy = (ti[inb], ox[inb], oy[inb])
            key = ti.astype(np.int64) * (FIELD_W * FIELD_H) + oy.astype(np.int64) * FIELD_W + ox
            (_, ui) = np.unique(key, return_index = True)
            ti, ox, oy = (ti[ui], ox[ui], oy[ui])
        else:
            ti = np.empty(0, np.int32)
            ox = oy = np.empty(0, np.int64)
        t2 = time.perf_counter()
        raw = []
        fi16 = f.astype(np.int16)
        order = np.argsort(ti, kind = 'stable')
        ti, ox, oy = (ti[order], ox[order], oy[order])
        bounds = np.searchsorted(ti, np.arange(len(self.tpl) + 1))
        for t in np.unique(ti):
            (s0, s1) = (bounds[t], bounds[t + 1])
            tp = self.tpl[t]
            (oxs, oys) = (ox[s0:s1], oy[s0:s1])
            if len(tp['wgt']) > CASCADE_K:
                topk = np.argsort(-tp['wgt'])[:CASCADE_K]
                Yp = oys[:, None] + tp['py'][topk][None, :]
                Xp = oxs[:, None] + tp['px'][topk][None, :]
                hp = (np.abs(fi16[Yp, Xp] - tp['pcol'][topk][None, :, :]).max(2) <= self.probe_tol).sum(1)
                keep = hp >= CASCADE_PREMIN
                if not keep.any():
                    continue
                (oxs, oys) = (oxs[keep], oys[keep])
            Y = oys[:, None] + tp['py'][None, :]
            X = oxs[:, None] + tp['px'][None, :]
            d = np.abs(fi16[Y, X] - tp['pcol'][None, :, :]).max(axis = 2)
            hit = d <= self.probe_tol
            frac = hit.mean(axis = 1)
            bits = (hit * tp['wgt'][None, :]).sum(axis = 1)
            sc = np.maximum(frac, np.minimum(bits / (self.min_bits * 1.5), 1.0))
            low = tp['py'] >= tp['h'] * 0.55
            n_low = max(1, int(low.sum()))

            frac_low = (hit & low[None, :]).sum(axis = 1) / n_low
            for j in np.nonzero((frac >= self.accept) | (bits >= self.min_bits) & (frac >= self.min_frac))[0]:
                raw.append((float(sc[j]), int(oxs[j]), int(oys[j]), tp['w'], tp['h'], tp['tag'], float(frac_low[j])))
        t3 = time.perf_counter()
        raw.sort(reverse = True)
        out = []
        for sc, x, y, w, h, tag, lowf in raw:
            (cx, cy) = (x + w / 2, y + h / 2)
            if all(((cx - (kx + kw / 2)) ** 2 + (cy - (ky + kh / 2)) ** 2 > 144 for kx, ky, kw, kh, _s, _t, _lf in out)):
                out.append((x, y, w, h, sc, tag, lowf))
        if timings is not None:
            timings.update(prefilter = (t1 - t0) * 1000.0, expand = (t2 - t1) * 1000.0, verify = (t3 - t2) * 1000.0, total = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0, cands = len(ti))
        return out

    
    def _ensure_torch(self):
        st = getattr(self, '_torchpk', 'untried')
        if st != 'untried':
            return st
        
        try:
            import torch
            dev = 'cpu'
            
            A = lambda a, dt: torch.as_tensor(np.asarray(a, dt), device = dev)

            T = len(self.tpl)
            P = max((len(t['px']) for t in self.tpl))
            py = np.zeros((T, P), np.int64)
            px = np.zeros((T, P), np.int64)
            pcol = np.zeros((T, P, 3), np.int16)
            wgt = np.zeros((T, P), np.float32)
            low = np.zeros((T, P), bool)
            valid = np.zeros((T, P), bool)
            nprobe = np.zeros(T, np.float32)
            nlow = np.zeros(T, np.float32)
            tk_py = np.zeros((T, CASCADE_K), np.int64)
            tk_px = np.zeros((T, CASCADE_K), np.int64)
            tk_pcol = np.zeros((T, CASCADE_K, 3), np.int16)
            for i, t in enumerate(self.tpl):
                n = len(t['px'])
                nprobe[i] = n
                py[i, :n] = t['py']
                px[i, :n] = t['px']
                pcol[i, :n] = t['pcol']
                wgt[i, :n] = t['wgt']
                valid[i, :n] = True
                lw = t['py'] >= t['h'] * 0.55
                low[i, :n] = lw
                nlow[i] = max(1, int(lw.sum()))
                top = np.argsort(-t['wgt'])[:CASCADE_K]
                kk = len(top)
                tk_py[i, :kk] = t['py'][top]
                tk_px[i, :kk] = t['px'][top]
                tk_pcol[i, :kk] = t['pcol'][top]
            pk = {
                'dev': dev,
                'a_colors': A(self.a_colors, np.int64),
                'a_start': A(self.a_start, np.int64),
                'a_t': A(self.a_t, np.int64),
                'a_ax': A(self.a_ax, np.int64),
                'a_ay': A(self.a_ay, np.int64),
                'lutR': torch.as_tensor(self.lutR, device = dev),
                'w': A(self.w, np.int64),
                'h': A(self.h, np.int64),
                'py': A(py, np.int64),
                'px': A(px, np.int64),
                'pcol': A(pcol, np.int16),
                'wgt': A(wgt, np.float32),
                'low': torch.as_tensor(low, device = dev),
                'valid': torch.as_tensor(valid, device = dev),
                'nprobe': A(nprobe, np.float32),
                'nlow': A(nlow, np.float32),
                **{
                    'tk_py': A(tk_py, np.int64),
                    'tk_px': A(tk_px, np.int64),
                    'tk_pcol': A(tk_pcol, np.int16),
                    'tw': np.array([ t['w'] for t in self.tpl ]),
                    'th': np.array([ t['h'] for t in self.tpl ]),
                    'tags': [ t['tag'] for t in self.tpl ] } }
            self._torchpk = pk
            self.log(f'''[매처] torch(CPU) 가속 경로 활성 — 템플릿 {T}''')
            return pk
        except Exception as e:
            self._torchpk = False
            self.log(f'''[매처] torch 경로 비활성, numpy 사용: {e!r}''')
            return False


    
    def _detect_torch(self, frame_bgr, pk, timings = None):
        import torch
        dev = pk['dev']
        tol = self.probe_tol
        accept = self.accept
        min_bits = self.min_bits
        min_frac = self.min_frac
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        f = torch.as_tensor(frame_bgr[:FIELD_H, :FIELD_W], device = dev).to(torch.int64)
        mask = pk['lutR'][f[:, :, 2]]
        pts = mask.nonzero(as_tuple = False)
        if pts.numel() == 0:
            return []
        (ys, xs) = (pts[:, 0], pts[:, 1])
        enc = f[ys, xs, 0] | f[ys, xs, 1] << 8 | f[ys, xs, 2] << 16
        idx = torch.clamp(torch.searchsorted(pk['a_colors'], enc), max = pk['a_colors'].numel() - 1)
        ok = pk['a_colors'][idx] == enc
        (ys, xs, cid) = (ys[ok], xs[ok], idx[ok])
        if cid.numel() > MAX_MATCH_PX:
            sub = torch.linspace(0, cid.numel() - 1, MAX_MATCH_PX, device = dev).long()
            (ys, xs, cid) = (ys[sub], xs[sub], cid[sub])
        if cid.numel() == 0:
            return []
        cnt = pk['a_start'][cid + 1] - pk['a_start'][cid]
        tot = int(cnt.sum())
        if tot == 0:
            return []
        first = pk['a_start'][cid]
        cum = torch.cumsum(cnt, 0)
        item = torch.repeat_interleave(first, cnt) + (torch.arange(tot, device = dev) - torch.repeat_interleave(cum - cnt, cnt))
        ti = pk['a_t'][item]
        ox = torch.repeat_interleave(xs, cnt) - pk['a_ax'][item]
        oy = torch.repeat_interleave(ys, cnt) - pk['a_ay'][item]
        inb = (ox >= 0) & (oy >= 0) & (ox + pk['w'][ti] <= FIELD_W) & (oy + pk['h'][ti] <= FIELD_H)
        (ti, ox, oy) = (ti[inb], ox[inb], oy[inb])
        if ti.numel() == 0:
            return []
        key = torch.unique(ti * (FIELD_W * FIELD_H) + oy * FIELD_W + ox)
        ti = key // (FIELD_W * FIELD_H)
        rem = key - ti * (FIELD_W * FIELD_H)
        oy = rem // FIELD_W
        ox = rem - oy * FIELD_W
        fi16 = f.to(torch.int16)
        ncand = int(ti.numel())
        raw = []
        CHUNK = 16384
        for s in range(0, ncand, CHUNK):
            tic = ti[s:s + CHUNK]
            oxc = ox[s:s + CHUNK]
            oyc = oy[s:s + CHUNK]
            Yp = oyc[:, None] + pk['tk_py'][tic]
            Xp = oxc[:, None] + pk['tk_px'][tic]
            hp = ((fi16[Yp, Xp] - pk['tk_pcol'][tic]).abs().amax(dim = 2) <= tol).sum(1)
            kp = hp >= CASCADE_PREMIN
            if not bool(kp.any()):
                continue
            (tic, oxc, oyc) = (tic[kp], oxc[kp], oyc[kp])
            Y = oyc[:, None] + pk['py'][tic]
            X = oxc[:, None] + pk['px'][tic]
            g = fi16[Y, X]
            dd = (g - pk['pcol'][tic]).abs().amax(dim = 2)
            hit = (dd <= tol) & pk['valid'][tic]
            hf = hit.float()
            frac = hf.sum(1) / pk['nprobe'][tic]
            bits = (hf * pk['wgt'][tic]).sum(1)
            sc = torch.maximum(frac, torch.clamp(bits / (min_bits * 1.5), max = 1.0))
            frac_low = (hit & pk['low'][tic]).sum(1).float() / pk['nlow'][tic]
            keep = (frac >= accept) | (bits >= min_bits) & (frac >= min_frac)
            kj = keep.nonzero(as_tuple = False).flatten()
            if kj.numel():
                scj = sc[kj].tolist()
                flj = frac_low[kj].tolist()
                tij = tic[kj].tolist()
                oxj = oxc[kj].tolist()
                oyj = oyc[kj].tolist()
                (tw, th, tags) = (pk['tw'], pk['th'], pk['tags'])
                for a in range(len(scj)):
                    t = tij[a]
                    raw.append((scj[a], oxj[a], oyj[a], int(tw[t]), int(th[t]), tags[t], flj[a]))
        raw.sort(reverse = True)
        out = []
        for sc, x, y, w, h, tag, lowf in raw:
            (cx, cy) = (x + w / 2, y + h / 2)
            if all(((cx - (kx + kw / 2)) ** 2 + (cy - (ky + kh / 2)) ** 2 > 144 for kx, ky, kw, kh, _s, _t, _lf in out)):
                out.append((x, y, w, h, sc, tag, lowf))
        if timings is not None:
            timings.update(total = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0, cands = ncand)
        return out



===== FILE: ./run_gato.py (    8860 chars) =====
"""GATO launcher — preflight checks, then hand off to prism.main().

Windows entry point. Keep thin: everything here is fail-fast preflight; the
actual bot logic lives in prism.
"""
import os
import sys

GAME_EXE = "gersang.exe"
MIN_PY = (3, 11)


def _fail(msg: str) -> None:
    print("ERROR: " + msg)
    if os.name == "nt":
        # The packaged app has no console: show the reason instead of exiting silently.
        try:
            import ctypes

            ctypes.windll.user32.MessageBoxW(0, msg, "GATO", 0x10)
        except Exception:
            pass
    sys.exit(1)


def _check_python() -> None:
    if sys.version_info[:2] < MIN_PY:
        _fail(
            "Python %d.%d is too old. This program needs Python %d.%d or newer "
            "(install Python 3.11 64-bit)." % (*sys.version_info[:2], *MIN_PY)
        )
    if os.name != "nt":
        print("WARNING: this program is Windows-only; continuing for testing.")


def _check_admin() -> None:
    if os.name != "nt":
        return
    try:
        import ctypes

        is_admin = bool(ctypes.windll.shell32.IsUserAnAdmin())
    except Exception as e:  # pragma: no cover - Windows only
        print(f"WARNING: could not check administrator rights ({e}).")
        return
    if not is_admin:
        _fail(
            "Administrator rights are required (the bot sends keyboard/mouse "
            "input and reads other processes). Right-click and choose "
            "'Run as administrator'."
        )


def _check_game_window() -> None:
    if os.name != "nt":
        return
    try:
        import psutil

        found = [p for p in psutil.process_iter(["name"]) if (p.info.get("name") or "").lower() == GAME_EXE]
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"WARNING: could not scan running processes ({e}).")
        return
    if not found:
        _fail(
            f"The game window was not found. Start the game ('{GAME_EXE}') "
            "before starting the bot."
        )


# 거상 only ships 3 fixed windowed-mode resolutions, all 4:3 (confirmed from the
# game's own patch notes): 1024x768 (reference), 1280x960, 1440x1080. Every
# captured frame is scaled to the 1024x768 reference before any pixel/trigger
# check runs, so all three are safe automatically (pure uniform scale, same
# aspect ratio). Fullscreen "화면 맞춤" (fit-to-screen) stretches the content to
# the monitor's real aspect (usually 16:9), which breaks every pixel
# coordinate in this bot silently. Catch that here instead of misclicking.
SUPPORTED_RESOLUTIONS = ((1024, 768), (1280, 960), (1440, 1080))


def _check_game_resolution() -> None:
    if os.name != "nt":
        return
    try:
        import win32con
        import win32gui

        import capture  # noqa: PLC0415

        hwnd = capture.find_window()
        if not hwnd:
            return  # already covered by _check_game_window
        left, top, right, bottom = win32gui.GetClientRect(hwnd)
        w, h = right - left, bottom - top
        style = win32gui.GetWindowLong(hwnd, win32con.GWL_STYLE)
        is_popup_fullscreen = bool(style & win32con.WS_POPUP) and not bool(style & win32con.WS_CAPTION)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"WARNING: could not check game resolution ({e}).")
        return
    if is_popup_fullscreen:
        _fail(
            "The game is in fullscreen mode. This bot only works in windowed "
            "mode (ESC -> 일반 설정 -> 화면 설정 -> 게임 화면 방식 -> 창 모드), because "
            "fullscreen stretches the image and every button location would be "
            "wrong."
        )
    if (w, h) not in SUPPORTED_RESOLUTIONS:
        _fail(
            f"The game window is {w}x{h}, not one of the 3 supported sizes "
            f"{SUPPORTED_RESOLUTIONS}. In the game: ESC -> 일반 설정 -> 화면 설정 -> "
            "해상도 설정, pick 1024x768, 1280x960 or 1440x1080 (창 모드 only)."
        )


def _check_assets() -> None:
    try:
        import config  # noqa: PLC0415

        paths = set()
        for name in dir(config):
            v = getattr(config, name)
            if isinstance(v, str) and (os.sep in v or "/" in v) and any(
                v.lower().endswith(ext) for ext in (".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".bmp", ".onnx", ".pt", ".json")
            ):
                paths.add(v)
        missing = [p for p in sorted(paths) if not os.path.exists(p)]
        if missing:
            _fail("Missing data files (re-copy the program folder intact): " + ", ".join(missing[:10]))
    except ImportError as e:
        _fail(f"Could not load config.py ({e}); the program folder looks incomplete.")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"WARNING: asset check skipped ({e}).")


def _seed_settings() -> None:
    """First run: copy settings.default.json -> prism_settings.json (never overwrite)."""
    import shutil

    import config

    here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    live = os.path.join(config.BASE_DIR, "prism_settings.json")
    default = os.path.join(here, "settings.default.json")
    if not os.path.exists(live) and os.path.exists(default):
        shutil.copyfile(default, live)
        print("First run: created prism_settings.json from defaults.")


def _selftest() -> int:
    """`Gato.exe --selftest`: import every heavy dependency and run one model inference, write
    selftest.txt next to the app, exit 0/1. Proves a packaged build is complete without the GUI."""
    import time
    import traceback

    here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    # Log next to the exe so the build step can always read it; fall back to the user dir if the
    # program directory happens to be read-only. Written incrementally so a hard crash still leaves
    # evidence of how far the run got.
    log_path = os.path.join(here, "selftest.txt")
    try:
        open(log_path, "a", encoding="utf-8").close()
    except OSError:
        import config as _c

        log_path = os.path.join(_c.BASE_DIR, "selftest.txt")
    log = open(log_path, "w", encoding="utf-8")

    out = []
    ok = True

    def emit(line):
        out.append(line)
        log.write(line + "\n")
        log.flush()

    def step(name, fn):
        nonlocal ok
        t0 = time.time()
        try:
            r = fn()
            emit("PASS %-22s %.1fs %s" % (name, time.time() - t0, r if r is not None else ""))
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            ok = False
            emit("FAIL %-22s %s: %s" % (name, type(e).__name__, e))
            emit(traceback.format_exc(limit=3).rstrip())

    emit("START %s %s" % (sys.version.split()[0], sys.executable))
    step("frozen", lambda: "yes" if getattr(sys, "frozen", False) else "no (running from source)")
    for mod in ("numpy", "cv2", "torch", "torchvision", "ultralytics", "win32gui", "win32api", "webview",
                "windows_capture", "psutil", "scapy.all", "websockets", "cryptography"):
        step("import " + mod, lambda m=mod: __import__(m) and None)
    for mod in ("config", "vision", "detector", "capture", "input", "combat", "brain", "prism"):
        step("module " + mod, lambda m=mod: __import__(m) and None)

    def _yolo():
        import numpy as np
        from ultralytics import YOLO

        path = os.path.join(here, "assets", "models", "jochul", "jochul.pt")
        m = YOLO(path)
        m.predict(source=np.zeros((512, 512, 3), dtype=np.uint8), imgsz=512, verbose=False)
        return os.path.basename(path)

    step("yolo model + predict", _yolo)

    def _assets():
        import config

        missing = [p for p in (config.JOCHUL_MODEL, config.TEXT_MODEL) if not os.path.exists(p)]
        if missing:
            raise FileNotFoundError(", ".join(missing))
        return "models present"

    step("assets", _assets)
    step("ui", lambda: os.path.getsize(os.path.join(here, "ui", "index.html")))
    emit("RESULT " + ("ALL PASS" if ok else "FAILED"))
    log.close()
    return 0 if ok else 1


def main() -> None:
    if "--selftest" in sys.argv:
        try:
            code = _selftest()
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
            import traceback

            sys.stderr.write(traceback.format_exc())
            code = 1
        sys.exit(code)
    print("GATO launcher: running preflight checks...")
    os.chdir(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
    _seed_settings()
    _check_python()
    _check_admin()
    _check_game_window()
    _check_game_resolution()
    _check_assets()
    print("Preflight OK. Starting bot UI...")
    try:
        import prism
    except ModuleNotFoundError as e:
        _fail(
            "A required package is missing: '%s'. Run: py -3.11 -m pip install "
            "-r requirements.txt --extra-index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu" % e.name
        )

    prism.main()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
===== FILE: ./dpi.py (     999 chars) =====
# Source Generated with Decompyle++
# File: dpi.pyc (Python 3.11)

import ctypes

try:
    ctypes.windll
    ctypes.WinDLL
except AttributeError:
    class _StubDLL:
        def __getattr__(self, name):
            return lambda *a, **k: 0
    class _StubLib:
        def __getattr__(self, name):
            return _StubDLL()
    ctypes.windll = _StubLib()
    ctypes.WinDLL = lambda *a, **k: _StubDLL()


def apply():
    
    try:
        u = ctypes.windll.user32
        u.SetProcessDpiAwarenessContext.restype = ctypes.c_bool
        u.SetProcessDpiAwarenessContext.argtypes = [
            ctypes.c_void_p]
        if u.SetProcessDpiAwarenessContext(ctypes.c_void_p(-4)):
            return None
    except Exception:
        pass

    
    try:
        ctypes.windll.shcore.SetProcessDpiAwareness(2)
        return None
    except Exception:
        pass

    
    try:
        ctypes.windll.user32.SetProcessDPIAware()
        return None
    except Exception:
        return None


apply()

===== FILE: ./wgc.py (    6983 chars) =====
# Decompiled with PyLingual (https://pylingual.io)
# Internal filename: 'wgc.py'
# Bytecode version: 3.11a7e (3495)

from __future__ import annotations
import ctypes
import ctypes.wintypes
import ctypes.wintypes as wt
import threading
import time
import numpy as np

try:
    ctypes.windll
    ctypes.WinDLL
except AttributeError:
    class _StubDLL:
        def __getattr__(self, name):
            return lambda *a, **k: 0
    class _StubLib:
        def __getattr__(self, name):
            return _StubDLL()
    ctypes.windll = _StubLib()
    ctypes.WinDLL = lambda *a, **k: _StubDLL()

user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
dwmapi = ctypes.windll.dwmapi
DWMWA_EXTENDED_FRAME_BOUNDS = 9
_import_err: str | None = None
try:
    from windows_capture import WindowsCapture
except Exception as e:
    WindowsCapture = None
    _import_err = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
def available() -> bool:
    return WindowsCapture is not None
def import_error() -> str | None:
    return _import_err
RENDER_SHIFT = ((-1), (-1))
def _client_box(hwnd: int):
    rc = wt.RECT()
    if not user32.GetClientRect(hwnd, ctypes.byref(rc)):
        return
    else:
        w, h = (rc.right - rc.left, rc.bottom - rc.top)
        if w <= 0 or h <= 0:
            return None
        else:
            org = wt.POINT(0, 0)
            if not user32.ClientToScreen(hwnd, ctypes.byref(org)):
                return
            else:
                ef = wt.RECT()
                if dwmapi.DwmGetWindowAttribute(wt.HWND(hwnd), DWMWA_EXTENDED_FRAME_BOUNDS, ctypes.byref(ef), ctypes.sizeof(ef)) != 0:
                    return
                else:
                    return (org.x - ef.left, org.y - ef.top, w, h)
class _Session:
    def __init__(self, hwnd: int):
        self.hwnd = hwnd
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self.frame = None
        self.frame_at = 0.0
        self.frame_mono = 0.0
        self.frames = 0
        self.started_at = time.time()
        self.error = None
        self.alive = True
        self._want_stop = False
        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True, name=f'wgc-{hwnd}')
        self.thread.start()
    def _run(self):
        try:
            cap = WindowsCapture(cursor_capture=False, window_hwnd=self.hwnd)
            @cap.event
            def on_frame_arrived(frame, ctl):
                received_at = time.monotonic()
                if self._want_stop:
                    ctl.stop()
                    return
                else:
                    buf = getattr(frame, 'frame_buffer', None)
                    if buf is None:
                        return
                    else:
                        bgr = np.array(buf[:, :, :3])
                        with self.lock:
                            self.frame = bgr
                            self.frame_at = time.time()
                            self.frame_mono = received_at
                            self.frames += 1
            @cap.event
            def on_closed():
                self.alive = False
            cap.start()
        except Exception as e:
            self.error = f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'
        finally:
            self.alive = False
    def stop(self):
        self._want_stop = True
    def client_frame(self) -> np.ndarray | None:
        sample = self.client_frame_info()
        return sample[0] if sample is not None else None
    def client_frame_info(self):
        box = _client_box(self.hwnd)
        if box is None:
            return
        else:
            ox, oy, w, h = box
            ox = max(0, ox + RENDER_SHIFT[0])
            oy = max(0, oy + RENDER_SHIFT[1])
            with self.lock:
                f = self.frame
                received_at, seq = (self.frame_mono, self.frames)
            if f is None or oy + h > f.shape[0] or ox + w > f.shape[1] or (ox < 0) or (oy < 0):
                return None
            else:
                return (np.ascontiguousarray(f[oy:oy + h, ox:ox + w]), received_at, seq)
_sessions: dict[int, _Session] = {}
_reg_lock = threading.Lock()
FIRST_FRAME_WAIT = 1.5
def _session(hwnd: int) -> _Session | None:
    if not available() or not hwnd or (not user32.IsWindow(hwnd)):
        _drop(hwnd)
        return
    else:
        with _reg_lock:
            s = _sessions.get(hwnd)
            if s is not None and (s.alive or s.thread.is_alive()):
                return s
            else:
                if s is not None:
                    _sessions.pop(hwnd, None)
                s = _Session(hwnd)
                _sessions[hwnd] = s
                return s
def _drop(hwnd: int):
    with _reg_lock:
        s = _sessions.pop(hwnd, None)
    if s is not None:
        s.stop()
def grab(hwnd: int) -> np.ndarray | None:
    # ***<module>.grab: Failure detected at line number 138 and instruction offset 76: Different bytecode
    s = _session(hwnd)
    if s is None:
        return
    else:
        f = s.client_frame()
        if f is None and s.frames == 0 and (time.time() - s.started_at < FIRST_FRAME_WAIT):
                    deadline = s.started_at + FIRST_FRAME_WAIT
                    while time.time() < deadline and s.frames == 0 and (s.error is None):
                        f = s.client_frame()
                    else:
                        time.sleep(0.01)
        if f is None and s.error:
                _drop(hwnd)
        return f
def grab_after(hwnd: int, after: float, timeout: float=0.25, stop=None):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(timeout))
    if stop is not None and stop() or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return None
    s = _session(hwnd)
    if s is None:
        return
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if stop is not None and stop():
                return
        if not s.alive or s.error or (not user32.IsWindow(hwnd)):
            return None
        sample = s.client_frame_info()
        if sample is not None:
            frame, received_at, seq = sample
            if seq > 0 and received_at > after:
                if (stop is None or not stop()) and time.monotonic() < deadline:
                    return frame
                else:
                    return None
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining > 0:
            time.sleep(min(0.01, remaining))
    return None
def stop_all():
    with _reg_lock:
        sessions = list(_sessions.values())
        _sessions.clear()
    for s in sessions:
        s.stop()
def prune(live_hwnds) -> None:
    keep = set(live_hwnds)
    with _reg_lock:
        dead = [h for h in _sessions if h not in keep or not user32.IsWindow(h)]
    for h in dead:
        _drop(h)
def stats() -> list[dict]:
    with _reg_lock:
        items = list(_sessions.items())
    now = time.time()
    return [{'hwnd': h, 'frames': s.frames, 'alive': s.alive, 'age_ms': round((now - s.frame_at) * 1000) if s.frame_at else None, 'error': s.error} for h, s in items]
===== FILE: ./watchdog.py (    3458 chars) =====
"""Standalone window-visibility watchdog, independent of the main bot.

Why this exists: on 2026-11-18/19 the Gersang game window went invisible
twice (window alive, process responding, but IsWindowVisible()==False --
looks exactly like "the game quit" from the user's side). The main bot's
own fix for this (brain.py _ensure_window_visible) could not be confirmed
to actually be running after a restart (no log output at all from the new
process), so this script is a dead-simple, independent safety net: it does
not import brain/prism/config at all, just win32gui + win32process, so
there is nothing else that can make it silently fail to start.

Run with: py -3.11 C:/Gato/watchdog.py
Logs to: C:/Gato/watchdog.log (plain UTF-8, flushed every write)
"""
import time
import win32gui
import win32process

LOG_PATH = r"C:\Gato\watchdog.log"
PROCESS_NAME = "gersang.exe"
POLL_SEC = 5


def log(msg):
    line = f"{time.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')} {msg}\n"
    with open(LOG_PATH, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(line)
        f.flush()
    print(line, end="")


def get_process_exe(pid):
    try:
        import win32api
        import win32con
        handle = win32api.OpenProcess(win32con.PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, False, pid)
    except Exception:
        return ""
    try:
        import win32process as wp
        return wp.GetModuleFileNameEx(handle, 0).lower()
    except Exception:
        return ""
    finally:
        handle.Close()


def find_gersang_window():
    result = []

    def cb(hwnd, _):
        if win32gui.GetWindowTextLength(hwnd) == 0:
            return
        _, pid = win32process.GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd)
        exe = get_process_exe(pid)
        if exe.endswith("\\" + PROCESS_NAME):
            result.append(hwnd)

    win32gui.EnumWindows(cb, None)
    if not result:
        return 0
    # Several helper windows share this process (GDI+ Window, DIEmWin) and
    # also have a non-empty title; only the one titled exactly "Gersang" is
    # the real game window with a real client rect -- observed live
    # 2026-11-19 toggling visibility on the wrong helper window repeatedly.
    exact = [h for h in result if win32gui.GetWindowText(h) == "Gersang"]
    return exact[0] if exact else result[0]


def main():
    log("watchdog started")
    last_state = None
    while True:
        try:
            hwnd = find_gersang_window()
            if not hwnd:
                if last_state != "not_found":
                    log("Gersang window not found (process may be closed)")
                    last_state = "not_found"
                time.sleep(POLL_SEC)
                continue
            visible = win32gui.IsWindowVisible(hwnd)
            if not visible:
                log(f"hwnd={hwnd} INVISIBLE -- restoring")
                import win32con
                win32gui.ShowWindow(hwnd, win32con.SW_RESTORE)
                win32gui.ShowWindow(hwnd, win32con.SW_SHOW)
                time.sleep(0.3)
                fixed = win32gui.IsWindowVisible(hwnd)
                log(f"hwnd={hwnd} restore result: visible={fixed}")
                last_state = "fixed" if fixed else "failed"
            else:
                if last_state != "ok":
                    log(f"hwnd={hwnd} visible=OK")
                last_state = "ok"
        except Exception as e:
            log(f"ERROR: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
        time.sleep(POLL_SEC)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

from __future__ import annotations
import os

import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

SCENE_POINTS = {
    'main': [
        (807, 676, (93, 45, 24))],
    'battle': [
        # vendor value. 77698a7 had put the MAIN screen's pixel here (73, 60, 44), so a
        # main frame matched both scenes; not yet re-measured in a real battle.
        (982, 722, (170, 54, 33))] }
SCENE_LABELS = {
    'main': '메인화면',
    'battle': '전투화면',
    'unknown': '알 수 없음' }
PIXEL_TOL = 10   # vendor value; live main pixel (99,42,25) is within it on every channel
# Exact pixel-sum signatures of popup text, as shipped by the vendor. 1530 = two pure-white
# pixels (255*3*2). 77698a7 replaced these with sums measured on the NORMAL screen and a
# +-60 tolerance, which made every trigger fire on an ordinary town frame; restored 2026-11-17.
