CHAT_HANGUL_XY = (43, 739)
WHISPER_FEATS = ((300, 117), (300, 294), (192, 230))
WHISPER_FEAT_SUMS = (460, 462, 463)
WHISPER_FEAT_TOL = 6
IMPLAUSIBLE_TRIGGER_COUNT = 4  # see check_triggers() -- this many at once means noise, not real popups

# Idle-kick warning dialog: '5분 뒤 캐릭터 선택 화면으로 이동됩니다' + single 확인
# button. This is the REAL mechanism behind "game auto-returns to login
# screen when idle" -- confirmed live 2026-11-19. It is a screen-centered
# modal (not camera/anchor-relative like the 2nd-password panel), always
# the same box, with exactly one button and no other interactive element,
# so detecting and clicking it is much safer than the old movement-based
# AFK nudge (which could land on map hotspots and open paid-action menus).
_ASSET_DIR = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), 'assets', 'dialog_templates')
_IDLE_WARNING_TEMPLATE_PATH = os.path.join(_ASSET_DIR, 'idle_warning.png')
_idle_warning_template = None
IDLE_WARNING_MATCH_THRESHOLD = 0.88
# button center, measured relative to the template's own top-left corner
IDLE_WARNING_CONFIRM_OFFSET = (250, 107)


def _load_idle_warning_template():
    global _idle_warning_template
    if _idle_warning_template is None and os.path.exists(_IDLE_WARNING_TEMPLATE_PATH):
        _idle_warning_template = cv2.imread(_IDLE_WARNING_TEMPLATE_PATH)
    return _idle_warning_template


def find_idle_warning(img: np.ndarray):
    """Returns (x, y) screen coords of the 확인 button if the idle-kick
    warning dialog is visible in img, else None. Pure template matching,
    no AI/model calls."""
    tpl = _load_idle_warning_template()
    if tpl is None or img is None:
        return None
    res = cv2.matchTemplate(img, tpl, cv2.TM_CCOEFF_NORMED)
    _, max_val, _, max_loc = cv2.minMaxLoc(res)
    if max_val < IDLE_WARNING_MATCH_THRESHOLD:
        return None
    bx, by = IDLE_WARNING_CONFIRM_OFFSET
    return (max_loc[0] + bx, max_loc[1] + by)

def _px_sum(img: np.ndarray, x0, y0, x1, y1) -> int:
    (h, w) = img.shape[:2]
    if x1 > w or y1 > h:
        return -1
    return int(img[y0:y1, x0:x1].sum())


def _point_match(img: np.ndarray, x, y, rgb, tol=PIXEL_TOL) -> bool:
    (h, w) = img.shape[:2]
    if not (0 <= x < w and 0 <= y < h):
        return False
    (b, g, r) = img[y, x]
    (er, eg, eb) = rgb
    # decompile dropped the red channel
    return abs(int(r) - er) <= tol and abs(int(g) - eg) <= tol and abs(int(b) - eb) <= tol


def is_scene(img: np.ndarray, name: str) -> bool:
    points = SCENE_POINTS.get(name)
    if img is None or not points:
        return False
    return all((_point_match(img, x, y, rgb) for x, y, rgb in points))


def detect_scene(img: np.ndarray) -> str:
    for name in SCENE_POINTS:
        if is_scene(img, name):
            return name
    return 'unknown'


def scene_probe(name: str):
    return SCENE_POINTS[name][0]


def px_is(px_bgr, rgb, tol: int = PIXEL_TOL) -> bool:
    # decompile turned this into `while not g-ok: return b-ok` (None whenever green matched),
    # and prism.py:611 calls it without tol
    (b, g, r) = (int(v) for v in px_bgr[:3])
    (er, eg, eb) = rgb
    return abs(r - er) <= tol and abs(g - eg) <= tol and abs(b - eb) <= tol


def check_triggers(img: np.ndarray) -> set[str]:
    fired = set()
    for name, regions in TRIGGERS.items():
        if all(_px_sum(img, x0, y0, x1, y1) == s for x0, y0, x1, y1, s in regions):
            fired.add(name)
    if len(fired) >= IMPLAUSIBLE_TRIGGER_COUNT:
        # Real triggers almost never co-occur more than 1-2 at a time. Seeing
        # this many at once (observed live 2026-11-18 during a Christmas-event
        # background: 5 fired simultaneously on an ordinary idle screen) means
        # seasonal/event background art is coincidentally matching the exact
        # pixel-sum signatures below, not that 5 real popups are all open.
        # Distrust the whole frame rather than act on noise -- this is what
        # was silently blocking the anti-AFK nudge and risked spamming wrong
        # key reactions (revive / eat / close-map) on a perfectly normal screen.
        return set()
    return fired


def chat_hangul_state(img: np.ndarray) -> str:
    (x, y) = CHAT_HANGUL_XY
    s = _px_sum(img, x, y, x + 1, y + 1)
    return {
        496: 'korean',
        0: 'english' }.get(s, 'inactive')


def whisper_is_open(img: np.ndarray) -> bool:
    return all(abs(_px_sum(img, x, y, x + 1, y + 1) - e) <= WHISPER_FEAT_TOL for (x, y), e in zip(WHISPER_FEATS, WHISPER_FEAT_SUMS))

SCENE_SIM_THRESH = 0.5
FIELD_H = 651

def scene_similarity(a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray) -> float:
    ha = cv2.calcHist([
        cv2.cvtColor(a, cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)], [
        0,
        1], None, [
        32,
        32], [
        0,
        180,
        0,
        256])
    hb = cv2.calcHist([
        cv2.cvtColor(b, cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)], [
        0,
        1], None, [
        32,
        32], [
        0,
        180,
        0,
        256])
    cv2.normalize(ha, ha)
    cv2.normalize(hb, hb)
    return float(cv2.compareHist(ha, hb, cv2.HISTCMP_CORREL))


def gm_chat_visible(img: np.ndarray) -> bool:
    a = img[:FIELD_H]
    tl, tr = (a[:-1, :-1], a[:-1, 1:])
    bl, br = (a[1:, :-1], a[1:, 1:])
    mask = (tl == 210).all(2) & (tr == 210).all(2) & (bl == 10).all(2) & (br == 10).all(2)
    return bool(mask.any())

@dataclass
class VisionState:
    scene: str = 'unknown'
    triggers: set = field(default_factory=set)
    chat: str = 'inactive'
    whisper: bool = False



class Vision:
    
    def __init__(self, max_events: int = 500):
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self.state = VisionState()
        self._events = []
        self._seq = 0
        self._max = max_events

    
    def attach(self, engine) -> None:
        engine.subscribe(self._on_frame)

    
    def _emit(self, kind: str, text: str) -> None:
        self._seq += 1
        self._events.append({
            'seq': self._seq,
            'kind': kind,
            'text': text,
            'time': time.strftime('%H:%M:%S') })
        if len(self._events) > self._max:
            del self._events[:len(self._events) - self._max]
        self._debug_log(kind, text)


    def _debug_log(self, kind: str, text: str) -> None:
        # Independent debug channel on top of the normal event buffer, which
        # only the live UI panel can read. This writes straight to a plain
        # file so the Windows PC's state is inspectable without a UI session
        # attached -- added 2026-11-18, lost once already when a vision.py
        # push from a stale local copy silently dropped it; keep this in the
        # repo, not just pushed ad hoc, so it survives future pushes.
        try:
            path = os.path.join(__import__('config').BASE_DIR, 'gato_debug.log')
            with open(path, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                f.write(f"{time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')} [{kind}] {text}\n")
        except Exception:
            pass

    
    def reset(self) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self.state = VisionState()

    
    def note(self, kind: str, text: str) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self._emit(kind, text)

    
    def events_since(self, seq: int) -> list[dict]:
        with self._lock:
            return [ e for e in self._events if e['seq'] > seq ]

    
    def snapshot(self) -> dict:
        with self._lock:
            st = self.state
            return {
                'scene': st.scene,
                'scene_label': SCENE_LABELS.get(st.scene, st.scene),
                'triggers': sorted(st.triggers),
                'chat': st.chat,
                'whisper': st.whisper,
                'seq': self._seq }

    
    def _on_frame(self, img: np.ndarray, frame_id: int) -> None:
        scene = detect_scene(img)
        fired = check_triggers(img)
        chat = chat_hangul_state(img)
        whisper = whisper_is_open(img)
        with self._lock:
            st = self.state
            if scene != st.scene:
                self._emit('scene', f'''화면 전환: {SCENE_LABELS.get(scene, scene)}''')
            for name in sorted(fired - st.triggers):
                self._emit('trigger', f'''감지: {name}''')
            for name in sorted(st.triggers - fired):
                self._emit('clear', f'''해제: {name}''')
            if whisper != st.whisper:
                self._emit('whisper', '귓말창 열림' if whisper else '귓말창 닫힘')
            (st.scene, st.triggers, st.chat, st.whisper) = (scene, fired, chat, whisper)




# ---- 불사신부 사용 확인 팝업 (용병/캐릭터 사망 시) -------------------------------------
# '불사신부 사용시 의원으로 가지 않고 그 자리에서 부활할 수 있습니다. 사용하시겠습니까?' + 확인/취소.
# Geometry measured 2026-11-19 from a client screenshot (1024x768 reference). Several other
# dialogs (e.g. paid travel) share the same two-button layout, so the BUTTONS alone are never
# enough to click: the message text must also match the stored text mask.
REVIVE_OK_BOX = (532, 434, 586, 449)
REVIVE_CANCEL_BOX = (604, 434, 658, 449)
REVIVE_GAP_BOX = (588, 434, 602, 449)
REVIVE_CLICK_XY = (558, 441)
REVIVE_TEXT_BOX = (380, 340, 640, 392)
REVIVE_TEXT_MIN = 0.6
_REVIVE_MASK_PATH = os.path.join(_ASSET_DIR, 'revive_text_mask.png')
_revive_mask = None


def _warm_fill(img: np.ndarray, box) -> float:
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = box
    reg = img[y0:y1, x0:x1].astype(np.int16)
    if reg.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    b, g, r = reg[..., 0], reg[..., 1], reg[..., 2]
    m = (r > 120) & (r < 190) & (g > 110) & (g < 170) & (b > 95) & (b < 150) & (r - b > 15) & (r - b < 60)
    return float(m.mean())


def _load_revive_mask():
    global _revive_mask
    if _revive_mask is None and os.path.exists(_REVIVE_MASK_PATH):
        m = cv2.imread(_REVIVE_MASK_PATH, cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
        if m is not None:
            _revive_mask = (m > 127).astype(np.uint8)
    return _revive_mask


def find_revive_popup(img: np.ndarray):
    """Returns ('ok', (x, y)) when the 불사신부 popup is visible (click (x, y) = 확인),
    ('unknown', None) when a 확인/취소 dialog is at that spot but the message text does not
    match (never click), or None when no such dialog is there."""
    tpl = _load_revive_mask()
    if tpl is None or img is None or img.shape[0] < 768 or img.shape[1] < 1024:
        return None
    if _warm_fill(img, REVIVE_OK_BOX) < 0.55 or _warm_fill(img, REVIVE_CANCEL_BOX) < 0.55:
        return None
    if _warm_fill(img, REVIVE_GAP_BOX) > 0.15:
        return None
    k3 = np.ones((3, 3), np.uint8)
    k5 = np.ones((5, 5), np.uint8)
    t_d = cv2.dilate(tpl, k3)
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = REVIVE_TEXT_BOX
    best = (0.0, 0.0)
    for dy in (-2, -1, 0, 1, 2):
        for dx in (-2, -1, 0, 1, 2):
            reg = img[y0 + dy:y1 + dy, x0 + dx:x1 + dx]
            if reg.shape[:2] != tpl.shape:
                continue
            c = (cv2.cvtColor(reg, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY) > 140).astype(np.uint8)
            n = int(c.sum())
            if n == 0:
                continue
            recall = float(np.logical_and(tpl, cv2.dilate(c, k5)).sum()) / max(1, int(tpl.sum()))
            prec = float(np.logical_and(c, t_d).sum()) / n
            if min(recall, prec) > min(best):
                best = (recall, prec)
    if min(best) >= REVIVE_TEXT_MIN:
        return ('ok', REVIVE_CLICK_XY)
    return ('unknown', None)


# ---- 포만감 gauge, 용병 portraits, inventory (measured 2026-11-19 on a native 1024x768 client) ----
_UI_DIR = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), 'assets', 'ui_templates')
FOOD_BAR_X = (89, 235)          # first/last pixel column of the gauge
FOOD_BAR_Y = 673                # a row through the fill (fill is y 670..677)
FOOD_LOW_FRAC = 0.2


def food_fraction(img: np.ndarray):
    """포만감 fill 0.0..1.0, or None if the gauge is not on screen (battle scene, covered...)."""
    if img is None or img.shape[0] < 768 or img.shape[1] < 1024:
        return None
    # frame of the gauge: black line above the fill and the dark right border
    if int(img[669, 150].sum()) > 40 or int(img[FOOD_BAR_Y, FOOD_BAR_X[1] + 1].sum()) > 60:
        return None
    n = 0
    for x in range(FOOD_BAR_X[0], FOOD_BAR_X[1] + 1):
        b, g, r = (int(v) for v in img[FOOD_BAR_Y, x])
        if g > r + 25 and g > b + 25 and g > 70:
            n += 1
        else:
            break
    return n / float(FOOD_BAR_X[1] - FOOD_BAR_X[0] + 1)


MERC_BAR_ROWS = tuple(35 + 41 * i for i in range(12))   # red HP bar rows of the 12 portrait slots
MERC_BAR_X = (4, 84)


def _merc_has_bar(img: np.ndarray, y0: int) -> bool:
    """Red HP bar present in the slot's bar row: 2 of 3 consecutive rows with >= 4 red pixels
    (searched +-9 px because the selection frame shifts one slot). Any HP above ~5% shows, a dead
    merc shows none, and portrait art rarely produces a 3-row red streak."""
    for y in range(y0 - 9, y0 + 12):
        band = img[y:y + 3, 6:88].astype(np.int16)
        red = (band[..., 2] > 140) & (band[..., 1] < 70) & (band[..., 0] < 70)
        if int((red.sum(axis=1) >= 4).sum()) >= 2:
            return True
    return False


def dead_mercs(img: np.ndarray) -> list:
    """1-based portrait slots whose portrait is shown but whose red HP bar is gone (= dead)."""
    if img is None or img.shape[0] < 768 or img.shape[1] < 1024:
        return []
    dead = []
    for i, y0 in enumerate(MERC_BAR_ROWS):
        if _merc_has_bar(img, y0):
            continue          # alive
        port = img[max(0, y0 - 34):y0 - 1, 6:80]
        gray = cv2.cvtColor(port, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
        if gray.mean() >= 30 and gray.std() >= 15:     # a portrait is there (not an empty panel)
            dead.append(i + 1)
    return dead


INV_HEADER_XY = (690, 100)
INV_CELL_R1C2 = (640, 172)       # 궁중 타락죽 (row 1, column 2 of the 가방 tab)
INV_CELL_ICON_XY = (622, 154)
INV_MATCH_MIN = 0.85
_tpl_cache = {}


def _tpl(name):
    if name not in _tpl_cache:
        _tpl_cache[name] = cv2.imread(os.path.join(_UI_DIR, name))
    return _tpl_cache[name]


def _match_at(img, tpl, xy, pad=6):
    if tpl is None or img is None or img.shape[0] < 768 or img.shape[1] < 1024:
        return 0.0
    x, y = xy
    h, w = tpl.shape[:2]
    reg = img[max(0, y - pad):y + h + pad, max(0, x - pad):x + w + pad]
    if reg.shape[0] < h or reg.shape[1] < w:
        return 0.0
    return float(cv2.matchTemplate(reg, tpl, cv2.TM_CCOEFF_NORMED).max())


def inventory_open(img: np.ndarray) -> bool:
    """The 가방 window is open at its default position."""
    return _match_at(img, _tpl('inventory_header.png'), INV_HEADER_XY) >= INV_MATCH_MIN


def porridge_in_r1c2(img: np.ndarray) -> bool:
    """Row 1, column 2 of the open inventory shows the 궁중 타락죽 icon."""
    return _match_at(img, _tpl('porridge_icon.png'), INV_CELL_ICON_XY) >= INV_MATCH_MIN


def merc_slots(img: np.ndarray) -> list:
    """[(slot 1..12, has_hp_bar, portrait_mean_gray)] for the 용병 portrait column."""
    if img is None or img.shape[0] < 768 or img.shape[1] < 1024:
        return []
    out = []
    for i, y0 in enumerate(MERC_BAR_ROWS):
        port = img[max(0, y0 - 34):y0 - 1, 6:80]
        out.append((i + 1, _merc_has_bar(img, y0), float(cv2.cvtColor(port, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY).mean())))
    return out


def merc_gauge(img: np.ndarray, y0: int):
    """(red_px, blue_px) of the HP and mana gauges in the slot's bar band (searched +-9 rows,
    the selection frame shifts the lower slots by a few pixels)."""
    best = (0, 0)
    for y in range(y0 - 9, y0 + 12):
        band = img[y:y + 12, 8:76].astype(np.int16)
        b, g, r = band[..., 0], band[..., 1], band[..., 2]
        red = int(((r > 140) & (g < 70) & (b < 70)).sum())
        blue = int(((b > 120) & (r < 130) & (b - r > 25)).sum())
        if red + blue > best[0] + best[1]:
            best = (red, blue)
    return best


MERC_GAUGE_MIN = 20     # alive units show >= ~200 red and ~200 blue px; a dead one shows 0 of both


def merc_alive_slots(img: np.ndarray) -> list:
    """Slots that show an HP or mana gauge (= a live unit)."""
    if img is None or img.shape[0] < 768 or img.shape[1] < 1024:
        return []
    out = []
    for i, y0 in enumerate(MERC_BAR_ROWS):
        red, blue = merc_gauge(img, y0)
        if red >= MERC_GAUGE_MIN or blue >= MERC_GAUGE_MIN:
            out.append(i + 1)
    return out


def dead_mercs_v2(img: np.ndarray, occupied=()) -> list:
    """Dead 용병 = a slot with NO HP gauge and NO mana gauge that still holds a unit.
    Held = a live unit sits in a lower slot (units fill the column from the top, so a gap above a
    live one is a dead one), or the slot was seen alive earlier (`occupied`, persisted)."""
    alive = merc_alive_slots(img)
    if not alive:
        return []          # no gauge anywhere: window covered, loading, or the wrong screen
    last_alive = max(alive)
    out = []
    for slot in range(1, 13):
        if slot in alive:
            continue
        if slot < last_alive or slot in occupied:
            out.append(slot)
    return out

# Source Generated with Decompyle++
# File: combat.pyc (Python 3.11)

import glob
import os
import time
import cv2
import numpy as np
import config as _config
import detector as _det
import vision
(MAP_X0, MAP_Y0) = (36, 670)
(MAP_W, MAP_H) = (180, 90)
MAP_CENTER = (MAP_W / 2.0, MAP_H / 2.0)
(BOX_W, BOX_H) = (66, 46)
FIELD_CENTER = (512, 373)
SUMMON_BOUNDS = (40, 40, 984, 614)
PAN_VY = 67.5
PAN_VX = 135.0
PAN_BASE = 0.4
PAN_RATIO = 0.7
PAN_SCREEN_VX = 2140.0
PAN_SCREEN_VY = 1080.0
PAN_SCREEN_MIN_HOLD = 0.04
MINIMAL_AIM_BOUNDS = (120.0, 100.0, 904.0, 550.0)
MINIMAL_PAN_INSET = 32.0
MINIMAL_BASE_TOLERANCE_PX = 12.0
ALLY_REF_UV = (0.44, 0.45)
LINK_DILATE = 5
MIN_SQUAD_PX = 12
DEFAULT_OPTS = {
    'squad_counts': [
        2,
        2,
        2,
        2,
        2,
        2],
    'use_summon': False,
    'summon_mode': None,
    'center_move_delay': 1.0,
    'lead_px': 40.0,
    'aim_y4': None,
    'multi_retreat': False,
    'noejeonju': False,
    'attack_keys': None,
    'aim_y': 40.0,
    'pan_dist': 0.4,
    'pan_ratio': PAN_RATIO }

def _noejeonju(io, o):
    if not o.get('noejeonju'):
        return None
    for k in ('0', 'r'):
        io.press(k)
        time.sleep(0.025)


def _squad_cycles(counts, idx):
    if not counts:
        return 2.0
    return float(counts[idx]) if idx < len(counts) else float(counts[-1])

BATTLE_PIXEL_XY = (986, 704)
BATTLE_PIXEL_SUM = 222
BATTLE_PIXEL_TOL = 20

def is_battle(frame):
    (x, y) = BATTLE_PIXEL_XY
    if frame is None or y >= frame.shape[0] or x >= frame.shape[1]:
        return False
    return abs(int(frame[y, x].sum()) - BATTLE_PIXEL_SUM) <= BATTLE_PIXEL_TOL


def minimap_center_black(mm):
    (cy, cx) = (MAP_H // 2, MAP_W // 2)
    block = mm[cy - 1:cy + 2, cx - 1:cx + 2]
    return bool((block == 0).all(axis = 2).any())


def is_main(frame):
    return vision.is_scene(frame, 'main')


def wait_for_main(io, log, max_frames = 100, stop = None):
    for _ in range(max_frames):
        if stop and stop():
            return False
        if is_main(io.capture()):
            return True
        time.sleep(0.1)
    log('퇴각실패 (100루프 내 메인 복귀 실패)')
    return False


def crop_minimap(frame):
    return frame[MAP_Y0:MAP_Y0 + MAP_H, MAP_X0:MAP_X0 + MAP_W]

BOX_WHITE_MIN = 170
BOX_WHITE_SPREAD = 12

def _box_mask(mm):
    v = mm.astype(np.int16)
    return (v.min(axis = 2) >= BOX_WHITE_MIN) & (v.max(axis = 2) - v.min(axis = 2) <= BOX_WHITE_SPREAD)


def detect_box(mm):
    white = _box_mask(mm)
    if white.sum() < 8:
        return None
    (ys, xs) = np.where(white)
    (x0, x1, y0, y1) = (int(xs.min()), int(xs.max()), int(ys.min()), int(ys.max()))
    (clip_l, clip_r) = (x0 <= 0, x1 >= MAP_W - 1)
    (clip_t, clip_b) = (y0 <= 0, y1 >= MAP_H - 1)
    if clip_l and not clip_r:
        x0 = x1 - (BOX_W - 1)
    elif clip_r and not clip_l:
        x1 = x0 + (BOX_W - 1)
    if clip_t and not clip_b:
        y0 = y1 - (BOX_H - 1)
    elif clip_b and not clip_t:
        y1 = y0 + (BOX_H - 1)
    return {
        'x0': x0,
        'y0': y0,
        'x1': x1,
        'y1': y1,
        'cx': (x0 + x1) / 2.0,
        'cy': (y0 + y1) / 2.0,
        'w': max(1, x1 - x0),
        'h': max(1, y1 - y0) }


def _dot_mask(mm):
    black = (mm.sum(axis = 2) == 0).astype(np.uint8)
    solid = cv2.morphologyEx(black, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, np.ones((9, 9), np.uint8))
    interior = cv2.erode(solid, np.ones((7, 7), np.uint8))
    hsv = cv2.cvtColor(mm, cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
    (s, v) = (hsv[(..., 1)], hsv[(..., 2)])
    return (s > 20) & (v > 45) & (interior > 0) & (black == 0)


def detect_squads(mm, min_px = MIN_SQUAD_PX, colors = None):
    mask = _dot_mask(mm)
    if colors is not None:
        known = np.zeros(mask.shape, dtype = bool)
        for color in colors:
            known |= (mm == color).all(axis = 2)
        mask &= known
    if not mask.any():
        return []
    pts = mm[mask]
    colors = np.unique(pts, axis = 0)
    assign = { tuple(c): i for i, c in enumerate(colors) }
    label = np.full(mm.shape[:2], -1, np.int32)
    (ys, xs) = np.where(mask)
    label[ys, xs] = [ assign[tuple(mm[y, x])] for y, x in zip(ys, xs) ]
    squads = []
    kernel = np.ones((LINK_DILATE, LINK_DILATE), np.uint8)
    for pi, rep in enumerate(colors):
        cmask = (label == pi).astype(np.uint8)
        (n, lab, _stats, _cent) = cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(cv2.dilate(cmask, kernel), 8)
        for i in range(1, n):
            comp = (lab == i) & (cmask > 0)
            px = int(comp.sum())
            if px < min_px:
                continue
            (yy, xx) = np.nonzero(comp)
            (x0, y0, x1, y1) = (int(xx.min()), int(yy.min()), int(xx.max()), int(yy.max()))
            squads.append({
                'color': tuple((int(v) for v in rep)),
                'cx': (x0 + x1) / 2.0,
                'cy': (y0 + y1) / 2.0,
                'px': px,
                'bounds': (x0, y0, x1, y1) })
    return squads


def analyze(mm):
    box = detect_box(mm)
    squads = detect_squads(mm)
    (ally, enemies) = (None, list(squads))
    if box and squads:
        best = None
        for s in squads:
            u = (s['cx'] - box['x0']) / box['w']
            v = (s['cy'] - box['y0']) / box['h']
            if 0.0 <= u <= 1.0 and 0.0 <= v <= 1.0:
                d = (u - ALLY_REF_UV[0]) ** 2 + (v - ALLY_REF_UV[1]) ** 2
                if best is None or d < best:
                    (best, ally) = (d, s)
        if ally is not None:
            enemies = [ s for s in squads if s is not ally ]
    return {
        'box': box,
        'ally': ally,
        'enemies': enemies }

CENTER_PING_XY = {
    'left': (984, 384),
    'right': (40, 384),
    'up': (512, 624),
    'down': (512, 40) }

def _dir_side(dx, dy):
    dx *= 0.5
    if abs(dx) >= abs(dy):
        return 'right' if dx > 0 else 'left'
    return 'down' if dy > 0 else 'up'


def _ally_side(ally):
    return _dir_side(ally['cx'] - MAP_CENTER[0], ally['cy'] - MAP_CENTER[1])


def mm_dist_sq(dx, dy):
    dx *= 0.5
    return dx * dx + dy * dy


def formation_center(squads):
    if len(squads) < 3:
        return None
    cx0 = sum((s['cx'] for s in squads)) / len(squads)
    cy0 = sum((s['cy'] for s in squads)) / len(squads)
    g = {
        'up': [],
        'down': [],
        'left': [],
        'right': [] }
    for s in squads:
        g[_dir_side(s['cx'] - cx0, s['cy'] - cy0)].append(s)
    xs = [ s['cx'] for s in g['up'] + g['down'] ]
    ys = [ s['cy'] for s in g['left'] + g['right'] ]
    if g['left'] and g['right']:
        xs.append((sum((s['cx'] for s in g['left'])) / len(g['left']) + sum((s['cx'] for s in g['right'])) / len(g['right'])) / 2)
    if g['up'] and g['down']:
        ys.append((sum((s['cy'] for s in g['up'])) / len(g['up']) + sum((s['cy'] for s in g['down'])) / len(g['down'])) / 2)
    if not xs or not ys:
        return None
    return (sum(xs) / len(xs), sum(ys) / len(ys))


def _pan(io, dx, dy, scale = 1.0, *, min_delta = 1.0, min_hold = 0.0):
    steps = []
    if abs(dx) >= min_delta:
        steps.append(('right' if dx > 0 else 'left', max(min_hold, (abs(dx) / PAN_VX) * scale)))
    if abs(dy) >= min_delta:
        steps.append(('down' if dy > 0 else 'up', max(min_hold, (abs(dy) / PAN_VY) * scale)))
    if not steps:
        return {}
    steps.sort(key=lambda s: s[1])
    held = { k: t for k, t in steps }
    for k, _ in steps:
        io.hold(k)
    elapsed = 0.0
    
    try:
        for k, t in steps:
            time.sleep(max(0.0, t - elapsed))
            io.release(k)
            elapsed = t
        return held
    except Exception:
        for k, _ in steps:
            io.release(k)
        raise 



def _pan_screen(io, dx, dy):
    return _pan(io, dx * PAN_VX / PAN_SCREEN_VX, dy * PAN_VY / PAN_SCREEN_VY, min_delta = 1e-06, min_hold = PAN_SCREEN_MIN_HOLD)

PAN_SETTLE_TIMEOUT = 0.25
PAN_STABLE_FRAMES = 3
PAN_STABLE_SPAN = 0.035
PAN_STABLE_EPS = 0.5

def _valid_pan_box(mm, box):
    if not box or box['w'] < 8 or box['h'] < 8:
        return False
    white = _box_mask(mm)
    (h, w) = white.shape
    (x0, x1, y0, y1) = (int(box[k]) for k in ('x0', 'x1', 'y0', 'y1'))
    (xl, xr, yt, yb) = (max(0, x0), min(w - 1, x1), max(0, y0), min(h - 1, y1))
    if xl >= xr or yt >= yb:
        return False
    horizontal = [ white[y, xl:xr + 1].mean() for y in (y0, y1) if 0 <= y < h ]
    vertical = [ white[yt:yb + 1, x].mean() for x in (x0, x1) if 0 <= x < w ]
    return bool(horizontal and vertical and min(horizontal + vertical) >= 0.5)


def _precision_note(message):
    if not _pan_log_path:
        return None
    
    try:
        with open(_pan_log_path, 'a', encoding = 'utf-8') as f:
            f.write(f'''[{time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')}] 정밀조준 {message}\n''')
            return None
    except OSError:
        return None



def _settled_pan_frame(io, stop, diagnostics = None):
    details = diagnostics if diagnostics is not None else { }
    details.clear()
    details.update(received = 0, missing = 0, invalid = 0, changed = 0, stable = 0, elapsed_ms = 0, reason = '')
    fresh = getattr(io, 'capture_after', None)
    if not callable(fresh):
        frame = io.capture()
        return (frame, detect_box(crop_minimap(frame)) if frame is not None else None)
    started = after = time.monotonic()
    deadline = started + PAN_SETTLE_TIMEOUT
    samples = []
    while not stop():
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining <= 0:
            break
        after = time.monotonic()
        frame = fresh(after, timeout = min(remaining, 0.05), stop = stop)
        after = time.monotonic()
        details['missing' if frame is None else 'received'] += 1
        if frame is not None:
            details['_last_frame'] = frame
        if stop() or after > deadline:
            break
        if frame is None:
            time.sleep(min(0.005, max(0.0, deadline - after)))
            continue
        mm = crop_minimap(frame)
        box = detect_box(mm)
        if not _valid_pan_box(mm, box):
            details['invalid'] += 1
            samples.clear()
            continue
        details['_last_valid_frame'] = frame
        details['_last_valid_box'] = dict(box)
        samples.append((after, box))
        count_before = len(samples)
        while any((max((s[1][k] for s in samples)) - min((s[1][k] for s in samples)) > PAN_STABLE_EPS for k in ('cx', 'cy', 'w', 'h'))):
            samples.pop(0)
        if len(samples) < count_before:
            details['changed'] += 1
        if len(samples) >= PAN_STABLE_FRAMES and after - samples[0][0] >= PAN_STABLE_SPAN:
            details.update(stable = len(samples), elapsed_ms = (after - started) * 1000)
            _precision_note(f'''안정 프레임 {len(samples)}장, {(after - started) * 1000:.0f}ms''')
            return (frame, box)
    details.update(stable = len(samples), elapsed_ms = (time.monotonic() - started) * 1000)
    if stop():
        reason = '정지 요청'
    elif details['received'] == 0:
        reason = '새 프레임 미수신'
    elif details['invalid'] == details['received']:
        reason = '흰박스 검출 실패'
    elif details['changed']:
        reason = '박스 위치·크기 변동'
    elif details['invalid']:
        reason = '흰박스 검출 불안정'
    elif len(samples) < PAN_STABLE_FRAMES:
        reason = '새 프레임 부족'
    else:
        reason = '안정 관측 시간 부족'
    details['reason'] = reason
    _precision_note(f'''카메라 안정 프레임 확보 실패: {reason}, 수신 {details['received']}장, 미수신 {details['missing']}회, 박스 무효 {details['invalid']}장, 변동 {details['changed']}회, 안정 {len(samples)}/{PAN_STABLE_FRAMES}장, {details['elapsed_ms']:.0f}ms''')
    return (None, None)


def _pan_settle_failure_detail(details):
    if not details or not details.get('reason'):
        return ''
    return f''' [{details['reason']}, 안정 관측 {details.get('stable', 0)}/{PAN_STABLE_FRAMES}장]'''


def _entry_pan_frame(io, stop):
    details = { }
    (frame, box) = _settled_pan_frame(io, stop, diagnostics = details)
    if stop():
        details['reason'] = '정지 요청'
        return (None, None, details)
    latest = details.get('_last_frame')
    if latest is None:
        latest = frame if frame is not None else io.capture()
    if isinstance(latest, np.ndarray) and is_main(latest):
        details['reason'] = '메인화면 복귀'
        return (None, None, details)
    if stop():
        details['reason'] = '정지 요청'
        return (None, None, details)
    if box is not None:
        return (frame, box, details)
    last_frame = details.get('_last_valid_frame')
    last_box = details.get('_last_valid_box')
    if details.get('reason') in ('새 프레임 부족', '안정 관측 시간 부족') and not details.get('invalid') and not details.get('changed') and last_frame is not None and latest is last_frame and last_box is not None:
        reason = f'''{details['reason']} · 이번 팬 뒤 정상 프레임 사용'''
        return (last_frame, dict(last_box, _entry_fallback = reason), details)
    return (None, None, details)


def _predict_pan_camera(cur, held, vx, vy):
    if not isinstance(held, dict):
        return None
    durations = [ held.get(k, 0.0) for k in ('right', 'left', 'down', 'up') ]
    if not all((np.isfinite(v) and v >= 0 for v in durations)) or not all((np.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in (vx, vy))):
        return None
    result = [
        cur[0] + (held.get('right', 0.0) - held.get('left', 0.0)) * vx,
        cur[1] + (held.get('down', 0.0) - held.get('up', 0.0)) * vy]
    return result if all((np.isfinite(v) for v in result)) else None


def _attack_pan_frame(io, stop, cur, predicted = None):
    details = { }
    (frame, box) = _settled_pan_frame(io, stop, diagnostics = details)
    if stop():
        details['reason'] = '정지 요청'
        return (None, None, details)
    latest = details.get('_last_frame')
    if latest is None:
        latest = frame if frame is not None else io.capture()
    if isinstance(latest, np.ndarray) and is_main(latest):
        details['reason'] = '메인화면 복귀'
        return (None, None, details)
    if stop():
        details['reason'] = '정지 요청'
        return (None, None, details)
    if box is not None:
        return (frame, box, details)
    reason = details.get('reason') or '카메라 확인 부족'
    last_box = details.get('_last_valid_box')
    if last_box is not None:
        box = dict(last_box, _aim_fallback = f'''{reason} · 마지막 박스 사용''')
        return (details.get('_last_valid_frame'), box, details)
    center = predicted if predicted is not None else cur
    if not (all((np.isfinite(v) for v in center)) and -BOX_W <= center[0] <= MAP_W + BOX_W and -BOX_H <= center[1] <= MAP_H + BOX_H):
        details['reason'] = '카메라 추정 기준 없음'
        return (None, None, details)
    source = 'pan 이동량 추정' if predicted is not None else '직전 카메라 사용'
    box = dict(cx = center[0], cy = center[1], w = BOX_W - 1, h = BOX_H - 1, _camera_estimated = True, _aim_fallback = f'''{reason} · {source}''')
    return (None, box, details)


def _fourway_projection_profile(box, ally, enemies):
    if not box or not ally or not isinstance(enemies, (list, tuple)) or len(enemies) not in (2, 3) or box.get('w') != BOX_W - 1 or box.get('h') != BOX_H - 1:
        return None
    if any(('cx0' in e or 'cy0' in e for e in enemies)):
        return None
    
    try:
        raw = [(float(s['cx']), float(s['cy'])) for s in [ally, *enemies]]
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
        return None

    if any((not np.isfinite(x) or not np.isfinite(y) or not (0 <= x < MAP_W and 0 <= y < MAP_H) for x, y in raw)):
        return None
    points = sorted(((x * 0.5, y) for x, y in raw))
    if len(set(points)) != len(points):
        return None
    pairs = [ (sum(((a[k] - b[k]) ** 2 for k in (0, 1))), a, b) for i, a in enumerate(points) for b in points[i + 1:] ]
    (distance2, a, b) = max(pairs)
    diameter = distance2 ** 0.5
    profiles = ((38.0, 43.0, 26.7, 26.1, '궁형'), (54.0, 61.0, 17.04, 16.97, '도사형'))
    chosen = next((p for p in profiles if p[0] <= diameter <= p[1]), None)
    if chosen is None:
        return None
    transverse_tolerance = 2.0
    radial_tolerance = 2.5
    if min(abs(a[0] - b[0]), abs(a[1] - b[1])) > transverse_tolerance:
        return None
    (cx, cy) = ((a[0] + b[0]) / 2, (a[1] + b[1]) / 2)
    radius = diameter / 2
    slots = { }
    for x, y in points:
        (dx, dy) = (x - cx, y - cy)
        horizontal = abs(dx) >= abs(dy)
        (radial, transverse) = (abs(dx), abs(dy)) if horizontal else (abs(dy), abs(dx))
        if abs(radial - radius) > radial_tolerance or transverse > transverse_tolerance:
            return None
        slot = ('right' if dx > 0 else 'left') if horizontal else 'down' if dy > 0 else 'up'
        if slot in slots:
            return None
        slots[slot] = (x, y)
    for first, second in (('left', 'right'), ('up', 'down')):
        if first in slots and second in slots:
            (p, q) = (slots[first], slots[second])
            observed = sum(((p[k] - q[k]) ** 2 for k in (0, 1))) ** 0.5
            if not chosen[0] <= observed <= chosen[1]:
                return None
    return (chosen[2], chosen[3], f'''{chosen[4]} 초기간격 {diameter:.2f}px''')


def _projection_scale(o, box):
    scale = o.get('projection_scale')
    measured_zoom = box['w'] == BOX_W - 1 and box['h'] == BOX_H - 1
    if isinstance(scale, (list, tuple)) and len(scale) == 2 and measured_zoom:
        
        try:
            (x, y) = map(float, scale)
            if np.isfinite(x) and np.isfinite(y) and 1 <= x <= 100 and 1 <= y <= 100:
                return (x, y)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            pass

    return (FIELD_CENTER[0] * 2 / BOX_W, FIELD_CENTER[1] * 2 / BOX_H)

KEY_HOLD_S = 0.03
KEY_GAP_S = 0.03
MOVE_SETTLE_S = 0.05
HOLD_REPEAT_S = 0.05
RELEASE_GAP_S = 0.05
HOLD_AS_WAIT = False
ATTACK_KEYS_ALL = ('3', '4', '5', '6', '7', '8', '9')
ATTACK_KEYS_DEFAULT = ('3', '4', '5', '6')
ATTACK_TAIL = '2'
ATTACK_FINISH = 'e'

def normalize_attack_keys(raw) -> list:
    if raw is None:
        return list(ATTACK_KEYS_DEFAULT)
    
    try:
        want = { str(k).strip() for k in raw }
    except TypeError:
        return list(ATTACK_KEYS_DEFAULT)

    return [ k for k in ATTACK_KEYS_ALL if k in want ]


def _attack(io, x, y, hold_sec, stop = None, keys = None):
    seq = list(keys if keys is not None else ATTACK_KEYS_DEFAULT) + [
        ATTACK_TAIL]
    io.move(x, y)
    time.sleep(MOVE_SETTLE_S)
    io.move(x, y)   # land + verify again right before the casts (the guard also pins it)
    for k in seq:
        io.press('r', KEY_HOLD_S)
        time.sleep(KEY_GAP_S)
        io.press(k, KEY_HOLD_S)
        time.sleep(KEY_GAP_S)
    io.press(ATTACK_FINISH, KEY_HOLD_S)
    time.sleep(KEY_GAP_S)
    io.move(x, y)
    if HOLD_AS_WAIT:
        end = time.time() + max(0.0, float(hold_sec))
        while time.time() < end:
            if stop and stop():
                return None
            time.sleep(HOLD_REPEAT_S)
    else:
        
        try:
            io.hold('r')
            end = time.time() + max(0.0, float(hold_sec))
            while time.time() < end:
                if stop and stop():
                    return None
                io.hold('r')
                time.sleep(HOLD_REPEAT_S)
        finally:
            time.sleep(RELEASE_GAP_S)
            io.release('r')
    time.sleep(KEY_GAP_S)
    io.press(ATTACK_FINISH, KEY_HOLD_S)
    return None



def run_battle(io, opts = None, log = print, detect = None, should_stop = None, pre_retreat = None):
    o = {
        **DEFAULT_OPTS, **(opts or {}) }
    o['summon_mode'] = _config.summon_mode(o)
    o['use_summon'] = o['summon_mode'] != 'none'
    o['attack_keys'] = normalize_attack_keys(o.get('attack_keys'))
    _pan_log_begin(o)
    stop = should_stop or (lambda: False)

    
    def retreat():
        if pre_retreat:
            
            try:
                pre_retreat(io.capture())
            except Exception:
                pass

        _retreat(io)

    frame = io.capture()
    if frame is None:
        return 'no-frame'
    st = analyze(crop_minimap(frame))
    (box, ally, enemies) = (st['box'], st['ally'], st['enemies'])
    if not box or not ally or not enemies:
        log(f'''전투: 검출 부족 — 퇴각·집계 (박스 {'O' if box else 'X'} / 아군 {'O' if ally else 'X'} / 적 {len(enemies)}부대)''')
        _save_skip_frame(o, frame, box, ally, enemies)
        retreat()
        if not wait_for_main(io, log, stop = stop):
            return 'stopped' if stop() else 'retreat-fail'
        return 'ok'
    _pan_log_entry(frame, box, ally, enemies)
    log(f'''전투 진입 — 적 {len(enemies)}부대''')
    _lock_ally_origin(ally)
    if o.get('multi_retreat') and len(enemies) >= 4:
        log(f'''다부대 즉시 퇴각 (적 {len(enemies)}부대)''')
        retreat()
        if not wait_for_main(io, log, stop = stop):
            return 'stopped' if stop() else 'retreat-fail'
        return 'ok'
    pan_scale = max(0.5, min(2.0, 65.0 / box['w']))
    pan_scale *= o['pan_dist'] / PAN_BASE
    u_ref = 0.5 + (ally['cx'] - box['cx']) / BOX_W
    v_ref = 0.5 + (ally['cy'] - box['cy']) / BOX_H
    multi = len(enemies) >= 4
    _enable_fragment_tracking(ally, enemies, multi)
    (kx, ky) = _projection_scale(o, box)
    profile = None if multi else _fourway_projection_profile(box, ally, enemies)
    o['_minimal_pan'] = profile is not None
    if profile:
        (kx, ky, profile_name) = profile
        log(f'''4방향 자동 팬 — {profile_name}, 타격영역 진입 / 추가 추격 최대 1회''')
        _precision_note(f'''자동 프로필 {profile_name}, 공통 화면속도 {PAN_SCREEN_VX:.0f}/{PAN_SCREEN_VY:.0f}px/s''')
    elif not multi:
        _precision_note('진입 배치 프로필 미확정 — 기존 몹별 팬/배율 유지')
    _precision_note(f'''v1 배율 x={kx:.3f} y={ky:.3f} 초기기준({ally['cx'] - box['cx']:.2f},{ally['cy'] - box['cy']:.2f})''')
    
    def unstable_entry(details):
        if stop() or details.get('reason') == '정지 요청':
            return 'stopped'
        if details.get('reason') == '메인화면 복귀':
            log('진입 확인 중 메인화면 복귀 — 전투 종료')
            return 'ok'
        log(f'''진입 팬 안정화 실패{_pan_settle_failure_detail(details)} — 소환/공격 없이 퇴각''')
        retreat()
        if not wait_for_main(io, log, stop = stop):
            return 'stopped' if stop() else 'retreat-fail'
        return 'stopped' if stop() else 'ok'

    
    def note_entry_frame(settled_box, details):
        if settled_box.get('_entry_fallback'):
            message = f'''진입 화면 확인 보완{_pan_settle_failure_detail(details)} — 확보한 정상 프레임으로 계속'''
            log(message)
            _precision_note(message)
            return None

    summon_tgt = None
    ping_at = time.time()
    if multi:
        _noejeonju(io, o)
        _pan(io, MAP_CENTER[0] - ally['cx'], MAP_CENTER[1] - ally['cy'], pan_scale)
        if callable(getattr(io, 'capture_after', None)):
            (_, settled_box, settle_details) = _entry_pan_frame(io, stop)
            if settled_box is None:
                return unstable_entry(settle_details)
            note_entry_frame(settled_box, settle_details)
        io.move(*FIELD_CENTER)
        time.sleep(0.05)
        for k in '1mq2w' if o['use_summon'] else '1mq2':
            io.press(k)
            time.sleep(0.025)
    else:
        _noejeonju(io, o)
        io.move(*CENTER_PING_XY[_ally_side(ally)])
        time.sleep(0.05)
        for k in '1mq2':
            io.press(k)
            time.sleep(0.025)
        if o['use_summon']:
            center_summon = bool(o.get('summon_center'))
            summon_tgt = None if center_summon else max(enemies, key=lambda e: mm_dist_sq(e['cx'] - MAP_CENTER[0], e['cy'] - MAP_CENTER[1]))
            if o['_minimal_pan']:
                _pan_screen(io, (MAP_CENTER[0] - ally['cx']) * kx, (MAP_CENTER[1] - ally['cy']) * ky)
            else:
                _pan(io, MAP_CENTER[0] - ally['cx'], MAP_CENTER[1] - ally['cy'], pan_scale)
            sx = sy = None
            (frame2, settled_box, settle_details) = _entry_pan_frame(io, stop)
            if settled_box is None and (callable(getattr(io, 'capture_after', None)) or settle_details.get('reason') in ('정지 요청', '메인화면 복귀')):
                return unstable_entry(settle_details)
            if settled_box is not None:
                note_entry_frame(settled_box, settle_details)
            if frame2 is not None:
                mm2 = crop_minimap(frame2)
                box2 = settled_box
                if box2:
                    fc = formation_center(detect_squads(mm2))
                    (tx, ty) = fc if fc else MAP_CENTER
                    log('소환 기준 — ' + ('부대 십자 중앙' if fc else '부대<3, 맵중앙 폴백'))
                    sx = FIELD_CENTER[0] + (tx - box2['cx'] - (u_ref - 0.5) * BOX_W) * kx
                    sy = FIELD_CENTER[1] + (ty - box2['cy'] - (v_ref - 0.5) * BOX_H) * ky
            if sx is None:
                (sx, sy) = FIELD_CENTER
            if not center_summon:
                side = _dir_side(summon_tgt['cx'] - MAP_CENTER[0], summon_tgt['cy'] - MAP_CENTER[1])
                if side == 'left':
                    sx -= 400
                elif side == 'right':
                    sx += 400
                elif side == 'up':
                    sy -= 200
                else:
                    sy += 200
            sx = min(SUMMON_BOUNDS[2], max(SUMMON_BOUNDS[0], sx))
            sy = min(SUMMON_BOUNDS[3], max(SUMMON_BOUNDS[1], sy))
            io.move(int(round(sx)), int(round(sy)))
            time.sleep(0.05)
            io.press('w')
    time.sleep(max(0.0, o['center_move_delay'] - (time.time() - ping_at)))
    current_box = _rescan(io, list(enemies), ally)
    for e in enemies:
        (e['cx0'], e['cy0']) = (e['cx'], e['cy'])
    if multi:
        order = sorted(enemies, key=lambda e: mm_dist_sq(e['cx'] - ally['cx'], e['cy'] - ally['cy']))
        if summon_tgt is not None:
            s_side = _dir_side(summon_tgt['cx'] - MAP_CENTER[0], summon_tgt['cy'] - MAP_CENTER[1])
            rest = [ e for e in order if e is not summon_tgt ]
            other_side = [ e for e in rest if _dir_side(e['cx'] - MAP_CENTER[0], e['cy'] - MAP_CENTER[1]) != s_side ]
            same_side = [ e for e in rest if e not in other_side ]
            order = other_side + same_side + [
                summon_tgt]
        (remaining, greedy) = (order, False)
    else:
        remaining = [ e for e in enemies if e is not summon_tgt ]
        greedy = True
    ref = (ally['cx'], ally['cy'])
    cur = [
        current_box['cx'],
        current_box['cy']] if current_box else [
        MAP_CENTER[0],
        MAP_CENTER[1]]
    o['_camera_estimated'] = not bool(current_box)
    total = len(enemies)
    order_idx = 0
    while order_idx < total:
        if stop():
            log('정지 명령 — 즉시 퇴각')
            retreat()
            return 'stopped'
        if order_idx > 0:
            pending = list(remaining)
            if greedy and summon_tgt is not None:
                pending.append(summon_tgt)
            current_box = _rescan(io, pending, ally)
            if current_box:
                cur = [
                    current_box['cx'],
                    current_box['cy']]
                o['_camera_estimated'] = False
        if remaining:
            if greedy:
                enemy = min(remaining, key=lambda e: mm_dist_sq(e['cx'] - ref[0], e['cy'] - ref[1]))
                remaining.remove(enemy)
            else:
                enemy = remaining.pop(0)
        else:
            enemy = summon_tgt
        cycles = _squad_cycles(o['squad_counts'], order_idx)
        lead_mult = 0.0 if enemy is summon_tgt and o['summon_mode'] == 'taunt' else 1.0
        cur = _aim_minimap(io, enemy, ally, u_ref, v_ref, pan_scale, kx, ky, o, cycles, log, order_idx, lead_mult, stop, cur, multi, detect)
        if o.get('_battle_main_seen') and not stop():
            return 'ok'
        ref = (enemy['cx'], enemy['cy'])
        order_idx += 1
    retreat()
    if stop():
        return 'stopped'
    if not wait_for_main(io, log, stop = stop):
        return 'stopped' if stop() else 'retreat-fail'
    return 'ok'

CROSS_DIST = 35.0
CROSS_RATIO = 0.9

def _approach(io, enemy, u_ref, v_ref, pan_scale, kx, ky, log, idx, ratio = PAN_RATIO):
    frame = io.capture()
    box = detect_box(crop_minimap(frame)) if frame is not None else None
    if not box:
        log(f'''{idx + 1}부대: 흰박스 미검출 — 팬 생략''')
        return (FIELD_CENTER, None)
    goal_x = enemy['cx'] - (u_ref - 0.5) * BOX_W
    goal_y = enemy['cy'] - (v_ref - 0.5) * BOX_H
    (dx, dy) = (goal_x - box['cx'], goal_y - box['cy'])
    if ratio < 1.0 and max(abs(dx) * 0.5, abs(dy)) >= CROSS_DIST:
        ratio = CROSS_RATIO
    _pan(io, ratio * dx, ratio * dy, pan_scale)
    ex = FIELD_CENTER[0] + (1.0 - ratio) * dx * kx
    ey = FIELD_CENTER[1] + (1.0 - ratio) * dy * ky
    moved = dict(box)
    moved['cx'] = box['cx'] + ratio * dx
    moved['cy'] = box['cy'] + ratio * dy
    return ((ex, ey), moved)

AIM_Y_OFFSET = 40.0

ATTACK_AIM_OFFSET = (20.0, -10.0)

AIM_ROI_FRAC = 0.75
AIM_DIR_EPS = 40.0
MOB_CHASE_ATTEMPTS = 2
CHASE_JUMP_R = 12.0
BOSS_GATE_X = 360.0
BOSS_GATE_Y = 180.0
AIM_LEAD_XFRAC = 2.0

def _clamp_aim(x, y):
    (x0, y0, x1, y1) = AIM_BOUNDS
    return (min(x1, max(x0, x)), min(y1, max(y0, y)))

        self.auth_id = os.environ.get('GATOSUPER_AUTH_ID', '').strip() or config.load().get('auth_id', '')
        self.brain = Brain(self.engine, self.vision, self.hands, note=lambda m: self.vision.note('brain', m), notify=lambda s: self.vision.note('input', s), notify_img=self.notifier.text, report_battle=self.auth.report_battle, report_appear=self.auth.report_appear, on_stopped=self._on_brain_stopped, banja_ok=lambda: self.auth.banja, no_battle_limit=lambda: self.auth.no_battle_limit, debug_jochul=self.auth.upload_jochul_debug, debug_jamun=self.auth.upload_jamun_debug, debug_jamun_wrong=self.auth.report_jamun_wrong, auth_id=lambda: self.auth.auth_id if self.auth.ok else '', on_auto_restart=self._auto_restart_bot)
        self._start_auth()
        self.bot_poller = BotPoller(get_token=lambda: config.load().get('discord_token', ''), get_channel=lambda: config.load().get('discord_channel', ''), on_message=self._on_discord_command, log=lambda s: self.vision.note('input', s), poll=2.0)
        self.bot_poller.start()
        self._chat_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._chat_log = []
        self._chat_seq = 0
        self.sniffer = ChatSniffer(on_chat=self._on_chat, log=lambda s: self.vision.note('input', s))
        self.sniffer.start()
        self._sched_thread = None
        self._sched_stop = threading.Event()
        self._sched = {'active': False, 'cycle': 0, 'cycles': 0, 'battles': 0, 'target': 0, 'resting': False, 'rest_left': 0}
        self._run_since = 0.0
        self._last_note = ''
        self._wins = []
        self._wins_at = 0.0
        self._live_until = 0.0
        self._live_thread = None
        self._live_lock = threading.Lock()
        self.start_cmd_poller()
        try:
            import remote_link
            self.remote = remote_link.install(self)   # None when remote.json is absent or invalid
        except Exception as _e:
            self.remote = None
            self.vision.note('input', f'[원격링크] 설치 실패 — {type(_e).__name__}: {_e}')
        try:
            import agent_link
            self.link = agent_link.install(self)   # always-on link to the Mac; None when disabled
        except Exception as _e:
            self.link = None
            self.vision.note('input', f'[링크] 설치 실패 — {type(_e).__name__}: {_e}')
        try:
            import update
            self.updater = update.install(self)   # frozen builds only; None in a source checkout
        except Exception as _e:
            self.updater = None
            self.vision.note('input', f'[업데이트] 설치 실패 — {type(_e).__name__}: {_e}')
    _CHAT_NOTIFY_KEY = {'귓말': 'notify_whisper', '일반': 'notify_normal', '반복': 'notify_repeat'}
    def _on_chat(self, kind: str, name: str, msg: str) -> None:
        # ***<module>.Api._on_chat: Failure: Different control flow
        with self._chat_lock:
            self._chat_seq += 1
            self._chat_log.append({'seq': self._chat_seq, 'kind': kind, 'name': name, 'msg': msg, 'time': time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')})
            if len(self._chat_log) > 400:
                del self._chat_log[:len(self._chat_log) - 400]
        key = self._CHAT_NOTIFY_KEY.get(kind)
        if not key or config.load().get(key, False):
                self.notifier.text(f'{kind} {name}: {msg}')
    _REMOTE_KEYS = {'key_f': 'f', 'key_m': 'm', 'key_i': 'i', 'key_b': 'b', 'key_enter': 'enter'}
    _INPUT_CMDS = frozenset(_REMOTE_KEYS) | {'click_l', 'click_r'}
    def start_cmd_poller(self):
        def _loop():
            while True:
                if not self.auth.token:
                    time.sleep(3.0)
                    continue
                else:
                    try:
                        cmds = self.auth.poll_cmd()
                    except Exception:
                        cmds = []
                    if not cmds:
                        time.sleep(0.5)
                        continue
                    else:
                        for c in cmds:
                            try:
                                self._run_remote(c.get('cmd', ''), c.get('arg', ''))
                            except Exception as e:
                                self.vision.note('input', f'[원격] 명령 오류: {e}')
        threading.Thread(target=_loop, daemon=True).start()
    def _note(self, msg: str):
        self._last_note = msg[:200]
        self.vision.note('input', msg)
        self.notifier.text(msg)
    def _run_remote(self, cmd: str, arg: str=''):
        if cmd in self._INPUT_CMDS and self.brain.running:
            self._note('가동 중에는 입력이 차단됩니다 — 봇을 정지한 뒤 다시 시도하세요')
        else:
            if cmd == 'start':
                self.start()
                self._note('▶ 패널 시작' if self.brain.running else f'⚠️ 시작 불가 — {self.brain.start_block_reason() or self.auth.label()}')
            else:
                if cmd == 'stop':
                    if not self.brain.running:
                        self._note('이미 정지 상태')
                    else:
                        if self.brain._shutdown_at is not None:
                            self._note('이미 정지 예약됨')
                        else:
                            self.stop_grace('패널')
                    return None
                else:
                    if cmd == 'shot':
                        img = self.brain._grab_main()
                        if img is None:
                            self._note(f'[원격] 캡처 실패 — {self._capture_fail_reason()}')
                            return
                        else:
                            ok, enc = cv2.imencode('.jpg', img, [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 70])
                            self.vision.note('input', '[원격] 화면 캡처 전송' if ok and self.auth.upload_shot(enc.tobytes()) else '[원격] 캡처 업로드 실패')
                    else:
                        if cmd == 'live_start':
                            self._live_extend()
                        else:
                            if cmd == 'live_stop':
                                self._live_until = 0.0
                            else:
                                if cmd == 'game_close':
                                    self._close_game()
                                else:
                                    if cmd in ['chat_normal', 'chat_sangdan', 'whisper']:
                                        self._run_chat(cmd, arg)
                                    else:
                                        if cmd in ['gato_login', 'gato_launch']:
                                            self._run_gato(cmd, arg)
                                        else:
                                            if cmd == 'gato_stats':
                                                self._run_gato_stats()
                                            else:
                                                if cmd in ['click_l', 'click_r']:
                                                    self._run_click(cmd, arg)
                                                else:
                                                    if cmd == 'settings_reload':
                                                        threading.Thread(target=self._pull_settings, daemon=True).start()
                                                        self.vision.note('input', '[원격] 설정 다시 불러오기')
                                                    else:
                                                        if cmd == 'sched_start':
                                                            try:
                                                                c, b, r = (int(x) for x in (arg or '').split(',')[:3])
                                                            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                                                                self._note('스케쥴 시작 실패 — 값 오류')
                                                                return None
                                                            result = self.schedule_start(c, b, r)['schedule_result']
                                                            if result['ok']:
                                                                self._note(f"스케쥴 시작 — {result['cycles']}사이클 × {result['battles']}전투, 휴식 {result['rest_sec']}초")
                                                            else:
                                                                self._note(f"스케쥴 시작 실패 — {result['reason']}")
                                                            return None
                                                        else:
                                                            if cmd == 'sched_stop':
                                                                if not self._sched.get('active'):
                                                                    self._note('스케쥴이 실행 중이 아닙니다')
                                                                else:
                                                                    self.schedule_stop()
                                                                    self._note('스케쥴 정지')
                                                                return None
                                                            else:
                                                                key = self._REMOTE_KEYS.get(cmd)
                                                                if key is None:
                                                                    self.vision.note('input', f'[원격] 알 수 없는 명령: {cmd}')
                                                                    return
                                                                else:
                                                                    if not self.engine.hwnd and (not find_window()):
                                                                        self._note(f'[원격] {key.upper()} 입력 실패 — 거상 창 없음')
                                                                        return
                                                                    else:
                                                                        self.hands.press(key)
                                                                        self.vision.note('input', f'[원격] {key.upper()} 입력')
    def _restore_game_focus(self):
        # ***<module>.Api._restore_game_focus: Failure: Different control flow
        if not self.brain.running or not self.hands.focus_game():
                self.vision.note('input', '⚠ 고트 조작 후 거상 창 전면 복귀 실패')
    def _run_gato_stats(self):
        def _worker():
            note = lambda s: self.vision.note('input', s)
            try:
                import traders_ctl
            except Exception as e:
                note(f'[고트] 지표 읽기 불가: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')
                return None
            try:
                traders_ctl.ensure_battle_analysis(log=note)
                d = traders_ctl.battle_stats(log=note)
                ok = self.auth.upload_stats(d['state'], d['stats'])
                note(f"[고트] 지표 {len(d['stats'])}건 {('전송' if ok else '전송 실패')} (state={d['state']})")
            except traders_ctl.TradersError as e:
                note(f'[고트] 지표 읽기 실패: {e}')
                self.auth.upload_stats('error', {'오류': str(e)[:80]})
            except Exception as e:
                note(f'[고트] 지표 오류: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')
            finally:
                self._restore_game_focus()
        threading.Thread(target=_worker, daemon=True).start()
    def _run_click(self, cmd: str, arg: str):
        try:
            (xs, _, ys) = (arg or '').partition(',')
            (x, y) = (int(xs), int(ys))
        except ValueError:
            self.vision.note('input', f'[원격] 클릭 좌표 오류: {arg!r}')
            return None
        if not (0 <= x < REF_W and 0 <= y < REF_H):
            self.vision.note('input', f'[원격] 클릭 좌표 범위 밖: ({x}, {y})')
            return None
        if not self.engine.hwnd and not find_window():
            self._note('[원격] 클릭 실패 — 거상 창 없음')
            return None
        button = 'right' if cmd == 'click_r' else 'left'
        self.hands.click(x, y, button)
        word = '우' if button == 'right' else '좌'
        self.vision.note('input', f'[원격] {word}클릭 ({x}, {y})')
    def _run_gato(self, cmd: str, arg: str):
        parts = (arg or '').split(',')
        try:
            slot = max(1, min(3, int(parts[0] or 1)))
        except ValueError:
            slot = 1
        otp = parts[1].strip() if len(parts) > 1 else ''
        def _worker():
            try:
                import traders_ctl
            except Exception as e:
                self._note(f'고트 기능 사용 불가: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')
                return None
            try:
                msg = traders_ctl.login_slot(slot, otp, log=lambda s: self.vision.note('input', s)) if cmd == 'gato_login' else traders_ctl.launch_game(slot, log=lambda s: self.vision.note('input', s))
                self._note(f'고트: {msg}')
            except traders_ctl.TradersError as e:
                self._note(f'고트 실패: {e}')
            except Exception as e:
                self._note(f'고트 오류: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')
        threading.Thread(target=_worker, daemon=True).start()
    LIVE_LEASE_SEC = 25.0
    LIVE_INTERVAL = 0.35
    LIVE_SIZE = (640, 480)
    LIVE_Q = 50
    LIVE_MIN_DIFF = 0.4
    LIVE_KEYFRAME_SEC = 5.0
    def _live_extend(self):
        with self._live_lock:
            fresh = self._live_until < time.time()
            self._live_until = time.time() + self.LIVE_LEASE_SEC
            if self._live_thread is None or not self._live_thread.is_alive():
                self._live_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._live_loop, daemon=True, name='live-view')
                self._live_thread.start()
        if fresh:
            self.vision.note('input', '[원격] 라이브 보기 시작 (~1fps)')
    def _live_loop(self):
        cv_ = threading.Condition()
        done = threading.Event()
        slot = {'jpg': None}

        def upload(jpg):
            try:
                self.auth.upload_live(jpg)
            except Exception:
                pass

        def _sender():
            while not done.is_set():
                with cv_:
                    while not slot['jpg'] and not done.is_set():
                        cv_.wait(0.5)
                    jpg = slot['jpg']
                    slot['jpg'] = None
                if jpg:
                    upload(jpg)

        threading.Thread(target=_sender, daemon=True, name='live-send').start()
        fails = 0
        prev = None
        last_q = 0.0
        try:
            while time.time() < self._live_until:
                t0 = time.time()
                img = self.brain._grab_main()
                if img is None:
                    fails += 1
                    if fails >= 5:
                        self.vision.note('input', f'[원격] 라이브 중단 — {self._capture_fail_reason()}')
                        self._live_until = 0.0
                        break
                else:
                    fails = 0
                    small = cv2.resize(img, self.LIVE_SIZE, interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
                    changed = prev is None or cv2.absdiff(small, prev).mean() >= self.LIVE_MIN_DIFF
                    if changed or time.time() - last_q >= self.LIVE_KEYFRAME_SEC:
                        (ok, enc) = cv2.imencode('.jpg', small, [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, self.LIVE_Q])
                        if ok:
                            with cv_:
                                slot['jpg'] = enc.tobytes()
                                cv_.notify()
                            prev = small
                            last_q = time.time()
                time.sleep(max(0.0, self.LIVE_INTERVAL - (time.time() - t0)))
        finally:
            done.set()
            with cv_:
                cv_.notify()
        self.vision.note('input', '[원격] 라이브 보기 종료')

    def _capture_fail_reason(self) -> str:
        hwnd = find_window()
        if not hwnd:
            return '거상 창이 없습니다 (게임 종료됨)'
        try:
            (left, top, right, bottom) = win32gui.GetClientRect(hwnd)
            if right - left <= 0 or bottom - top <= 0:
                return '거상 창이 최소화되어 있습니다 (복원 후 다시 시도)'
        except Exception:
            return '거상 창을 읽을 수 없습니다'
        return '캡처에 실패했습니다 (일시적 오류일 수 있음)'
    def _close_game(self):
        hwnd = self.engine.hwnd or find_window()
        if not hwnd:
            self._note('게임 종료 — 거상 창이 이미 없습니다')
            return
        else:
            if self.brain.running:
                self.stop()
                time.sleep(0.3)
            win32gui.PostMessage(hwnd, win32con.WM_CLOSE, 0, 0)
            self._note('게임 종료 요청 (창 닫기)')
    def _run_chat(self, cmd: str, arg: str):
        def _worker():
            try:
                if cmd == 'whisper':
                    slot_s, _, text = (arg or '').partition('|')
                    try:
                        slot = max(1, min(9, int(slot_s)))
                    except ValueError:
                        slot = 1
                    self._discord_whisper(slot, text)
                else:
                    self._discord_chat(arg, 'sangdan' if cmd == 'chat_sangdan' else 'normal')
            except Exception as e:
                self.vision.note('input', f'[패널] 채팅 오류: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')
        threading.Thread(target=_worker, daemon=True).start()
    def start(self):
        if net.LOCAL and not self.auth.ok:
            self.auth.login(self.auth_id)
        if not self.auth.ok:
            self.vision.note('input', f'시작 차단 — {self.auth.label()} (로그인 필요)')
            return self.status()
        else:
            if self.auth.must_update():
                self.vision.note('input', f'시작 차단 — 업데이트 필요 (최소 {self.auth.min_ver})')
                return self.status()
            block = self.brain.start_block_reason()
            if block:
                self.vision.note('input', f'시작 차단 — {block}')
                return self.status()
            else:
                self.vision.reset()
                if not self.hands.focus_game():
                    self.vision.note('input', '⚠ 거상 창을 전면으로 올리지 못했습니다 — 캡처/입력이 실패할 수 있습니다')
                self.engine.start()
                self.brain.start()
                self._run_since = time.time()
                self._ensure_gato_analysis()
                return self.status()
    def _ensure_gato_analysis(self):
        def _worker():
            note = lambda s: self.vision.note('input', s)
            try:
                import traders_ctl
            except Exception as e:
                note(f'[고트] 전투 분석 확인 불가: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')
                return None
            try:
                note(f'[고트] {traders_ctl.ensure_battle_analysis(log=note)}')
            except traders_ctl.TradersError as e:
                note(f'[고트] 전투 분석 시작 실패: {e}')
            except Exception as e:
                note(f'[고트] 전투 분석 오류: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')
            finally:
                self._restore_game_focus()
        threading.Thread(target=_worker, daemon=True).start()
    def stop(self):
        self._sched_stop.set()
        self._sched['active'] = False
        self._sched['resting'] = False
        self._stop_bot()
        return self.status()
    def _stop_bot(self):
        self.brain.stop()
        self.engine.stop()
        self.hands.release_all()
    def stop_grace(self, author='', reason='', user_requested=True):
        self._sched_stop.set()
        self._sched['active'] = False
        self._sched['resting'] = False
        self.brain._stop_bot(reason or (f'■ 원격 정지({author})' if author else '■ 원격 정지'), user_requested=user_requested)
    def schedule_start(self, cycles, battles, rest_sec):
        def _result(ok, reason=''):
            status = self.status()
            result = {'ok': ok, 'reason': reason}
            if ok:
                result.update(cycles=cycles, battles=battles, rest_sec=rest_sec)
            status['schedule_result'] = result
            return status
        self._sched_stop.set()
        t = self._sched_thread
        if t and t.is_alive() and (t is not threading.current_thread()):
                    t.join(timeout=2.0)
        try:
            cycles = max(1, int(float(cycles)))
            battles = max(1, int(float(battles)))
            rest_sec = max(0, int(float(rest_sec)))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            self.vision.note('input', '스케쥴 값 오류 — 숫자로 입력')
            return _result(False, '값 오류 — 숫자로 입력')
        if net.LOCAL and not self.auth.ok:
            self.auth.login(self.auth_id)
        if not self.auth.ok:
            self.vision.note('input', f'스케쥴 차단 — {self.auth.label()} (로그인 필요)')
            return _result(False, f'{self.auth.label()} (로그인 필요)')
        else:
            if self.auth.must_update():
                self.vision.note('input', f'스케쥴 차단 — 업데이트 필요 (최소 {self.auth.min_ver})')
                return _result(False, f'업데이트 필요 (최소 {self.auth.min_ver})')
            block = self.brain.start_block_reason()
            if block:
                self.vision.note('input', f'스케쥴 차단 — {block}')
                return _result(False, block)
            else:
                s = config.load()
                s['sched_cycles'], s['sched_battles'], s['sched_rest_sec'] = (cycles, battles, rest_sec)
                if not config.save(s):
                    self.vision.note('input', '스케쥴 차단 — 설정 저장 실패')
                    return _result(False, '설정 저장 실패')
                else:
                    self._sched_stop.clear()
                    self._sched = {'active': True, 'cycle': 0, 'cycles': cycles, 'battles': 0, 'target': battles, 'resting': False, 'rest_left': 0}
                    self._sched_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._sched_loop, args=(cycles, battles, rest_sec), daemon=True)
                    self._sched_thread.start()
                    return _result(True)
    def schedule_stop(self):
        return self.stop()
    def _sched_loop(self, cycles, battles, rest_sec):
        start_total = self.brain.battle_count
        for c in range(1, cycles + 1):
            if self._sched_stop.is_set():
                break
            self._sched.update(cycle=c, battles=0, resting=False)
            base = self.brain.battle_count
            self.vision.note('input', f'[스케쥴] 사이클 {c}/{cycles} 시작 (목표 {battles}전투)')
            if not self.brain.running:
                self.start()
            self.brain.sched_target = base + battles
            while not self._sched_stop.is_set():
                done = self.brain.battle_count - base
                self._sched['battles'] = done
                if done >= battles:
                    break
                if not self.brain.running:
                    self.vision.note('input', '[스케쥴] 봇이 멈춰 스케쥴 중단')
                    self.notifier.text(f'⚠️ 스케쥴 중단 — 봇 정지 (사이클 {c}/{cycles}, 총 {self.brain.battle_count - start_total}전투)')
                    break
                time.sleep(1.0)
            if self._sched_stop.is_set():
                break
            if c < cycles and rest_sec > 0:
                self.vision.note('input', f'[스케쥴] 사이클 {c} 완료 — {rest_sec}초 휴식 (색적만 정지)')
                self._sched.update(resting=True, rest_left=rest_sec)
                self.brain.rest_mode = True
                end = time.time() + rest_sec
                try:
                    while not self._sched_stop.is_set():
                        left = int(round(end - time.time()))
                        if left <= 0:
                            break
                        self._sched['rest_left'] = left
                        if not self.brain.running:
                            self.vision.note('input', '[스케쥴] 휴식 중 봇 정지 — 스케쥴 중단')
                            self.notifier.text(f'⚠️ 스케쥴 중단 — 휴식 중 봇 정지 (사이클 {c}/{cycles})')
                            break
                        time.sleep(1.0)
                finally:
                    self.brain.rest_mode = False
                    self._sched['resting'] = False
        if not self._sched_stop.is_set():
            self.vision.note('input', '[스케쥴] 전체 완료')
            self.notifier.text(f'🏁 스케쥴 완료 — {cycles}사이클 · 총 {self.brain.battle_count - start_total}전투')
            self._stop_bot()
        self._sched.update(active=False, resting=False)
        self.brain.sched_target = None
    def _on_brain_stopped(self):
        self.engine.stop()
        self.hands.release_all()
    def _auto_restart_bot(self):
        """The bot stopped itself (a false no-fight timeout, a transient capture glitch, etc. - never
        a user/schedule stop, see Brain._auto_restart). Run the SAME sequence F3/the panel start button
        does, not just brain.start(): the engine was already stopped by _on_brain_stopped above, and
        the game window needs re-focusing."""
        self.vision.note('input', '[자동재시작] 재시작 시도')
        try:
            res = self.start()
            if not self.brain.running:
                self.vision.note('input', f'[자동재시작] 실패 — {self.brain.start_block_reason() or self.auth.label()}')
        except Exception as e:
            self.vision.note('input', f'[자동재시작] 오류: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')
    _WINS_TTL = 2.0
    def _find_windows_cached(self):
        now = time.time()
        if now - self._wins_at > self._WINS_TTL:
            self._wins = find_windows()
            self._wins_at = now
        return self._wins
    def tick(self, since_seq: int=0, chat_seq: int=0):
        return {'status': self.status(), 'events': self.vision.events_since(int(since_seq)), 'chat': self.chat(chat_seq)}

        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return None
        try:
            u = ctypes.windll.user32
            max_w, max_h = (u.GetSystemMetrics(0), u.GetSystemMetrics(1))
        except Exception:
            max_w, max_h = (3840, 2160)
        return (max(WIN_MIN_W, min(w, max_w)), max(WIN_MIN_H, min(h, max_h)))

        size = self._clamp_size(w, h)
        if size and webview.windows:
                webview.windows[0].resize(*size)
