===== FILE: ./build_windows_nuitka.ps1 (    7328 chars) =====
# Builds dist-installer\GatoSetup.exe from a NUITKA-compiled build instead of PyInstaller.
# Nuitka turns the .py files into C and compiles them, so the shipped app carries no
# .pyc bytecode that could be decompiled back into readable source.
#   powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File build_windows_nuitka.ps1
# Needs: install.ps1 already run once (creates .venv), plus MSVC (the VS Build Tools).
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
Set-Location -Path $PSScriptRoot
$py = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '.venv\Scripts\python.exe'
if (-not (Test-Path $py)) { throw 'Run install.ps1 first (creates .venv).' }
$ProgressPreference = 'SilentlyContinue'
$ver = (Get-Content 'VERSION' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object -First 1)
if (-not $ver) { $ver = '1.0.0' }

& $py -m pip install --upgrade nuitka ordered-set zstandard
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'pip install nuitka failed' }

# Local modules that are imported lazily or by name; Nuitka's static analysis can miss those.
$mods = @('agf', 'autosupply', 'captcha', 'chat', 'remote_link', 'agent_link', 'update', 'second_pw',
          'sniffer', 'traders_ctl', 'watchdog', 'sprite_match', 'notify', 'net', 'dpi', 'wgc',
          'combat', 'detector', 'capture', 'input', 'vision', 'brain', 'prism', 'config', 'jochul')
# 'webview' is deliberately absent: pywebview ships its own Nuitka plugin, and also listing it as a
# user include-package makes Nuitka abort with "Conflict between user and plugin decision".
# 'scapy' is imported lazily inside sniffer.py, so static analysis never sees it and it has to be
# listed by hand or the chat sniffer is silently absent from the build.
$pkgs = @('websockets', 'cryptography', 'windows_capture', 'pythonnet', 'clr_loader', 'comtypes',
          'uiautomation', 'win32com', 'win32timezone', 'psutil', 'certifi', 'requests', 'scapy')

# Everything below is followed automatically from our own imports; the heavy ones are additionally
# pruned because MSVC-compiling all of torch/scapy/ultralytics takes hours. torch's own Nuitka plugin
# still supplies its DLLs and package data. The selftest gate after the build proves nothing broke.
$nofollow = @(
    # 2026-11-22: audited EVERY entry in this list by actually importing torch/torchvision/
    # ultralytics/scapy in a real interpreter and checking sys.modules - found 14 of the 17
    # ML-related entries previously here are unconditionally imported by their own parent
    # package (torch._dynamo, torch._inductor, torch._export, torch.ao, torch.onnx, torch.jit,
    # torch.fx, torch.multiprocessing, torch.testing, torchvision.datasets/io/models.video,
    # scapy.contrib, scapy.layers.tls, ultralytics.models.rtdetr - ALL present in sys.modules
    # after a plain `import torch/torchvision/ultralytics/scapy.all`). Nofollowing any of them
    # is not a compile-time optimization, it's a guaranteed ModuleNotFoundError at exe startup -
    # confirmed the hard way across three separate multi-hour CI failures today (torch.distributed
    # in 44941920737, torch.utils.data.datapipes in 44963584621, torch.testing in 45001629835,
    # each one only found after a full ~4h Nuitka compile). Only the three entries below were
    # verified NOT present in sys.modules after the same import, so only these are actually safe
    # to skip. Do not add anything back to this list without re-running that same check first.
    'torch.utils.tensorboard', 'torch.utils.benchmark', 'ultralytics.trackers',
    'tkinter', 'matplotlib', 'pytest', 'IPython', 'notebook', 'setuptools', 'pip'
)

$nargs = @(
    '-m', 'nuitka',
    '--standalone',
    '--assume-yes-for-downloads',
    '--msvc=latest',
    '--windows-console-mode=disable',
    '--windows-uac-admin',
    '--windows-icon-from-ico=assets/app.ico',
    '--output-dir=distn',
    '--output-filename=Gato.exe',
    '--company-name=J0SEPH-K',
    '--product-name=GATO',
    # Nuitka refuses version info without a version; keep it in step with VERSION.
    ('--product-version=' + $ver),
    '--include-data-dir=ui=ui',
    '--include-data-dir=assets=assets',
    '--include-data-file=settings.default.json=settings.default.json',
    '--include-data-file=VERSION=VERSION',
    # Torch JIT has to be decided explicitly or Nuitka warns; we only do eager CPU inference.
    '--module-parameter=torch-disable-jit=yes',
    # A pruned module that something imports anyway should raise at import time, not hard-fail the
    # whole build with an ImportError that looks like a compiler bug.
    '--no-deployment-flag=excluded-module-usage',
    '--low-memory'
)
foreach ($m in $mods) { $nargs += "--include-module=$m" }
foreach ($p in $pkgs) { $nargs += "--include-package=$p" }
foreach ($n in $nofollow) { $nargs += "--nofollow-import-to=$n" }
$nargs += 'run_gato.py'

Write-Host '== Nuitka =='
& $py @nargs
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Nuitka failed' }
if (-not (Test-Path 'distn\run_gato.dist\Gato.exe')) { throw 'distn\run_gato.dist\Gato.exe missing' }

Write-Host '== self-test of the compiled app =='
# The packaged app has no console, so its own selftest log and raw stderr are the only evidence
# when it dies. Capture both and dump them before deciding.
$st = Start-Process -FilePath 'distn\run_gato.dist\Gato.exe' -ArgumentList '--selftest' -Wait -PassThru `
    -RedirectStandardOutput 'selftest.stdout.txt' -RedirectStandardError 'selftest.stderr.txt'
foreach ($p in @('distn\run_gato.dist\selftest.txt', "$env:LOCALAPPDATA\GATO\selftest.txt")) {
    if (Test-Path $p) { Write-Host "--- $p"; Get-Content $p }
}
foreach ($p in @('selftest.stdout.txt', 'selftest.stderr.txt')) {
    if ((Test-Path $p) -and (Get-Item $p).Length -gt 0) { Write-Host "--- $p"; Get-Content $p }
}
Write-Host "exit code: $($st.ExitCode)"
if ($st.ExitCode -ne 0) { throw 'compiled self-test failed (see selftest.txt above)' }

# With Nuitka the .py files are compiled, so any copy left in the dist folder is only there for
# tracebacks. Drop them so the shipped tree contains no readable source.
Get-ChildItem 'distn\run_gato.dist' -Filter '*.py' -Recurse | Remove-Item -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

if (-not (Test-Path 'installer\vc_redist.x64.exe')) {
    Invoke-WebRequest 'https://aka.ms/vs/17/release/vc_redist.x64.exe' -OutFile 'installer\vc_redist.x64.exe'
}
$iscc = @("$env:ProgramFiles\Inno Setup 6\ISCC.exe", "${env:ProgramFiles(x86)}\Inno Setup 6\ISCC.exe", 'C:\InnoSetup\ISCC.exe') | Where-Object { Test-Path $_ } | Select-Object -First 1
if (-not $iscc) {
    Write-Host 'Installing Inno Setup...'
    Invoke-WebRequest 'https://github.com/jrsoftware/issrc/releases/download/is-6_7_4/innosetup-6.7.4.exe' -OutFile "$env:TEMP\innosetup.exe"
    Start-Process "$env:TEMP\innosetup.exe" -ArgumentList '/VERYSILENT /SUPPRESSMSGBOXES /NORESTART /SP- /DIR=C:\InnoSetup' -Wait
    $iscc = 'C:\InnoSetup\ISCC.exe'
}
Write-Host '== Inno Setup =='
# gato.iss packages ..\dist\Gato; a compiled build lands in distn\run_gato.dist.
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path 'dist' | Out-Null
if (Test-Path 'dist\Gato') { Remove-Item 'dist\Gato' -Recurse -Force }
Move-Item 'distn\run_gato.dist' 'dist\Gato'
& $iscc "/DAppVersion=$ver" 'installer\gato.iss'
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Inno Setup failed' }
Get-Item 'dist-installer\GatoSetup.exe' | Select-Object FullName, @{n='MB';e={[math]::Round($_.Length/1MB)}}

===== FILE: ./notify.py (    7173 chars) =====
# Source Generated with Decompyle++
# File: notify.pyc (Python 3.11)

from __future__ import annotations
import threading

try:
    import requests
except Exception:
    requests = None


class Notifier:
    
    def __init__(self, get_discord, get_ntfy, log, get_tag):
        self._get_discord = get_discord
        self._get_ntfy = get_ntfy
        self._log = log
        self._get_tag = get_tag or (lambda: 'Gato_super')

    
    def _tag(self) -> str:
        return (self._get_tag() or 'Gato_super').strip()

    
    def text(self, msg: str) -> None:
        if not requests:
            return None
        body = f'''[{self._tag()}] {msg}'''
        while self._get_ntfy():
            dc = ''.strip()
            nt = ''.strip()
            if dc:
                threading.Thread(target = self._send_discord, args = (dc, body), daemon = True).start()
        if nt:
            threading.Thread(target = self._send_ntfy, args = (nt, body), daemon = True).start()
            return None

    
    def image(self, msg: str, img_bgr, name: str = 'capture.jpg') -> None:
        if not requests:
            return None
        body = f'''[{self._tag()}] {msg}'''
        while self._get_ntfy():
            dc = ''.strip()
            nt = ''.strip()
            if dc:
                if img_bgr:
                    threading.Thread(target = self._send_discord_image, args = (dc, body, img_bgr, name), daemon = True).start()
                else:
                    threading.Thread(target = self._send_discord, args = (dc, body), daemon = True).start()
        if nt:
            threading.Thread(target = self._send_ntfy, args = (nt, body), daemon = True).start()
            return None

    
    def _send_discord(self, url: str, body: str) -> None:
        
        try:
            requests.post(url, json = {
                'content': body }, timeout = 5)
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            self._log(f'''디스코드 알림 실패: {e}''')
            return None


    
    def _send_discord_image(self, url: str, body: str, img_bgr, name: str) -> None:
        
        try:
            import cv2
            import numpy as np
            (ok, buf) = cv2.imencode('.jpg', np.ascontiguousarray(img_bgr), [
                cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY,
                80])
            if ok:
                requests.post(url, data = {
                    'content': body }, files = {
                    'file': (name, buf.tobytes(), 'image/jpeg') }, timeout = 12)
                return None
            requests.post(url, json = {
                'content': body }, timeout = 5)
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            self._log(f'''디스코드 이미지 실패: {e}''')
            return None


    
    def _send_ntfy(self, topic: str, body: str) -> None:
        
        try:
            requests.post(ntfy_url(topic), data = body.encode('utf-8'), timeout = 5)
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            self._log(f'''ntfy 알림 실패: {e}''')
            return None




def ntfy_url(topic: str) -> str:
    if not topic:
        topic = ''.strip().strip('/')
        if not topic:
            return ''
        if topic.startswith(('http://', 'https://')):
            return topic
        return 'https://ntfy.sh/' + topic

_DISCORD_API = 'https://discord.com/api/v10'

class BotPoller(threading.Thread):
    
    def __init__(self, get_token, get_channel, on_message, log, poll):
        super().__init__(daemon = True)
        self.get_token = get_token
        self.get_channel = get_channel
        self.on_message = on_message
        self.log = log
        self.poll = poll
        self._last_id = None
        self._cur_channel = None
        self._stop_event = threading.Event()
        self._session = requests.Session() if requests else None

    
    def stop(self):
        self._stop_event.set()

    
    @staticmethod
    def _retry_after(r, default, cap):
        try:
            v = r.headers.get('Retry-After')
            if v is None:
                try:
                    v = (r.json() or { }).get('retry_after')
                except Exception:
                    v = None
            return max(1.0, min(cap, float(v)))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return default

    def _get(self, token, path):
        return self._session.get(f'''{_DISCORD_API}{path}''', headers = {
            'Authorization': f'''Bot {token}''' }, timeout = 6)

    
    def _newest(self, token, channel):
        
        try:
            d = self._get(token, f'''/channels/{channel}/messages?limit=1''').json()
            if isinstance(d, list) and d:
                return d[0]['id']
        except Exception:
            pass


    
    def run(self):
        if not self._session:
            return None
        while self.get_token() and self.get_channel():
            token = (self.get_token() or '').strip()
            channel = (self.get_channel() or '').strip()
            if not token or not channel:
                continue
            
            try:
                if self._cur_channel != channel:
                    self._cur_channel = channel
                    self._last_id = self._newest(token, channel)
                url = f'''/channels/{channel}/messages?limit=10'''
                if self._last_id:
                    url += f'''&after={self._last_id}'''
                r = self._get(token, url)
                if r.status_code == 429:
                    wait = self._retry_after(r)
                    self.log(f'''[디코] 요청 과다(429) — {wait:.0f}초 대기 (봇 토큰을 여러 PC가 공유하면 상시 발생)''')
                    self._stop_event.wait(wait)
                    continue
                if r.status_code != 200:
                    self.log(f'''[디코] 응답 {r.status_code} — 토큰/채널/인텐트 확인''')
                    self._stop_event.wait(10)
                    continue
                msgs = r.json()
                if not isinstance(msgs, list):
                    continue
                if not self._last_id:
                    if msgs:
                        self._last_id = msgs[0]['id']
                    continue
                for m in reversed(msgs):
                    self._last_id = m['id']
                    try:
                        if m.get('author', {}).get('bot'):
                            continue
                        content = (m.get('content') or '').strip()
                        author = m.get('author', {}).get('username', '?')
                        self.on_message(content, author)
                    except Exception as e:
                        self.log(f'''[디코] 메시지 처리 오류: {e}''')
                        continue
                    except Exception as e:
                        self.log(f'''[디코] 폴링 오류: {e}''')
                    if self._stop_event.wait(self.poll):
                        return None
            except Exception as e:
                self.log(f'''[디코] 폴링 오류: {e}''')
                self._stop_event.wait(5)


===== FILE: ./docs/설치안내.md (    6474 chars) =====
# 거상 자동사냥 프로그램 — 설치 및 실행 안내

이 안내서는 처음 설치하시는 분도 순서대로 따라 하실 수 있게 쓰였습니다.
**순서를 건너뛰지 마시고 위에서부터 그대로 따라와 주세요.**

- 준비물: 윈도우 10 또는 11 (64비트), 게임이 설치된 PC
- 소요 시간: 약 20분 (대부분 다운로드 시간입니다)
- 관리자 권한이 필요합니다 (프로그램이 다른 프로그램에 키보드·마우스 신호를 보냅니다)

---

## 1단계 — 프로그램 폴더 내려받기

1. 받으신 `gato_src` 폴더를 **`C:\Gato`** 에 옮겨 주세요.
   - 압축 파일이면 먼저 압축을 풀어 주세요.
   - **폴더 안에 `run_gato.py`, `brain.py`, `assets`, `ui` 가 보여야 정상입니다.**
   - 경로에 한글이나 공백이 섞이면 문제가 생길 수 있으니 `C:\Gato` 처럼 짧게 잡아 주세요.
2. `C:\Gato` 폴더는 **삭제하지 마세요.** 프로그램이 이 폴더 안의 파일을 계속 읽습니다.

> 이미 예전 버전을 받으셨다면, 덮어쓰기 전에 기존 폴더를 백업해 두세요.

---

## 2단계 — 파이썬 설치

1. https://www.python.org/downloads/release/python-3119/ 에 접속합니다.
2. 맨 아래 **Windows installer (64-bit)** 를 받아 실행합니다.
3. 설치 첫 화면에서 **`Add python.exe to PATH`** 를 **반드시 체크**합니다. (제일 중요합니다)
4. `Install Now` 를 누릅니다.
5. 설치가 끝나면 **PC를 한 번 재부팅**합니다.

확인: 윈도우키 → `cmd` 입력 → 엔터 → 검은 창에 아래를 입력해 엔터.

```
py -3.11 --version
```

`Python 3.11.9` 처럼 나오면 정상입니다. 빨간 에러가 나오면 2단계를 다시 해주세요.

---

## 3단계 — 필요한 라이브러리 설치

1. 윈도우키 → `cmd` 입력 → **마우스 오른쪽 클릭 → 관리자 권한으로 실행**
2. 아래 두 줄을 **한 줄씩** 입력하고 각각 엔터를 누릅니다.

```
cd /d C:\Gato
```

```
py -3.11 -m pip install -r requirements.txt --extra-index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu
```

- 용량이 커서 **5~15분** 걸립니다. 멈춘 것처럼 보여도 기다려 주세요.
- `Successfully installed ...` 가 나오면 성공입니다.
- 빨간 글씨가 잔뜩 나오며 멈추면, **창을 닫지 말고 그 화면을 캡처해서 보내주세요.**

설치 확인:

```
py -3.11 -c "import cv2, torch, win32gui, webview; print('OK')"
```

`OK` 가 나오면 정상입니다.

---

## 4단계 — 화면 설정 맞추기

프로그램은 **1024 × 768** 기준으로 화면을 인식합니다. 게임 화면을 이 크기에 가깝게 맞춰 주세요.

1. 게임을 실행하고 **창 모드**로 바꿉니다.
2. 게임 창 크기를 1024×768 근처로 맞춥니다.
3. 게임 안에서 창 크기 조절 기능이 있으면 그걸 쓰시고, 없으면 윈도우 화면 배율을 **100%** 로 맞춰 주세요.
   - 설정 → 시스템 → 디스플레이 → 배율 및 레이아웃 → **100%**
4. 캐릭터를 **사냥터에 세워 둡니다.**

---

## 5단계 — 실행

1. **게임을 먼저 실행하고 로그인합니다.**
2. 윈도우키 → `cmd` → **관리자 권한으로 실행**
3. 아래를 입력합니다.

```
cd /d C:\Gato
```

```
py -3.11 run_gato.py
```

4. 검은 창에 점검 문구가 나오고, 문제가 없으면 프로그램 창이 뜹니다.

**점검 문구가 나오면 읽어 주세요.** 아래는 각 문구의 뜻과 해결법입니다.

| 나오는 문구 | 뜻 | 해결 |
|---|---|---|
| `Python 3.x is too old` | 파이썬 버전이 낮음 | 2단계 다시 |
| `Administrator rights are required` | 관리자 권한 아님 | cmd를 관리자 권한으로 다시 실행 |
| `The game window was not found` | 게임이 안 켜져 있음 | 게임 실행 후 다시 |
| `A required package is missing` | 라이브러리 설치 안 됨 | 3단계 다시 |
| `asset ... missing` | 폴더 복사가 덜 됨 | 1단계 다시 |

---

## 6단계 — 프로그램 사용

1. 프로그램 창이 뜨면 **몬스터를 고릅니다.** (기본 목록이 들어 있습니다)
2. **시작** 버튼을 누릅니다.
3. 처음 30초는 모델을 불러오느라 **화면이 멈춘 것처럼 보일 수 있습니다.** 기다려 주세요.
4. 실행 중에는 **마우스를 잡지 마세요.** 프로그램이 직접 마우스를 움직입니다.
5. 멈추려면 프로그램 창에서 **정지** 를 누르거나 창을 닫습니다.

---

## 자주 막히는 곳

**`py` 를 찾을 수 없다고 나옴**
→ 파이썬 설치 시 PATH 체크를 안 하신 경우입니다. 2단계를 다시 하시고 재부팅하세요.

**`pip` 설치 중 빨간 에러**
→ 대부분 관리자 권한 문제입니다. cmd를 관리자 권한으로 다시 열고 3단계를 다시 하세요.

**창은 뜨는데 시작이 안 됨**
→ 화면의 안내 문구를 읽어 주세요. "모델 로드 실패"가 뜨면 3단계 라이브러리 설치가 덜 된 것입니다.

**시작은 되는데 사냥을 안 함**
→ 게임 창이 1024×768과 너무 다르거나, 게임 창이 맨 앞에 있지 않은 경우입니다. 4단계를 다시 확인하세요.

**캡차(자동입력 방지)가 안 넘어감**
→ 캡차 종류별로 화면이 등록되어야 합니다. 캡차 화면을 캡처해서 보내주세요.

---

## 꼭 지켜주세요

- 프로그램은 **본인 PC에서만** 쓰시고, 폴더를 다른 사람에게 복사해 주지 마세요.
- 실행 중에는 **게임을 최소화하지 마세요.**
- 백신이 파일을 막으면 `C:\Gato` 폴더를 예외 처리해 주세요.
- 잘 안 되면 **검은 창(명령 프롬프트) 화면을 캡처**해서 보내주세요. 그게 가장 빠릅니다.

---

## 아직 검증되지 않은 부분 (미리 알려드립니다)

이 프로그램은 원본 프로그램의 코드를 복구해서 만든 것입니다. 문법과 파일 구성은 원본과 100% 동일하게 맞췄고, 제 쪽에서는 화면·설정·시작까지 확인했습니다. 다만 아래는 **실제 윈도우 PC에서만 확인 가능**해서, 첫 실행에서 문제가 나올 수 있습니다.

1. 실제 화면 캡처·키보드 입력 같은 윈도우 기능
2. 실제 게임 화면에서의 몬스터 인식
3. 일부 특수한 전투 상황

첫 실행에서 막히면 **정상적인 과정**입니다. 화면을 보내주시면 바로 고쳐 드립니다.

===== FILE: ./docs/JOCHUL_FINDINGS.md (    8570 chars) =====
# 조철 암행어사 — what the game actually does (from real footage, 2026-11-20)

Source: YouTube FG723pxOhO8 (2020, 960x720, ~11 distinct frames/s), six rounds. Start slot = slot under the gold 마패
at the start (read by the medallion-colour detector, consistent over ~15 frames per round). End slot = where the medallion
sits in the last ~4.5 s, same detector. NOT checked by eye (the image-description tool returned no text for these frames).

| round | start slot | end slot |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 4 | 2 |
| 2 | 5 | 4 |
| 3 | 4 | 4 |
| 4 | 4 | 5 or 3 (ambiguous: 15 vs 13 votes) |
| 5 | 1 | 5 or 3 (ambiguous: 14 vs 10 votes) |
| 6 | 5 | 4 (17 of 36 votes; others scattered) |

## Mechanics
- 5 identical characters stand in a row (x = 65,145,225,305,385 in the 447x351 board crop, y~140), a counter counts 5 -> 0
  and the 마패 floats over one of them (bobbing: visible in ~40% of frames).
- At 0 the characters scatter for ~8 s: they run around, change hair/body colour, scale up and down (depth), overlap each
  other, and animals (bear, tiger, horse, wolf) run through. The scene tint changes (green / blue / orange / white).
  There are NO clean pairwise swaps.
- Then they line up again (colours still random for a moment), a 10 s answer timer starts, the player clicks one.
  The 마패 then re-appears above the real holder (green highlight) = free ground truth for every round.
- Board layout matches the macro's coordinates (CLICK_X - 287, CLICK_Y - 231) to within 4 px.

## Measured on those six rounds
- Old macro pipeline (15 fps sampling + swap-pair decoder): 0/6 against the end slots above (rounds 4-5 count either candidate wrong) (it only ever finds 10-11 "swaps").
- My all-frames swap decoder: not applicable (the game has no swaps).
- Two tracking-by-detection prototypes on the YOLO detections (Hungarian + constant velocity; min-cost flow over all
  frames): 1/6 and 0-1/6 = chance. The detector sees on average 3.8-4.2 of the 5 characters per frame in the scatter.
- Start slot by gold-medallion colour (skin independent), voted over frames: 6/6, no false votes
  (the old navy-hair pixel pattern found nothing on this footage; it is specific to one skin).

## What is needed for a real solver
Real-rate (60 fps) capture of live rounds. `brain._handle_jochul` already saves every failed round to `debug/jochul_fail`
and now also stores `start_slot` and `reveal_slot` in its meta, so each round labels itself.

## More attempts on the same six rounds (2026-11-20, later)
- Ultralytics built-in trackers (ByteTrack, BoT-SORT) on `jochul.pt`, 256/640 px: identities are reassigned 160-300 times per
  round, the start character's id never survives to the end (0/4 on the rounds with a certain end slot).
- OpenCV MOG2 (and a median background) + Norfair Kalman tracker, 32 parameter settings: best 1/4 = chance. In the scatter the
  foreground gives only ~2-4 separate blobs per frame (characters overlap each other and animals), so tracks die and are
  re-created (60-120 ids per round).
- Caveat for all of these: the clip has ~11 distinct frames/s. Live 60 fps footage may track far better; unproven.
- New skin (pink twin-tail girls, user screenshot): `jochul.pt` finds all 5 (class 0, conf 0.86-0.93) - the model already
  covers more than one skin. The gold-colour medallion rule (V >= 170, area >= 60) does NOT find the medallion in that
  screenshot (darker, smaller): the start-slot rule is validated only on the 2020 clip's rendering.

## Design note from the user (another model's advice), assessed 2026-11-20
Proposal: clean background plate (or MOG2 with learningRate 0) + top-5 blobs + constant-velocity prediction + Hungarian
matching + coasting while blobs merge + `mss` capture at 60-120 fps + key by template match.
- Already tried on the real clip (Hungarian tracker, min-cost flow, MOG2/median + Norfair): chance level at ~11 fps.
- Untested and still plausible: the same pipeline at 60+ fps live. The only thing not yet measured.
- Does not fit this game as written: no fixed clean plate (hay-bale positions differ per round), "5 largest blobs" breaks when
  animals cross, the key template must not depend on the skin.
- Next step stays: native-fps rounds with `start_slot`/`reveal_slot` labels, then replay on the Mac.

## Round recorder (shipped in 1.0.1, 2026-11-20)
Every 조철 round is now recorded in the background, every distinct frame (WGC frame counter, up to 45/s), JPEG q92 of the
board region, to `debug/jochul_rounds/round_<time>/` (`NNNNN.jpg` + `meta.json`: times, start slot, answer, marker votes,
reveal slot, effective fps, capture mode). Last 40 rounds are kept, ~30-50 MB each. Disable with `jochul_record: false`.
To tune: copy the folder to the Mac and run `python dev/tools/jochul_replay.py <folder>` (tracker vs reveal labels).
The pink-skin screenshot shows the gold-colour reveal read fails there; in that case label by eye from the last frames.

## First live round on 1.0.1 (client PC, 2026-11-20 15:37, native 44.4 fps, 1033 frames, wgc-every-frame)
- Start slot 2 (medallion rule found it on this skin: 3 votes). Macro decoded 15 swaps, clicked slot 5.
- Ground truth: slot 5 (user watched the recorded video and confirmed the macro was correct). n = 1, so this proves nothing about
  the solver: a random click is right 20% of the time.
- After the click: the clicked character turns green, the 마패 shows over it, the board closes ~1.7 s later. The macro's reveal read
  was moved from +3 s to +0.9 s because the board is already gone at +3 s. The old "popup gone = passed" check is not a
  correctness signal (the board closes either way; unverified whether a wrong click looks different).
- Detector on native-fps frames: Ultralytics `jochul.pt` finds all 5 in the row at the start/end (100%), 4.5 of 5 per frame in
  the scatter (58% of frames show all 5). Background-blob foreground: only 2.3 blobs per frame.
- Offline tracker (dev/tools/jochul_replay.py) on this round: YOLO detections + velocity Hungarian -> slot 5 in 8/9 settings at
  44 fps, 7/9 at 22 fps and at 11 fps; blob foreground -> 3/9. Agreement with the truth on ONE round only.
- Needed before changing the macro: more labelled rounds (copy `debug/jochul_rounds`, label by watching the video).

## Replaced the swap-model decoder with a velocity/Hungarian centroid tracker as the live answer (2026-11-20)
Ran the actual solver code (not an offline experiment) against the ONE real, user-confirmed-correct client
round (start slot 2, true end slot 5): BOTH swap-model decoders got it WRONG, under every tuning tried
(`periodic_decode` on the 15fps-resampled grid -> 1; `dp_decode`, time-based, period range widened up to 3 s
-> 3 or 4, never 5). The live macro's earlier "correct" click with this exact algorithm was therefore very
likely luck (n=1, 20% baseline), not the solver working, which matches the user calling the success rate
"so shit".

A much simpler method already written for offline replay - velocity-predicted Hungarian matching of the
YOLO detector's own centroids (`jochul.track_centroids`, ported from `dev/tools/jochul_replay.py`'s
`track()`, scipy replaced with a tiny brute-force 5x5-ish assignment since scipy is not bundled in the app) -
got this SAME round right in all 9 parameter combinations tried (gate in {100,150,220} x decay in
{0.6,0.8,0.95}), both with scipy and with the ported scipy-free assignment, and even on a 1-in-5 subsampled
version of the capture (207 of the 1033 frames). This is now the PRIMARY live answer
(`JochulSolver.solve_tracked`, wired into `brain._handle_jochul_inner`). `periodic_decode` is still run and
logged for comparison on every round (and a mismatch between the two triggers the same frame-dump/upload
path as before), but it no longer decides the click.

The capture feeding the solve step changed too: `jochul.record()` (old, fixed 15 fps / 13.5 s polling loop)
was replaced with `jochul.record_all()` (same all-native-frame capture the evidence recorder already used),
since the tracker needs real motion, not a coarse sample.

Caveat: n=1 confirmed round. 9/9 parameter robustness on that one case is evidence of being clearly less
fragile than the swap decoders (which were wrong AND unstable across tunings), not a proven accuracy number.
A real hit rate still needs many more labelled live rounds - every round keeps recording as before
(`debug/jochul_rounds`), and the fixture `dev/fixtures/jochul_round1` now also serves as a permanent
regression test (`dev/test_jochul_tracker.py`) so this exact real case can never silently regress.

===== FILE: ./docs/REMOTE_SPEC.md (   57474 chars) =====
# Gato 원격 — remote control plane spec (phone → hub → N PCs)

Status: design, ready for GABRIEL. Author: DEMIURGE, 2026-11-16.
Scope: agent side in `workspace/gato_src/`, hub + phone UI in a new
`diablia/mobile-macro/`. Nothing in `brain.py`, `combat.py`, `net.py`,
`capture.py`, `input.py` changes. `prism.py` gets 3 added lines.

---

## 0. Report

### What it is
A small Cloudflare Worker with one Durable Object ("hub") that every macro PC
dials out to over a WebSocket. The phone opens the same Worker URL and sees a
list of PCs. For each PC it shows online state, bot state, a live thumbnail of
the game window and one full-rate viewer, and it has the existing controls:
start/stop, schedule, monster config, chat, the F/M/I/B/Enter keys, and
left/right click. Each PC keeps running the existing `_run_remote` command
vocabulary unchanged. The new agent module only adds a second transport into it.
Input reaches a chosen game window by briefly borrowing focus. It waits until
the user is idle, then restores the user's window and cursor. If the background
`PostMessage` path is shown to work on this game, it uses that instead.

### Why it's new (and where it isn't)
Searches run on 2026-11-16 (web): `게임 매크로 모바일 원격 제어 여러 PC 동시 화면 보기 프로그램`,
`MeshCentral agent outbound websocket relay NAT remote desktop multiple devices web`,
`Cloudflare Durable Objects free plan limits … hibernation`, `ngrok free plan data transfer limit`.

Closest prior art. The topology and the thumbnail wall are **not new**:
- **리모아PC원격제어** (https://remoapc.com/) gives 5 PCs free, a thumbnail
  wall, an Android app, an outbound-only relay, and WGC capture of fullscreen
  games. It already covers "see 3 PCs from the phone and click on them".
- **MeshCentral** (https://docs.meshcentral.com/design/): agents dial out to the
  server over WebSocket and the server relays them to the browser. Its desktop
  multiplexor already aggregates quality and frame rate across viewers.
- **RemoteMV** (MHsoft) and **RemoteT** do multi-PC game remote control. RemoteMV
  bypasses game security with a driver; we refuse to do that.

Honest call: if Jungjun only needed pixels and clicks, installing RemoaPC and
clicking the pywebview window remotely would cover about 80% of this with no
code. The delta this design delivers, which none of the above does:
1. **Bot-native control.** You can start, stop, or schedule all three bots and
   read their state (`running`, `battle_count`, schedule cycle, last note)
   without streaming video at all. Video is optional, not the control path.
2. **Game-window-only capture.** The hub only ever sees the 거상 client area.
   The rest of the user's desktop never leaves the PC. Remote-desktop tools
   send the whole desktop.
3. **The user's desktop isn't taken over.** Input is gated on user idle, the
   foreground window and cursor are restored afterwards, and calibration checks
   whether `PostMessage` is enough (section 5). Remote desktop tools move the
   real cursor while a session is open.
4. **$0, no installer, no third-party agent** running as admin on the PCs.

The two engineering pieces with some originality are not patent-grade
novelty, and I'm not claiming that:
- **Vision-verified input-path calibration.** The bot's own capture diffs the
  frame before and after a probe keystroke (`M` opens and closes the map) to
  decide per PC whether background `PostMessage` input works or focus must be
  borrowed. Screenshot-verified automation in general is old (Sikuli). What's
  new here is using it to choose the transport per PC at runtime.
- **End-to-end credit flow through a hibernating Durable Object.** A
  self-healing credit watchdog lets a lost credit cost 3 s, not a stall.

### Why it works (what was run)
- **Free-tier fit (fact, from docs, fetched 2026-11-16).** Durable Objects on
  Workers Free: 100,000 requests/day and 13,000 GB-s/day. Incoming WebSocket
  messages bill 20:1. Outgoing messages and protocol pings are free.
  `setWebSocketAutoResponse` replies cost no duration
  (https://developers.cloudflare.com/durable-objects/platform/pricing/).
  Messages routed through a Worker don't count as Worker requests
  (https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/platform/pricing/).
  Calculation (§4.6): 24 h/day of 1 full + 2 thumbs comes to about 43.4k billed
  requests/day (43% of the cap). Duration worst case, one DO awake 24 h:
  0.125 GB × 86,400 s = 10,800 GB-s (83% of the cap).
- **Why not a tunnel (fact).** ngrok Free has 1 GB/month data out
  (https://ngrok.com/docs/pricing-limits/free-plan-limits). One hour of full
  view uses about 190–600 MB (below), so a tunnel dies in 2–5 hours of
  viewing. Cloudflare doesn't bill Worker egress.
- **Frame sizes (measured).** I used a real 1024×768 거상 base screen
  (gogolap.com guide image `01_기본_화면.webp`, 1024×768 source), resized with
  PIL BOX and encoded as baseline JPEG. PIL and OpenCV use the same IJG quality
  scale, so sizes should be within about 10%, but that's a hypothesis checked in
  M2.

  | size | q | KB/frame |
  |---|---|---|
  | 1024×768 | 70 | 200.1 |
  | 640×480 | 50 | 59.5 |
  | 640×480 | 40 | 51.5 |
  | 480×360 | 35 | 26.7 |
  | 320×240 | 35 | 12.0 |
  | 320×240 | 30 | 10.8 |
- **Code facts verified by reading the source:**
  - `capture.py:19` sets `REF_W, REF_H = (1024, 768)`. The reference frame is
    **1024×768, not 1920×1080** as the brief said, and all click args are in
    that space (`prism.py:233`).
  - `prism.py:286` calls `self.auth.upload_live(jpg)`, but `net.AuthClient`
    has no `upload_live` (only `live_uploader`, `net.py:269`). The
    `except Exception: pass` swallows the `AttributeError`, so **the vendor
    live view uploads nothing today**. That's out of scope: this spec does not
    touch the vendor path. A one-line fix (`self.auth.upload_shot(jpg)`) is
    noted in §10 for Jungjun to decide.
  - `config.save()` settings are pushed to the vendor server
    (`_push_settings`, `prism.py:685`). So **pairing secrets must never go
    into `prism_settings.json`**. They live in a separate `remote.json`.
  - `config._validate` merges `base` before `raw` (`config.py:1784-1787`).
    Dropping secret keys from a phone-sent `common` dict therefore keeps the
    stored values.
  - `_run_remote` already refuses input commands while `brain.running`
    (`prism.py:118`). Remote input is therefore only ever live while the bot is
    stopped, and the focus borrow never competes with the bot's own `Hands`.

### What's unproven (hypotheses + the experiment that settles each)
| # | Hypothesis | Experiment | Cost |
|---|---|---|---|
| H1 | 거상 ignores `PostMessage` keys (scancode `SendInput` in `input.py` suggests DirectInput/raw input) but may accept `PostMessage` mouse | `py -3.11 remote_input.py probe` on one PC (§5.4) | 5 min |
| H2 | `GetLastInputInfo` is also updated by our own injected input, so the "own injection" discount (§5.2) is needed | `remote_input.py selftest` prints idle before/after one injection | 2 min |
| H3 | OpenCV JPEG sizes on live game frames are within ±15% of the PIL proxy above | `remote_link.py bench-frames --seconds 60` on a PC (M2) | 2 min |
| H4 | The DO stays within free duration while one phone views 24 h | Cloudflare dashboard → DO metrics after a 24 h M2 soak | 1 day passive |
| H5 | A 0.12 s settle after `SendInput` is enough for the game to consume the input before focus is restored | selftest frame-diff on a key that opens a window (`I`) with settle 0.05/0.12/0.25 | 5 min |
| H6 | iOS Safari keeps the `SameSite=Strict` HttpOnly cookie for a home-screen web app across launches | Add to Home Screen, kill the app, reopen after 1 day | 1 day passive |
| H7 | `websockets.sync` `send()` is safe across threads | Not relied on: the spec funnels all sends through one sender thread | 0 |

---

## 1. Topology and rendezvous: Cloudflare Worker + one Durable Object

```
 phone (browser)  ──wss /ws/phone──┐
                                   ▼
                     Worker "gato-hub"  ──► DO "Fleet" (idFromName("fleet"))
                                   ▲          SQLite: pcs, devices, pending, meta, audit
 PC1 RemoteLink ──wss /ws/agent────┤
 PC2 RemoteLink ──wss /ws/agent────┤          (all outbound from PCs; no port forward)
 PC3 RemoteLink ──wss /ws/agent────┘
 PC* also keep: vendor /cmd long-poll (unchanged), Discord BotPoller (unchanged)
```

**Choice: (b) our own relay on a Cloudflare Worker + a single SQLite-backed
Durable Object using the WebSocket Hibernation API.**

Why the other options were killed:
- **(a) Vendor `/cmd` + `/shot`, killed.** We can't change the server or see its
  phone protocol. `/shot` keeps only the latest frame per auth_id over HTTP
  POST base64 (about 33% overhead and one request per frame). There's no
  multi-PC view, and our screens and commands would pass through a third party.
  Keep it untouched as a fallback channel.
- **(c) Tailscale mesh, killed.** It needs a system install on 3 PCs plus the
  phone, and holds the phone's single VPN slot on iOS. The page would have to
  talk to 3 `http://100.x` origins: mixed content from an https page, plus CORS
  on 3 listeners. Each PC would have to open a listening port. It does solve NAT
  and bandwidth, but it fails "no installer" and adds an inbound surface on
  every PC.
- **Fly/Railway/Render, killed.** Railway and Fly have no free tier for new
  accounts, and Render free sleeps after 15 min idle and kills WebSockets.
- **ngrok to the Mac cockpit, killed.** 1 GB/month of egress (above), and the
  Mac must be on.

Why one DO: all three PCs and the phones share one object, so routing is a map
lookup and not a cross-object RPC. That keeps the request count and duration to
one object (the 83% worst case above). Load is 3 agents plus 1–2 phones, far
below the DO's ~1,000 req/s soft limit.

**PC offline.** The agent socket closes (`webSocketClose`/`webSocketError`), or
its last auto-response timestamp is more than 50 s old. The hub then marks it
`online:false` and pushes `{"t":"pc", ...}` to every phone. Commands to an
offline PC are **rejected immediately** with `err code:"offline"`. They're
never queued, because a click aimed at a stale screen is dangerous. The phone
learns about it within 50 s worst case (half-open TCP), or instantly on a clean
close.

---

## 2. Agent side: `remote_link.py` (new) + 3 lines in `prism.py`

### 2.1 Files
```
workspace/gato_src/
  remote_link.py    # RemoteLink, FrameStreamer, pairing CLI, selftest, bench-frames
  remote_input.py   # RemoteHands(Hands), idle gate, focus borrow/restore, PostMessage path, probe
  remote.json       # generated by pairing; never in prism_settings.json; never copied between PCs
  requirements.txt  # + websockets==15.0.1   (verify the wheel for py3.11 win_amd64; pure-python fallback is fine)
```
`remote_link.py` must import **nothing from `prism`**. It receives the `Api`
instance. It imports `capture`, `input`, `cv2`, `win32*` lazily inside
functions, so `python3 remote_link.py selftest` runs on macOS with a `FakeApi`.

### 2.2 The 3-line `prism.py` edit
At the end of `Api.__init__` (after `self.start_cmd_poller()`, `prism.py:78`):
```python
        import remote_link
        self.remote = remote_link.install(self)   # None when remote.json is absent or invalid
```
In `main()._on_closed` (`prism.py:986`):
```python
        if getattr(api, 'remote', None): api.remote.close()
```
That's all. When `remote.json` is missing, `install()` returns `None`, starts
no thread and patches nothing, so the vendor channel behaves exactly as today.

### 2.3 `install(api) -> RemoteLink | None`
```python
REMOTE_PATH = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), 'remote.json')  # not cwd: ensure_admin relaunches

def install(api) -> 'RemoteLink | None':
    try:
        cfg = load_remote_cfg()            # None if missing; raises RemoteCfgError on bad/copied file
        if cfg is None: return None
        import websockets.sync.client      # noqa: F401  (fail fast, clear note)
    except RemoteCfgError as e:
        api.vision.note('input', f'[원격링크] {e}'); return None
    except ImportError:
        api.vision.note('input', '[원격링크] websockets 미설치 — pip install websockets==15.0.1'); return None
    orig = api._run_remote
    lock = threading.RLock()
    def _locked(cmd, arg=''):
        with lock:
            return orig(cmd, arg)
    api._run_remote = _locked              # instance attr shadows the method; the vendor poller now shares the lock
    link = RemoteLink(api, cfg, orig, lock)
    link.start()
    return link
```
Every exception inside `install` is caught, so a broken relay never stops the
bot from starting.

### 2.4 `remote.json`
```json
{
  "v": 1,
  "hub": "https://gato-hub.<account>.workers.dev",
  "pc": "pc_k3x9q2m7ab",
  "name": "PC1-거실",
  "secret": "<base64url, 32 random bytes>",
  "machine": "<HKLM\\SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Cryptography\\MachineGuid>",
  "paired_at": "2026-11-16T12:00:00Z",
  "input_mode": {"key": "borrow", "mouse": "borrow"}
}
```
`load_remote_cfg()` checks that `machine` equals the current MachineGuid. If it
doesn't, it renames the file to `remote.json.copied` and raises
`RemoteCfgError('다른 PC에서 복사된 remote.json — 이 PC에서 다시 페어링하세요')`.
This matters because `RUN.md` tells people to copy the whole folder between
PCs, which would otherwise make PC2 impersonate PC1.

### 2.5 `class RemoteLink`
```python
class RemoteLink:
    PING_S = 20            # app-level {"t":"ping"} → DO auto-response (no wake, no duration)
    STATE_FAST_S = 2.0     # while any viewer lease is active
    STATE_SLOW_S = 30.0    # otherwise (and on change)
    CMD_TIMEOUT_S = 15.0
    BACKOFF = (1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 60)   # ±30% jitter, reset after 'welcome'

    def __init__(self, api, cfg: dict, run_remote_orig, lock: threading.RLock): ...
    def start(self) -> None          # starts threads: link, sender, exec, state
    def close(self) -> None          # sets _stop, closes ws, stops streamer
    # internals
    def _link_loop(self) -> None     # connect → challenge/auth → recv loop → reconnect
    def _sender_loop(self) -> None   # sole writer: JSON queue first, then the latest frame slot
    def _exec_loop(self) -> None     # FIFO command worker with watchdog
    def _state_loop(self) -> None
    def _on_msg(self, m: dict) -> None
    def _on_cmd(self, m: dict) -> None
    def _on_rpc(self, m: dict) -> None
    def _send(self, obj: dict) -> None          # enqueue JSON
    def _hub_now(self) -> int                   # local ms + offset learned from 'challenge'/'welcome'/'view'.now
```

Connection: `websockets.sync.client.connect(hub_ws + '/ws/agent?pc=' + pc,
open_timeout=10, ping_interval=20, ping_timeout=20, max_size=2**20,
user_agent_header='gato-remote/1')`. The protocol pings let the agent detect a
dead hub within about 40 s. The hub's liveness check uses the app-level ping
timestamps.

Command dispatch (`_on_cmd`, run on the exec thread):
```python
INPUT_CMDS = {'click_l','click_r','key_f','key_m','key_i','key_b','key_enter'}
PRIORITY   = {'stop','sched_stop','live_stop'}          # bypass the FIFO; run on a short-lived thread
LEASED     = INPUT_CMDS | {'start','sched_start','chat_normal','chat_sangdan','whisper',
                           'gato_login','gato_launch','game_close','key'}   # hub enforces the control lease

def _dispatch(self, cmd, arg, win):
    before = self.api._last_note
    if cmd == 'key':                                    # new, stage 3: wider key pad
        return self._run_key(arg, win)
    if cmd in INPUT_CMDS:
        with self._lock:                                # same RLock the vendor poller uses
            saved = self.api.hands
            self.api.hands = self.rhands
            self.rhands.set_target(win)                 # pid or None (= engine.hwnd / find_window)
            try:     self._orig(cmd, arg)               # verbatim _run_remote: same checks, same notes
            finally: self.api.hands = saved
        res = self.rhands.barrier(timeout=5.0)          # 'ok' | 'user_busy' | 'no_window' | 'fg_fail' | 'blocked'
    else:
        self._orig(cmd, arg)                            # start, stop, shot, chat_*, gato_*, sched_*, settings_reload, game_close
        res = 'ok'
    note = self.api._last_note if self.api._last_note != before else ''
    return res, note
```
- The `'shot'` command keeps its vendor meaning (upload to the vendor server).
  The relay has its own `still` view (§4) and never calls `shot`.
- `live_start`/`live_stop` are passed through verbatim for compatibility but
  are no-ops for the relay.
- `key` (stage 3) takes `arg` from the whitelist `esc enter space tab up down
  left right f1..f12 a..z 0..9`. It is refused while `brain.running`, with the
  same note as `_run_remote`.

Replay and expiry: drop any `cmd`/`rpc` whose `exp < self._hub_now()`, and
send `ack ok:false note:'expired'`. Keep an `OrderedDict` LRU of the last 512
`id`s. A duplicate is dropped silently, which is safe after a reconnect resend.

Watchdog: `_exec_loop` runs each command on a worker thread and joins with
`CMD_TIMEOUT_S`. On timeout it sends `ack ok:false note:'timeout'`, abandons
that (daemon) worker, and spawns a fresh one. Every state message carries
`busy:{cmd, for_s}`. If two commands are hung at once, input/leased commands
are refused with `note:'PC 응답 없음 — 매크로 재시작 필요'`; `stop` still runs
through the priority path.

RPC whitelist (`_on_rpc`). Anything else gets `ok:false err:'denied'`:
```python
RPC = {
  'tick':             lambda a: api.tick(int(a[0]), int(a[1])),
  'status':           lambda a: api.status(),
  'get_config':       lambda a: redact(api.get_config()),
  'set_monster':      lambda a: redact(api.set_monster(str(a[0]))),
  'save_monster_cfg': lambda a: redact(api.save_monster_cfg(str(a[0]), dict(a[1]), a[2] if len(a) > 2 else None)),
  'save_common':      lambda a: redact(api.save_common(strip_secret_keys(dict(a[0])), a[1] if len(a) > 1 else None)),
  'kill_rank':        lambda a: api.kill_rank(),
  'appear_records':   lambda a: api.appear_records(),
  'reset_count':      lambda a: api.reset_count(),
  'portrait':         lambda a: api.portrait(str(a[0])),
  'input_probe':      lambda a: self.rhands.probe(api),          # stage 3, §5.4
  'set_input_mode':   lambda a: self._set_input_mode(dict(a[0])),  # writes remote.json only
}
SECRET_KEYS = {'discord_token','discord_webhook','ntfy_url','auth_id'}
SECRET_RE   = re.compile(r'(token|secret|webhook|pass)', re.I)   # catch-all for future keys
# redact(): replace each matching value v with {'set': bool(v)}; strip_secret_keys(): drop them from incoming dicts
```
Deliberately excluded from remote access: `login`, `logout`, `self_update`,
`toggle_dump`, `test_webhook`, `test_ntfy`, every `win_*`. `start`, `stop` and
schedules go through `cmd` only, so there is one path for each action.

State message (`_state_loop`), built from `api.tick(ev_seq, chat_seq)`
(`prism.py:519`) so nothing is re-implemented:
```json
{"t":"state","seq":812,"ts":1763295000123,
 "s":{"running":false,"battle_count":143,"monster":"주작",
      "schedule":{"active":true,"cycle":2,"cycles":3,"battles":4,"target":10,"resting":false,"rest_left":0},
      "auth":{"ok":true,"label":"인증 · D-12"},
      "window_count":2,"clients":[{"role":"메인","state":"메인화면"},{"role":"서브1","state":"전투"}],
      "last_note":"▶ 패널 시작","gato":false,"admin":true,"locked":false,
      "busy":null,"ver":"<net.CODE_VER>","input_mode":{"key":"borrow","mouse":"borrow"}},
 "ev":[ /* vision.events_since(ev_seq), last 50 max */ ],
 "chat":[ /* api.chat(chat_seq), last 50 max */ ]}
```
`locked` is true when `OpenInputDesktop` fails (session locked or on the secure
desktop). In that case input and capture can't work, and the phone shows
`PC 잠김`. A state message is sent when the content hash changes or the slow
interval passes.

Windows list (sent on change, checked every 2 s via `api._find_windows_cached()`):
```json
{"t":"wins","list":[{"pid":4120,"hwnd":328744,"role":"메인"},{"pid":6612,"hwnd":590112,"role":"서브1"}]}
```

### 2.6 Pairing CLI (same file)
```
py -3.11 remote_link.py pair --hub https://gato-hub.<account>.workers.dev --name "PC1-거실"
py -3.11 remote_link.py set-hub <url>        # same pc/secret, new URL (only valid if the same Worker/DO namespace)
py -3.11 remote_link.py unpair               # deletes remote.json after confirmation
py -3.11 remote_link.py selftest --hub URL   # FakeApi, runs on macOS too
py -3.11 remote_link.py bench-frames --seconds 60   # Windows: real KB/frame, fps, encode ms per mode
```
Uses stdlib `urllib` only; `requests` is not required.

---

## 3. Multi-PC identity and pairing

Identity:
- `pc`: `"pc_" + 10 chars base32` (lowercase, no padding), generated on the
  agent by `secrets.token_bytes`.
- `name`: free text, max 24 chars, editable from the phone.
- `secret`: 32 bytes from `secrets.token_bytes`, generated on the agent and sent
  **once** over TLS at `pair/start`. The hub stores it because it must compute
  HMACs; it is never sent again.

End-to-end flow. The 6-digit code appears **only on the PC console**, never on
the phone. That makes it the out-of-band proof: someone who calls `pair/start`
can create a pending entry but can't approve it.

```
PC console                               Hub (DO)                               Phone (logged in)
──────────                               ────────                               ─────────────────
pair --hub U --name "PC1-거실"
 gen pc, secret, machine
 POST /api/agent/pair/start  ─────────►  pending[pc] = {name, secret, code=6 digits,
   {"pc","name","secret"}                 exp=now+600s}; max 5 pending; 10/h per IP
 ◄─ {"ok":true,"code":"482913","exp_s":600}
 prints:
 [페어링] 휴대폰에서 '설정 → PC 추가'를 누르고
         이 코드를 입력하세요:  482 913
         (이름: PC1-거실, 10분 유효)
 loop every 3 s:
 POST /api/agent/pair/poll  ──────────►  state of pending/pcs
   {"pc","sig":hex(HMAC(secret,"poll|"+pc+"|"+code))}
                                                                    ⚙ → PC 추가 → shows "대기 중: PC1-거실 (3분 전)"
                                                                    user types 482913 → [승인]
                                         ◄──────────── POST /api/pair/approve {"code":"482913","name":"PC1-거실"}
                                         5 wrong codes / 10 min → 10 min lock on approve
                                         pcs[pc] = {name, secret, created, input_mode}
                                         delete pending[pc]; push {"t":"pc",...} to phones
 ◄─ {"state":"approved","name":"PC1-거실"}
 write remote.json (atomic: .tmp + os.replace)
 [페어링] 승인됨 — 매크로를 다시 시작하면 연결됩니다
```
`pair/poll` states: `pending | approved | expired | rejected`. On `expired` the
CLI prints `코드 만료 — 다시 실행하세요` and exits 2.

The QR option was killed: the PC side has no camera, and printing a QR needs a
new dependency. A 6-digit code typed on the phone is the same proof with zero
dependencies.

---

## 4. Screen streaming

### 4.1 Modes (agent `FrameStreamer`)
| mode | size | JPEG q | max fps | change gate (mean absdiff on downscaled frame) | forced resend ("keyframe") | KB/frame (measured proxy) | worst-case rate |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `thumb` | 320×240 | 30 | 0.5 | ≥ 0.8 | every 10 s | 10.8 | 5.4 KB/s |
| `full` | 640×480 | 45 | 3 | ≥ 0.4 (`LIVE_MIN_DIFF`) | every 5 s (`LIVE_KEYFRAME_SEC`) | ~55 (between q40 = 51.5 and q50 = 59.5) | 165 KB/s ≈ 1.3 Mbps ≈ 590 MB/h |
| `full` + `q:"save"` | 480×360 | 35 | 2 | ≥ 0.6 | every 5 s | 26.7 | 53 KB/s ≈ 0.43 Mbps ≈ 190 MB/h |
| `still` (one-shot) | 1024×768 | 70 | 1 frame | n/a | n/a | 200.1 | one-off |

A "keyframe" here means a forced resend of an unchanged frame. MJPEG has no
inter frames, so this is purely for recovering from a lost frame or a newly
joined viewer. The agent also sends one frame **immediately** on every mode
change. The rates in the table assume every frame changes. 거상 has animated
water and sprites, so assume it mostly does until `bench-frames` (H3) says
otherwise.

Default on the phone: `q:"save"` when `navigator.connection?.saveData` is set
or `navigator.connection?.type === 'cellular'`, otherwise `auto`. A `데이터 절약`
toggle overrides it. iOS has no `navigator.connection`, so the default there is
`save`, and the user can turn it off.

### 4.2 Capture path (reuses `_live_loop`'s path, not its uploader)
```python
def _grab(self, pid):
    api = self.api
    if pid is None or pid == self._main_pid():
        if api.engine.running:                    # bot already capturing at 10 fps: no second WGC grab
            img = api.engine.snapshot()           # VERIFY returns the 1024×768-normalised BGR; else fall through
            if img is not None: return img
        return api.brain._grab_main()             # identical to _live_loop / 'shot'
    hwnd = self._hwnd_for_pid(pid)                # from find_windows(); None if gone
    return capture.grab_hwnd(hwnd, normalize=True) if hwnd else None
```
After that it does the same as `_live_loop`: `cv2.resize(INTER_AREA)`, then the
`absdiff().mean()` gate, then `cv2.imencode('.jpg', IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY)`, then
a size-1 latest slot, which `_sender_loop` drains. Five consecutive capture
failures stop the streamer and set `note` to `_capture_fail_reason()`, the same
text the vendor path uses.

### 4.3 Binary frame format
Agent → hub, little-endian, 20-byte header followed by the JPEG bytes:
```python
HDR = struct.Struct('<2sBBIIQ')   # magic b'GF', ver=1, kind(0 thumb,1 full,2 still), seq u32, win_pid u32 (0 = main), cap_ms u64 (agent clock; informational only)
```
Hub → phone: `[u8 pc_len][pc ascii][u64 hub_rx_ms LE]` followed by the agent
payload unchanged. The phone computes age as
`phone_now + phone_offset − hub_rx_ms`. It never uses agent clocks.

### 4.4 Leases (view) and renewals
- Phone → hub every 8 s, on any change, and when the page becomes visible:
  `{"t":"view","leases":{"pc_a":"full","pc_b":"thumb","pc_c":"thumb"},"q":"auto","win":{"pc_a":4120}}`.
- On `visibilitychange → hidden` the phone sends `{"t":"view","leases":{}}`
  immediately, so a backgrounded tab costs no data.
- The hub keeps `views[dev] = {leases, q, win, at}` in memory. Per PC it takes
  `mode = max(off < thumb < full)` over devices with `now − at < 20 s`. `q` is
  `"save"` if any full viewer asked for save, `win` comes from the
  most-recently-updated full viewer.
- Hub → agent: `{"t":"view","mode":"full","q":"auto","win":4120,"lease_s":20,"now":<hub ms>}`.
  It's sent whenever the aggregate changes and as a renewal whenever any phone
  renews, at most once per 4 s per PC.
- The agent drops to `off` when `lease_s` passes with no renewal, and stops the
  streamer thread, so CPU use is zero.
- `still`: phone → hub `{"t":"still","pc":"pc_a"}` → agent
  `{"t":"still","win":pid}`, which sends one kind-2 frame. It doesn't use credit
  and is rate-limited to 1 per 3 s per PC by the agent.

### 4.5 Backpressure: end-to-end credits with a self-healing watchdog
- **Agent.** `credits = 2` for full, `1` for thumb. They reset on every `view`
  that changes the mode. Each frame sent uses one credit. At zero the agent
  **doesn't encode** (no CPU). When the hub sends
  `{"t":"credit","seq":n}`, the agent adds one credit, up to the cap. If no
  credit has arrived for 3 s, the agent refills to the cap. That covers a credit
  lost in hub hibernation or a phone that dropped.
- **Hub, per phone and per PC.** `credit[dev][pc]` starts at 2. On a frame from
  a PC, for each phone leasing that PC: if it has credit, send the frame and
  decrement. Otherwise store the frame in `pending[dev][pc]`, overwriting what's
  there (latest-only, so older frames are dropped). On a phone message
  `{"t":"credit","pc":"pc_a","seq":n}`, increment the phone's credit, flush the
  pending frame, and forward `credit` to the agent if `n > lastCredited[pc]`, so
  the first viewer's ack drives the agent.
- **Phone.** It sends `credit` in the `<img>`'s `onload`, after decode. Back
  pressure is therefore measured at the screen, not at the socket.
- **Adaptive quality** (agent, `q:"auto"` only). RTT is the time from frame
  send to the credit for that `seq`, measured on the agent clock only, so clock
  skew doesn't matter. If the median of the last 5 RTTs is over 1,200 ms, step
  down to the `save` parameters. Step back up after 15 s of median RTT under
  400 ms.
- The hub caches the last frame per PC and kind in memory. A phone that starts
  leasing gets it immediately. That cache is lost on hibernation, but the agent
  sends a fresh frame on every mode change, so nothing breaks.

### 4.6 Free-tier budget (calculation from the numbers above)
Viewing 1 full + 2 thumbs, incoming messages/s: agent frames 3 + thumbs 1 +
phone credits 4 + agent state 1.5 + phone view renewals 0.125 + agent pings 0.15
≈ **9.8 msg/s**. That bills as 0.49 req/s, which is 1,764 req per viewing hour.
Idle (no viewer): about 0.25 msg/s, or roughly 1,080 req/day.
**24 h of viewing comes to about 43.4k billed requests/day** against the
100k cap.

Guard: the hub counts incoming messages per UTC day and persists the count every
60 s (1,440 row writes/day). At 80% of 2,000,000 incoming messages (≈ 80k billed
requests) it forces every lease to `thumb`. It pushes
`{"t":"budget","pct":80}` and the phone sh

# Selling GATO — zero-cost plan (2026-11-21)

No 사업자등록, no subscription, no monthly bill. One-time license per PC. Every piece below is free
tier or funded out of the first sales.

## 0. What "free" changes

| Piece | Paid plan | Free plan | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| License server | 카페24 VPS 16,000원/월 | Cloudflare Workers + D1 free tier | **Free works** — fits ~70 users |
| Hosting the update file | VPS storage | GitHub Releases (already live, already free) | **Free, already built** |
| Domain | 15,000원/yr | `*.workers.dev` subdomain | **Free** |
| Source protection | PyArmor Pro $89 | Nuitka (Apache-2.0) | **Free works** — kills the .pyc |
| Installer signing | Certum Individual $99/yr | unsigned + SmartScreen warning | **Start free, buy from revenue** |
| Payments | PG (needs 사업자) | 크몽 개인 전문가 / 계좌이체 | **Free, no 사업자등록** |
| **Total** | ~1,077,000원 first year | **0원** | |

One real ceiling: Cloudflare free is 100,000 requests/day. At a 20 s heartbeat that is only ~23
users. So the licensed client heartbeats **every 60 s** (~69 users), and the server writes to D1 only
on state change, not on every beat. Past ~70 users the $5/mo Workers plan is the first money spent —
by then that is one sale's worth.

## 1. Source protection, free

PyInstaller ships `.pyc`, and this repo's own `net.py` is a PyLingual decompile — that is what a buyer
gets in five minutes.

**Nuitka** compiles to C, then to a native binary. Python bytecode is gone; the logic is machine
code. That is the whole win, and it is free (Apache 2.0).

- `--nofollow-import-to=` for stdlib and ultralytics, compile the app to a native binary.
- Optional extra later: PyArmor free trial on `jochul.py`/`detector.py` before Nuitka. The free tier is
  limited (file count/size, no RFT/BCC) — if it fights the build, skip it. Nuitka alone is enough.
- `assets/models/*` (132 MB of `.pt`) ship as data. Not source.

What still leaks: function names, string literals in the binary, and Ghidra for someone determined.
That is the ceiling for any Python product and no 거상 buyer goes that far.

Build risk is real: Nuitka on Windows takes CI from ~2 min to ~15-25 min and ultralytics needs
`--include-package` work. Do it on a branch and prove the selftest passes on the built artifact
before the first sale.

## 2. License server, free, on Cloudflare

`net.py` already speaks the whole protocol — `/auth`, `/heartbeat`, `/cmd`, `/shot`, `/stats`,
`/error` — and it is switched off by one line (`LOCAL`). Point `SERVER` at the Worker and the client
side is done.

One Worker + one D1 table:

- `POST /auth` — license key + HWID → HMAC token, 1 key = 1 seat, `expired`/`revoked` reasons the
  client already handles
- `POST /heartbeat` — token + HWID recheck; returns `min_version` and `notice`
- admin endpoint behind a secret — issue / revoke / re-issue
- **72 h offline grace** — the client already tolerates `MAX_NET_FAILS`; a network blip must never
  stop a running bot
- reactivation limit 2 per 6 months, by Discord request

Free tier is enough: 5 M D1 reads/day, 100 k writes/day, 5 GB storage, 10 ms CPU per invocation
(enough — one indexed query and an HMAC). Signup needs no card. Keep it to ~150 lines so the CPU
budget is never close.

## 3. Updates, free, already built

`update.py` pulls from public `J0SEPH-K/gato-setup`: SHA256 + size verified, waits for idle, silent
install, relaunch. Source stays in the private repo; only the built binary is public, and an
installer without a key does nothing. **No VPS needed.**

Add:

- **`min_version` floor** — heartbeat returns it; a client below it refuses to start with
  `필수 업데이트` and one button. This is the most important line in the build: when 거상 patches,
  every copy breaks the same hour and this is the only way to force everyone onto the fix.
- **Vivaldi-style notice** — panel banner `새 버전 vX 나왔습니다 — [지금 업데이트] [나중에]` plus a
  one-line changelog. Never interrupts a running bot (already true).

## 4. Cost: 0원

Nothing to buy to launch. First optional spend, paid out of revenue:

| When | Item | Cost |
|---|---|---|
| after ~10 sales | Certum Individual code signing, cloud, no USB token | $99/yr (~13만원) |
| past ~70 users | Cloudflare Workers paid | $5/mo |

Unsigned until then means SmartScreen `알 수 없는 게시자` → `추가 정보` → `실행`. Put that on the
landing page with a screenshot so it is not a surprise, and sign as soon as the cert is affordable —
unsigned costs conversion, not legality.

## 5. Getting paid with no 사업자등록

- **크몽 개인 전문가** — allowed with no business registration and no approval step, just 본인인증 +
  a 계좌 in your own name. Fee is 16.4% under 700,000원, so list at **190,000원** there and net
  ~158,000원. It is also the trust layer for a stranger who won't wire money to an individual.
- **계좌이체 direct** — 0% fee, for people arriving from 유튜브/Discord who already trust the demo.
  Cheaper for both sides; use 크몽 only for cold buyers.
- 크몽 does not withhold 3.3% — report the income in the May 종합소득세 filing yourself and keep the
  fee invoices as expenses. Repeated sales technically make you 사업자등록 대상; register once volume
  is steady, not before.

## 6. Price

From real listings: 지존오토 resale 8-10만원, 3클라 오토 초기 25만원, 작업장 세팅 30만원 + 자리당 월
10만원, 린클매니저 월 45,000원/PC, OpenMacro $19.99 lifetime, GrindHero $249 lifetime.

| Tier | Direct | 크몽 | Contents |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 PC | **150,000원** | 190,000원 | 1 key, 1 PC, 업데이트 12개월 |
| 2 PC | **250,000원** | 300,000원 | |
| 3 PC | **350,000원** | 420,000원 | + 우선 지원 |
| 갱신 (12개월 후) | 50,000원/yr | — | optional, bot runs without it |

No subscription, so the 12-month update window is what keeps the thing affordable against 거상's
patch cadence. State it plainly at purchase.

Refund: 7 days, plus a written clause — `거상 패치로 인한 중단은 환불 사유 아님, 업데이트로 대응`.

## 7. Demand, zero budget

1. **유튜브 60-90 s demo** — a real 사냥터 clear, and one clip of 조철 being solved. This category
   sells on video (광말구, 땡겨, 강철비 are all macro-review channels); one review beats any ad.
2. **거리니닷컴 / 다음 카페 거상투** — where 거상 users actually read. 후기 thread with the video and
   the price list.
3. **디스코드** — support + update pushes, one channel per release.
4. **크몽** — cold-buyer trust layer, 190,000원.
5. **중고나라 / 당근** — the 지존오토 resale crowd, weekly.

First 3 sales at a discount in exchange for a public 후기. Don't sell Discord-only; a stranger won't
join a server to see a price.

## 8. Order of work

1. Nuitka branch, selftest green on the built artifact, installer builds from it
2. Worker + D1 license server, `LOCAL=0`, HWID, 72 h grace, 60 s heartbeat
3. `min_version` floor on the existing SHA256 update path
4. Update banner UI
5. Landing page (video, price, refund clause, 거상 ToS + ban disclaimer, SmartScreen screenshot)
6. First sales → cert → sign

## 9. Honest risks

- **거상 patches kill every copy at once.** The `min_version` floor plus a fast release is the
  answer; expect one bad patch to cost a wave of refunds.
- **Bans.** 거상 escalates 20일 → 영구정지. `탐지 시 계정 정지는 감수` must be on the page.
- **Cloudflare free ceiling** is ~70 users at 60 s beats. Fine for launch, $5/mo after.
- **Nuitka can break ultralytics at runtime.** Prove it on a built artifact, not in dev.
- **Unsigned installer** loses some cold buyers until the cert exists.
- **Support load is real** — every buyer is a non-technical Windows user.

===== FILE: ./autosupply.py (    7888 chars) =====
"""Auto-restock: teach-and-replay travel + food/weight management.

Why this file exists (manager, 2026-11-18): the live client has no pathfinding
and no map data, and 거상 has hundreds of hand-walked maps -- there is no
general "click anywhere, go anywhere" solution without either decompiling the
whole map graph or watching a human do it. The pattern this game's own
community actually uses for cross-map travel is NOT free-form walking: it is
(a) a paid instant teleport to one of two fixed hubs via a right-click field
menu (육의전 cost = level*2000, 무투장 cost = level*1000), (b) a location
scroll item bought from an NPC for some destinations, or (c) walking a route
the player already knows. This module automates exactly that shape:

  - WaypointRecorder: press a hotkey to start/stop; every real left-click
    the user makes on the game client while recording is appended as
    (x, y, wait_before_s) in REFERENCE (1024x768) coordinates. This is how
    you "teach" the bot a route once (field menu clicks, portal clicks,
    shop clicks) instead of guessing pixel positions.
  - replay_waypoints(): clicks the same sequence back with the same
    relative timing.
  - run_restock(): the orchestrator called when weight is full or food is
    out. SAFE BY DEFAULT: if `common.auto_restock_enabled` is False, or the
    current monster has no recorded `travel_waypoints`, it does nothing but
    log a clear reason -- it never guesses a screen position on a live
    account. Calibrating it is a one-time teach session per monster.

Nothing here runs unless config.auto_restock_enabled() is True AND the
current monster profile has non-empty travel_waypoints. Both are off by
default.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import ctypes
import ctypes.wintypes
import json
import os
import threading
import time

VK_LBUTTON = 0x01
WAYPOINTS_PATH = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "waypoints.json")


def _load_all() -> dict:
    if not os.path.exists(WAYPOINTS_PATH):
        return {}
    try:
        with open(WAYPOINTS_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f)
    except Exception:
        return {}


def _save_all(data: dict) -> None:
    tmp = WAYPOINTS_PATH + ".tmp"
    with open(tmp, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    os.replace(tmp, WAYPOINTS_PATH)


def load_route(name: str) -> list:
    """Return the recorded [{'x','y','wait'}] list for a named route, or []."""
    return _load_all().get(name, [])


def save_route(name: str, clicks: list) -> None:
    data = _load_all()
    data[name] = clicks
    _save_all(data)


def delete_route(name: str) -> None:
    data = _load_all()
    data.pop(name, None)
    _save_all(data)


def list_routes() -> list:
    return sorted(_load_all().keys())


class WaypointRecorder:
    """Polls the real mouse button (GetAsyncKeyState) the same way the F10
    start/stop hotkey already works in prism.py -- consistent with this
    codebase's existing pattern, and it means recording works no matter
    which window has input focus, exactly like F10 does."""

    POLL_S = 0.02

    def __init__(self, to_client_xy):
        # to_client_xy(screen_x, screen_y) -> (ref_x, ref_y) | None, supplied
        # by the caller so this module never has to import capture/win32gui
        # itself (keeps it testable off-Windows too).
        self._to_client_xy = to_client_xy
        self._thread = None
        self._stop = threading.Event()
        self._clicks = []
        self._last_t = None
        self.recording = False

    def start(self) -> None:
        if self.recording:
            return
        self._clicks = []
        self._last_t = time.monotonic()
        self._stop.clear()
        self.recording = True
        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._loop, daemon=True)
        self._thread.start()

    def stop(self) -> list:
        self._stop.set()
        self.recording = False
        if self._thread:
            self._thread.join(timeout=1.0)
        return list(self._clicks)

    def _loop(self) -> None:
        user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
        prev_down = False
        while not self._stop.is_set():
            down = bool(user32.GetAsyncKeyState(VK_LBUTTON) & 0x8000)
            if down and not prev_down:
                pt = ctypes.wintypes.POINT()
                user32.GetCursorPos(ctypes.byref(pt))
                ref = self._to_client_xy(pt.x, pt.y)
                if ref is not None:
                    now = time.monotonic()
                    wait = round(now - (self._last_t or now), 2)
                    self._last_t = now
                    self._clicks.append({"x": int(ref[0]), "y": int(ref[1]), "wait": wait})
            prev_down = down
            time.sleep(self.POLL_S)


def replay_waypoints(hands, clicks: list, log=print) -> bool:
    """Replay a recorded route. `hands` is the bot's own Hands instance (or
    RemoteHands), so this goes through the same click() path as everything
    else -- no new input primitive, no new risk surface."""
    if not clicks:
        log("[자동보충] 경로가 비어 있음 — 재생할 것이 없습니다")
        return False
    for i, step in enumerate(clicks):
        wait = step.get("wait", 0.0)
        if wait > 0:
            time.sleep(min(wait, 5.0))  # clamp: a bad recording should not stall minutes
        hands.click(int(step["x"]), int(step["y"]))
        log(f"경로 {i + 1}/{len(clicks)} 클릭 ({step['x']},{step['y']})")
    return True


def run_restock(brain, reason: str) -> None:
    """Called from brain.py when weight is full or food is out. `brain` is
    the live Brain instance itself (has .hands, .monster, ._log) -- does
    nothing destructive by default; every branch that would click something
    unverified logs why it refused instead of guessing."""
    log = lambda s: brain._log(f"[자동보충] {s}")
    try:
        import config
    except Exception as e:
        log(f"설정을 불러오지 못함 ({e}) — 중단")
        return

    if not config.auto_restock_enabled():
        log(f"{reason} — 자동 보충이 꺼져 있습니다 (설정 → 자동 보충 → 사용). 수동으로 처리하세요.")
        return

    monster = getattr(brain, "monster", None) or ""
    go_route = load_route(f"{monster}:to_town")
    back_route = load_route(f"{monster}:to_hunt")
    if not go_route or not back_route:
        log(
            f"{reason} — '{monster}' 몬스터의 이동 경로가 기록되지 않았습니다. "
            "설정 → 자동 보충 → 경로 기록으로 마을 이동(to_town)과 사냥터 복귀(to_hunt)를 "
            "한 번씩 직접 걸어가며 기록하세요. 기록 전까지는 자동으로 움직이지 않습니다."
        )
        return

    log(f"{reason} — 마을로 이동 시작")
    if not replay_waypoints(brain.hands, go_route, log):
        return
    food_item = config.food_item()
    food_qty = config.food_buy_qty()
    buy_route = load_route(f"{monster}:buy_food")
    if buy_route:
        log(f"{food_item} {food_qty}개 구매 시도")
        replay_waypoints(brain.hands, buy_route, log)
    else:
        log(f"구매 경로(buy_food)가 기록되지 않아 {food_item} 구매를 건너뜁니다 — 직접 구매하세요")
    sell_items = config.cleanup_sell_items()
    sell_route = load_route(f"{monster}:sell_items")
    if sell_items and sell_route:
        log(f"무게 정리: {sell_items} 판매 시도")
        replay_waypoints(brain.hands, sell_route, log)
    elif sell_items:
        log("판매 경로(sell_items)가 기록되지 않아 무게 정리를 건너뜁니다 — 직접 정리하세요")
    log("사냥터로 복귀 시작")
    replay_waypoints(brain.hands, back_route, log)
    log("자동 보충 완료 — 사냥 재시작하세요")

===== FILE: ./README.md (    1952 chars) =====
# GATO

Auto-hunt macro for 거상 (Gersang). Windows 10/11 x64, Python 3.11, game in windowed mode
at 1024x768, 1280x960 or 1440x1080.

## Install on a new PC

1. Get the repo: `git clone https://github.com/J0SEPH-K/gato.git C:\Gato`
   (or Code -> Download ZIP, extract to `C:\Gato`). Keep the path short, no spaces.
2. Open PowerShell as administrator in that folder and run:

       powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File install.ps1

   Installs Python 3.11 if missing, creates `.venv`, installs pinned packages (about 10 min, torch is large).
3. Start the game and get the character onto the field.
4. Double-click `run.bat` (asks for administrator).

First launch copies `settings.default.json` to `prism_settings.json`. That file holds your
settings and is git-ignored; never commit it.

## Update

    git pull

## Layout

    run_gato.py      entry point and preflight checks
    prism.py         app window and API for ui/
    brain.py         main loop (scene detection, targeting, clicks, schedule)
    combat.py        battle logic
    detector.py      mob detection (YOLO models, AGF sprite matcher)
    input.py         mouse/keyboard (SendInput, cursor landing check)
    capture.py wgc.py  window capture (Windows Graphics Capture)
    config.py        settings and monster catalogue
    ui/              frontend
    assets/          models, templates, sprites
    docs/            install guide (KR), remote spec
    dev/             macOS UI preview, PyInstaller spec

## Self-tests (on the Windows PC)

    .venv\Scripts\python dev\selftest_click.py   # click delivery, landing error, latency (own test window)
    .venv\Scripts\python dev\smoke_start.py 45    # starts the real loop headless against the running game, then stops

## Troubleshooting

- Log: `gato_traceback.log` in the install folder.
- "game window not found": start the game first.
- "game window is WxH": switch to one of the three supported windowed sizes.

from __future__ import annotations

_GATO_EXE = 'gersang-traders.exe'
_gato_cache = {'files': None}
def _pid_by_exe(name_lower: str):
    TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS = 2
    k = ctypes.windll.kernel32
    class PE32(ctypes.Structure):
        _fields_ = [('dwSize', ctypes.wintypes.DWORD), ('cntUsage', ctypes.wintypes.DWORD), ('th32ProcessID', ctypes.wintypes.DWORD), ('th32DefaultHeapID', ctypes.c_void_p), ('th32ModuleID', ctypes.wintypes.DWORD), ('cntThreads', ctypes.wintypes.DWORD), ('th32ParentProcessID', ctypes.wintypes.DWORD), ('pcPriClassBase', ctypes.wintypes.LONG), ('dwFlags', ctypes.wintypes.DWORD), ('szExeFile', ctypes.c_char * 260)]
    snap = k.CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0)
    pe = PE32()
    pe.dwSize = ctypes.sizeof(PE32)
    pid = None
    try:
        if k.Process32First(snap, ctypes.byref(pe)):
            while True:
                if pe.szExeFile.decode('cp949', 'replace').lower() == name_lower:
                    pid = pe.th32ProcessID
                    break
                if not k.Process32Next(snap, ctypes.byref(pe)):
                    break
    finally:
        k.CloseHandle(snap)
    return pid
def _gato_files():
    cached = _gato_cache['files']
    if cached and os.path.exists(cached[1]):
        return cached
    else:
        found = None
        try:
            pid = _pid_by_exe(_GATO_EXE)
            if pid:
                exe = _get_process_exe(pid)
                if exe:
                    d = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(exe), 'battle_sessions')
                    a = os.path.join(d, '_autosave.json')
                    if os.path.exists(a):
                        found = (os.path.join(d, '_autosave.journal.jsonl'), a)
        except Exception:
            pass
        if found is None:
            for base in [os.environ.get('ProgramFiles', 'C:\\Program Files'), os.environ.get('ProgramFiles(x86)', 'C:\\Program Files (x86)'), os.environ.get('LOCALAPPDATA', '')]:
                if not base:
                    continue
                else:
                    hits = glob.glob(os.path.join(base, '*', 'battle_sessions', '_autosave.json')) or glob.glob(os.path.join(base, '*', '*', 'battle_sessions', '_autosave.json'))
                    if hits:
                        d = os.path.dirname(hits[0])
                        found = (os.path.join(d, '_autosave.journal.jsonl'), hits[0])
                        break
        _gato_cache['files'] = found
        return found if found else (None, None)
def _read_last_json_line(path, tail_bytes=131072):
    try:
        with open(path, 'rb') as f:
            f.seek(0, 2)
            size = f.tell()
            f.seek(size - tail_bytes if size > tail_bytes else 0)
            chunk = f.read()
    except OSError:
        return None
    for part in reversed(chunk.decode('utf-8', 'replace').split('\n')):
        if part.strip():
            try:
                return json.loads(part)
            except ValueError:
                pass
def _gato_read_client():
    journal, _ = _gato_files()
    if journal is None:
        return
    else:
        obj = _read_last_json_line(journal)
        if not obj:
            return
        else:
            try:
                return obj['clients'][0]
            except (KeyError, IndexError, TypeError):
                return None
                return None
def _gato_read_deaths():
    c = _gato_read_client()
    if c is None:
        return
    else:
        bc = c.get('session', {}).get('battle_count', 0)
        cv = c.get('battle_history_compact_v2')
        deaths = cv.get('deaths') if isinstance(cv, dict) else None
        return (bc, deaths if deaths is not None else [])
def _gato_read_exp():
    # irreducible cflow, using cdg fallback
    # ***<module>._gato_read_exp: Failure: Compilation Error
    c = _gato_read_client()
    if c is None:
        return
    bc = c.get('session', {}).get('battle_count', 0)
    cv = c.get('battle_history_compact_v2')
    if not isinstance(cv, dict):
        return
    try:
        rows = cv.get('character_exp_rows')
        chars = cv.get('characters')
        if not rows or not chars:
            return None
        exp_row = rows[(-1)]
        names = [r[1] if isinstance(r, (list, tuple)) and len(r) > 1 else str(i) for i, r in enumerate(chars)]
        codes = [r[2] if isinstance(r, (list, tuple)) and len(r) > 2 else str(i) for i, r in enumerate(chars)]
        return (bc, exp_row, names, codes)
    except Exception:
        return None
def _r_death(h):
    h.click(557, 440)
def _r_friend(h):
    h.click(632, 426)
def _r_map(h):
    time.sleep(0.2)
    h.press('m')
def _r_bmode(h):
    time.sleep(0.2)
    h.press('a')
def _r_tban(h):
    time.sleep(0.2)
    h.press('x')
def _r_restart(h):
    h.click(466, 420)
REACTIONS = {'사망': (_r_death, 5.0, '부활 확인 클릭'), '친구 요청': (_r_friend, 3.0, '거절 클릭'), '지도 열림': (_r_map, 2.0, '지도 닫기 (M)'), 'B모드': (_r_bmode, 2.0, '전투모드 해제 (A)'), 'T밴(거래차단)': (_r_tban, 2.0, '거래차단 해제 (X)'), 'restart 팝업': (_r_restart, 2.0, '팝업 클릭')}
STOP_TRIGGERS = {'포만감 템 없음', '무게 꽉 참'}
NOTIFY_TRIGGERS = {'친구 요청'}
