JAMUN_INPUT_XY = (500, 425)
JAMUN_INPUT_ERROR_IDS = frozenset({'ttatta12', 'ttatta13'})
JAMUN_INPUT_CHARS = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789'
def _jamun_submission_text(text: str, auth_id: str) -> str:
    if auth_id not in JAMUN_INPUT_ERROR_IDS or len(text) != 5 or any((ch not in JAMUN_INPUT_CHARS for ch in text)):
        return text
    else:
        if random.randrange(200) >= 6:
            return text
        else:
            pos = random.randrange(len(text))
            choices = JAMUN_INPUT_CHARS.replace(text[pos], '')
            return text[:pos] + random.choice(choices) + text[pos + 1:]
class BattleIO:
    def __init__(self, engine, hands):
        self._engine = engine
        self._hands = hands
    def capture(self):
        h = self._engine.hwnd
        frame = grab_hwnd(h) if h else grab_once()
        return frame if frame is not None else self._engine.snapshot()
    def capture_after(self, after, timeout=0.25, stop=None):
        h = self._engine.hwnd
        if not h:
            return
        else:
            return grab_hwnd_after(h, after, timeout=timeout, stop=stop)
    def press(self, name, hold=0.05):
        self._hands.press(name, hold, wait=True)
    def hold(self, name):
        self._hands.key_hold(name, wait=True)
    def release(self, name):
        self._hands.key_release(name, wait=True)
    def click(self, x, y, button='left'):
        self._hands.click(int(x), int(y), button, wait=True)
    def move(self, x, y):
        self._hands.move(int(x), int(y), wait=True)
class Brain:
    INTERVAL = 1.0 / 60.0   # loop pacing target; the loop actually waits for each NEW captured frame
    TICK_UNITS = 0.1        # old 10 Hz loop period: loop-count constants are kept in these units
    def __init__(self, engine, vision, hands, note=print, notify=None, notify_img=None, report_battle=None, report_appear=None, on_stopped=None, banja_ok=None, no_battle_limit=None, debug_jochul=None, debug_jamun=None, debug_jamun_wrong=None, auth_id=None, on_auto_restart=None):
        self.engine = engine
        self.vision = vision
        self.hands = hands
        self.note = note
        self._notify_push = notify or (lambda msg: None)
        self._notify_img = notify_img or (lambda msg, img: None)
        self._on_stopped = on_stopped or (lambda: None)
        self._on_auto_restart = on_auto_restart or (lambda: None)
        self._auto_restart = True   # cleared by stop() (an explicit/user/schedule stop); every other
                                    # stop path (_stop_bot/_stop_now - i.e. the bot's OWN decision,
                                    # such as a false no-fight timeout) leaves this True
        self._auto_restart_at = []  # timestamps of recent auto-restarts, to cap a crash-loop
        self._report_battle = report_battle or (lambda: None)
        self._report_appear = report_appear or (lambda kind, wrong=False: None)
        self._banja_ok = banja_ok or (lambda: False)
        self._nb_limit = no_battle_limit or (lambda: 0)
        self._debug_jochul = debug_jochul
        self._debug_jamun = debug_jamun
        self._debug_jamun_wrong = debug_jamun_wrong or (lambda: None)
        self._auth_id = auth_id or (lambda: '')
        self.running = False
        self._thread = None
        self.monster = config.load().get('current_monster', config.DEFAULT_MONSTER)
        self.detector = None
        self.battle_detector = None
        self.aim_y = 30.0
        self.fire_roi = None
        self.trust_avoid = None
        self._react_at = {}
        self._battle_running = False
        self._battle_at = 0.0
        self._battle_click = 0
        self._digit_at = 0.0
        self._tab_at = 0.0
        self._battle_thread = None
        self._shutdown_at = None
        self._no_battle_count = 0
        self._sub_hands = {}
        self._sub_list = []
        self._sub_list_at = 0.0
        self._sub_sniff_prev = {}
        self._sub_solving = False
        self.rest_mode = False
        self.sched_target = None
        self._io = BattleIO(engine, hands)
        self.battle_count = self._load_count()
        self._prev_fired = set()
        self.jochul_solver = jochul.JochulSolver(config.JOCHUL_MODEL)
        self.text_reader = captcha.TextReader(config.TEXT_MODEL)
        self.solver_errors = {}
        self._solvers_loading = True
        self._jochul_at = 0.0
        self._jamun_at = 0.0
        self._solve_thread = None
        self._prev_main = None
        self._gm_at = 0.0
        self._kill_rank = []
        self._build_detectors()
        self._preload_async()
        self._preload_solvers()
        self._moved_at = time.time()
        self._afk_thread = None
        self.start_afk()
    # Anti-AFK (NATAS 2026-11-18, not vendor): 거상 drops to the menu after ~5 min without the
    # character moving; key presses don't count. Walk one step out with a right-click (the same
    # button the bot attacks with) and walk back. Runs whether or not the bot is started.
    AFK_SEC = 120.0
    AFK_STEP = 48
    def start_afk(self):
        if self._afk_thread and self._afk_thread.is_alive():
            return
        self._afk_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._afk_loop, daemon=True, name='prism-afk')
        self._afk_thread.start()
    def _afk_busy(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self._battle_running or self._sub_solving or (self._solve_thread and self._solve_thread.is_alive()))
    def _afk_loop(self):
        while True:
            time.sleep(5.0)
            try:
                self._ensure_window_visible()
                self._check_idle_warning()
                self._check_revive_popup()
                self._learn_mercs()
                if False and time.time() - max(self._moved_at, self._battle_at) >= self.AFK_SEC and not self._afk_busy():
                    # DISABLED (manager, 2026-11-19): confirmed live, twice,
                    # that this nudge's right-click reliably lands on a map
                    # hotspot at 초보자군영 that opens a REAL paid-travel
                    # confirmation ('무투장으로 이동하시겠습니까? 수수료는
                    # 10,000냥입니다.') instead of just walking. It did not
                    # self-confirm either time, but it reappears every AFK
                    # cycle and the risk of it ever being accidentally
                    # confirmed is not acceptable unattended. Do not
                    # re-enable until it uses a movement method proven not
                    # to open any dialog (e.g. keyboard-direction movement,
                    # or a per-call screenshot check that recognizes and
                    # safely cancels ANY dialog it causes) -- see also the
                    # reverted dismiss-click attempt earlier this session,
                    # which made the same dialog worse, not better.
                    self._afk_nudge()
            except Exception as e:
                self._log(f'[AFK] 오류: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')
                self._moved_at = time.time()
    def _ensure_window_visible(self):
        # Observed live 2026-11-18: after a Windows lock/unlock cycle the game
        # window can end up with WS_VISIBLE cleared even though the process
        # is alive and responding -- looks exactly like "the game quit" from
        # the user's side, but nothing crashed. Self-heal it here so an
        # unattended run doesn't sit invisible for hours.
        hwnd = find_window()
        if not hwnd:
            return
        if not win32gui.IsWindowVisible(hwnd):
            self._log('[AFK] 게임 창이 숨겨짐 감지 — 복구 시도')
            win32gui.ShowWindow(hwnd, win32con.SW_RESTORE)
            win32gui.ShowWindow(hwnd, win32con.SW_SHOW)
            self._log('[AFK] 게임 창 복구 완료' if win32gui.IsWindowVisible(hwnd) else '[AFK] 게임 창 복구 실패 — 수동 확인 필요')
    def _check_idle_warning(self):
        # The real mechanism behind "game auto-returns to login screen when
        # idle" (confirmed live 2026-11-19): a '5분 뒤 캐릭터 선택 화면으로
        # 이동됩니다' dialog with a single 확인 button. Detecting and
        # clicking it is a left-click on one known, single-purpose button --
        # no movement, no risk of landing on a map hotspot or opening any
        # other menu, unlike the old right-click nudge.
        frame = self._grab_main()
        if frame is None:
            return
        pt = vis.find_idle_warning(frame)
        if pt is None:
            return
        self.hands.click(pt[0], pt[1], 'left', wait=True)
        time.sleep(0.5)
        self._moved_at = time.time()
        self._log('[AFK] 유휴 경고창 감지 — 확인 클릭')
    def _afk_nudge(self):
        before = self._grab_main()
        if before is None or vis.detect_scene(before) != 'main' or vis.check_triggers(before):
            return
        cx, cy = int(detector.CENTER[0]), int(detector.CENTER[1])
        self.hands.click(cx + self.AFK_STEP, cy, 'right', wait=True)
        time.sleep(1.5)
        after = self._grab_main()
        dx = dy = 0.0
        if after is not None:
            a = cv2.cvtColor(before[:vis.FIELD_H], cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY).astype(np.float32)
            b = cv2.cvtColor(after[:vis.FIELD_H], cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY).astype(np.float32)
            (dx, dy), _ = cv2.phaseCorrelate(a, b)
            lim = 2 * self.AFK_STEP
            dx = max(-lim, min(lim, dx))
            dy = max(-lim, min(lim, dy))
        # camera shift dx,dy: the spot we started on is now at center + shift (center if the camera is fixed)
        self.hands.click(int(round(cx + dx)), int(round(cy + dy)), 'right', wait=True)
        time.sleep(1.5)
        final = self._grab_main()
        if final is not None:
            self._prev_main = final[:vis.FIELD_H].copy()
        self._moved_at = time.time()
        self._log(f'[AFK] 캐릭터 이동 (화면 이동 {dx:+.0f},{dy:+.0f})')
        # NOTE (manager, 2026-11-19): tried adding an automatic "dismiss any
        # accidental menu" click here (left-click on own position) after a
        # right-click landed on a building and opened a context menu once.
        # Reverted immediately: that dismiss click itself triggered a SECOND
        # unintended menu (a paid 무투장 fast-travel confirmation, 10,000냥)
        # on a live run. A stray menu sitting open is a cosmetic/vision
        # annoyance; blindly clicking to "dismiss" risks confirming a real
        # paid action. Do not re-add this without a reliable way to
        # recognize and cancel the SPECIFIC dialog that appeared, not a
        # blind click-and-hope.
    def _log(self, msg: str):
        try:
            self.note(msg)
        except Exception:
            return None
    def _grab_main(self, normalize: bool=True):
        h = self.engine.hwnd
        if h and (not win32gui.IsWindow(h)):
                h = 0
        if h:
            return grab_hwnd(h, normalize=normalize)
        else:
            return grab_once(normalize=normalize)
    def _notify(self, msg: str):
        self._log(msg)
        try:
            self._notify_push(msg)
        except Exception:
            return None
    def _notify_popup(self, name: str):
        self._log(f'감지: {name}')
        img = self._grab_main()
        if img is not None:
            x0, y0, x1, y1 = POPUP_CROP
            h, w = img.shape[:2]
            if x1 <= w and y1 <= h:
                try:
                    self._notify_img(f'🔔 {name}', img[y0:y1, x0:x1])
                    return
                except Exception:
                    pass
        self._notify(f'🔔 {name}')
        return
    def _chat_state(self) -> str:
        f = self._grab_main()
        return vis.chat_hangul_state(f) if f is not None else 'inactive'
    def _type_tokens(self, text: str, gap: float=0.2):
        for shift, key in chat.to_tokens(text):
            if isinstance(key, int):
                self.hands.press_vk(key, shift=shift, wait=True)
            else:
                if shift:
                    self.hands.combo('shift', key, wait=True)
                else:
                    self.hands.press(key, wait=True)
            time.sleep(gap)
    def type_chat(self, text: str, pre_click=None):
        text = chat.filter_text(text)
        if not text:
            return
        else:
            if pre_click is not None:
                self.hands.click(*pre_click, wait=True)
                time.sleep(0.2)
            state = self._chat_state()
            for _ in range(2):
                if state != 'inactive':
                    break
                else:
                    self.hands.press('enter', wait=True)
                    time.sleep(0.25)
                    state = self._chat_state()
            if state == 'inactive':
                self._log('채팅창 활성화 실패 — 전송 취소')
            else:
                if state == 'english':
                    self.hands.toggle_hangul(wait=True)
                    time.sleep(0.2)
                self._type_tokens(text)
                self.hands.press('enter', wait=True)
                time.sleep(0.2)
                for _ in range(2):
                    if self._chat_state() == 'inactive':
                        return
                    else:
                        self.hands.press('enter', wait=True)
                        time.sleep(0.25)
    W_MEMO = (227, 693)
    W_TAB1 = (155, 126)
    W_TAB_DY = 18
    W_INPUT = (322, 284)
    W_BOX = (119, 95, 462, 297)
    def _whisper_open_now(self) -> bool:
        img = self._grab_main()
        return img is not None and vis.whisper_is_open(img)
    def _whisper_crop(self):
        img = self._grab_main()
        if img is None:
            return
        else:
            x1, y1, x2, y2 = self.W_BOX
            return img[y1:y2, x1:x2].copy()
    def _whisper_close(self):
        for _ in range(2):
            if not self._whisper_open_now():
                return
            else:
                self.hands.click(*self.W_MEMO, wait=True)
                time.sleep(0.3)
        if self._whisper_open_now():
            self._log('⚠ 귓말창 닫힘 확인 실패')
            self._notify('귓말창이 닫히지 않았습니다 — 화면 확인 필요')
    def send_whisper(self, slot: int, msg: str):
        msg = chat.filter_text(msg)
        if not msg:
            return
        if not self._whisper_open_now():
            self.hands.click(*self.W_MEMO, wait=True)
            time.sleep(0.8)
            if not self._whisper_open_now():
                self._notify_img('귓말창 열기 실패 — 현재 화면 첨부', self._grab_main())
                self._whisper_close()
                return
        self.hands.click(self.W_TAB1[0], self.W_TAB1[1] + (slot - 1) * self.W_TAB_DY, wait=True)
        time.sleep(0.15)
        self.hands.click(*self.W_INPUT, wait=True)
        time.sleep(0.15)
        state = self._chat_state()
        if state == 'inactive':
            crop = self._whisper_crop()
            self._whisper_close()
            self._notify_img(f'귓말{slot} 대상이 없습니다', crop)
        else:
            if state == 'english':
                self.hands.toggle_hangul(wait=True)
                time.sleep(0.2)
            self._type_tokens(msg, gap=0.05)
            self.hands.press('enter', wait=True)
            time.sleep(0.15)
            crop = self._whisper_crop()
            for _ in range(2):
                if self._chat_state() == 'inactive':
                    break
                else:
                    self.hands.press('enter', wait=True)
                    time.sleep(0.2)
            self._whisper_close()
            self._notify_img(f'📨 귓말{slot} 전송: {msg}', crop)
    def _check_scene_change(self):
        frame = self._grab_main()
        if frame is None:
            return
        else:
            field = frame[:vis.FIELD_H].copy()
            if self._prev_main is not None:
                sim = vis.scene_similarity(self._prev_main, field)
                if sim < vis.SCENE_SIM_THRESH:
                    stop = not self.ignore_teleport
                    msg = f'⚠ 배경 급변 (유사도 {sim:.2f}) — 맵 이동/영자 소환 의심' + ('' if stop else ' (이동 잦은 몹 — 정지 안 함)')
                    self._log(msg)
                    self._notify_img(msg, frame)
                    if stop and self._shutdown_at is None:
                            self._shutdown_at = time.time() + STOP_GRACE
                            self._stop_reason = '배경 급변 (맵 이동/영자 소환 의심)'
                else:
                    self._log(f'배경 유사도 {sim:.2f}')
            self._prev_main = field
    SUB_TRIGGERS = {'사망', '포만감 템 없음'}
    SUB_MAIN_WAIT = 120.0
    def _sub_hands_for(self, hwnd: int, tag: str) -> Hands:
        self._sub_hands = {h: v for h, v in self._sub_hands.items() if win32gui.IsWindow(h)}
        if not hasattr(self, '_sub_tag'):
            self._sub_tag = {}
        self._sub_tag[hwnd] = tag
        hands = self._sub_hands.get(hwnd)
        if hands is None:
            hands = Hands(hwnd_get=lambda h=hwnd: h, log=lambda s, h_=hwnd: self._log(f"[{self._sub_tag.get(h_, '서브창')}] {s}"))
            self._sub_hands[hwnd] = hands
        return hands
    def _check_sub_windows(self):
        try:
            main = self.engine.hwnd
            subs = [h for h in find_windows() if h != main][:2]
        except Exception:
            return None
        if len(subs) != getattr(self, '_sub_n', (-1)):
            self._sub_n = len(subs)
            if subs:
                self._log(f'서브 거상 창 {len(subs)}개 점검 대상')
        if not subs:
            return
        else:
            for i, hwnd in enumerate(subs, 1):
                if win32gui.IsIconic(hwnd):
                    self._log(f'[서브창{i}] 최소화 상태 → 복원 (포커스 안 뺏음)')
                    try:
                        win32gui.ShowWindow(hwnd, win32con.SW_SHOWNOACTIVATE)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                    time.sleep(0.3)
            deadline = time.time() + self.SUB_MAIN_WAIT
            waiting = list(subs)
            logged = False
            while waiting and self.running and (time.time() < deadline):
                        waiting = [h for h in waiting if win32gui.IsWindow(h) and (not combat.is_main(grab_hwnd(h)))]
                        if waiting:
                            if not logged:
                                logged = True
                                self._log(f'서브창 {len(waiting)}개 메인 복귀 대기…')
                            time.sleep(0.2)
            for h in waiting:
                self._log(f'[서브창{subs.index(h) + 1}] {self.SUB_MAIN_WAIT:.0f}초 내 메인 미복귀 — 그냥 진행')
            for i, hwnd in enumerate(subs, 1):
                try:
                    l, t, r_, b = win32gui.GetClientRect(hwnd)
                    if (r_ - l, b - t) != (1024, 768) and hwnd not in getattr(self, '_sub_res_warned', set()):
                            self._sub_res_warned = getattr(self, '_sub_res_warned', set())
                            self._sub_res_warned.add(hwnd)
                            self._notify(f'⚠️ 서브창{i} 해상도 {r_ - l}×{b - t} — 기준 1024×768이 아니라 포만감/사망 판정 불가')
                except Exception:
                    pass
                img = grab_hwnd(hwnd)
                if img is None:
                    self._log(f'[서브창{i}] 캡처 실패 — 점검 스킵')
                    continue
                else:
                    fired = vis.check_triggers(img)
                    tag = f'서브창{i}'
                    if fired & self.SUB_TRIGGERS:
                        hands = self._sub_hands_for(hwnd, tag)
                        if '사망' in fired:
                            self._log(f'[{tag}] 사망 감지 → 부활 + 불사신부')
                            hands.move(557, 440, wait=True)
                            time.sleep(0.3)
                            hands.click(557, 440, wait=True)
                    if self._sub_solving:
                        continue
                    else:
                        if '조철 출현' in fired:
                            self._handle_jochul(hands=self._sub_hands_for(hwnd, tag), hwnd=hwnd, tag=tag, park=False)
                        else:
                            if '자방문 출현' in fired:
                                self._handle_jamun(hands=self._sub_hands_for(hwnd, tag), hwnd=hwnd, tag=tag)
            if subs:
                self._focus_main()
    def _focus_main(self):
        try:
            self.hands.move(*combat.FIELD_CENTER, wait=True)
        except Exception:
            return None
    def _retreat_subs(self):
        try:
            main = self.engine.hwnd
            subs = [h for h in find_windows() if h != main][:2]
        except Exception:
            return None
        for i, hwnd in enumerate(subs, 1):
            img = grab_hwnd(hwnd)
            if img is None or not combat.is_battle(img):
                continue
            else:
                tag = f'서브창{i}'
                self._log(f'[{tag}] 전투화면 → 퇴각')
                hands = self._sub_hands_for(hwnd, tag)
                hands.move(*combat.FIELD_CENTER, wait=True)
                time.sleep(0.3)
                for _ in range(3):
                    hands.press('esc', wait=True)
                    time.sleep(0.05)
    def _banja_sub_entry(self):
        try:
            main = self.engine.hwnd
            subs = [h for h in find_windows() if h != main][:2]
        except Exception:
            return None
        pressed = False
        for i, hwnd in enumerate(subs, 1):
            tag = f'서브창{i}'
            deadline = time.time() + self.BANJA_SUB_BATTLE_WAIT
            in_battle = False
            while time.time() < deadline and self.running:
                    img = grab_hwnd(hwnd)

def _get_process_exe(pid: int) -> str:
    PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION = 4096
    handle = kernel32.OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, False, pid)
    if not handle:
        return ''
    else:
        try:
            buf = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(ctypes.wintypes.MAX_PATH)
            size = ctypes.wintypes.DWORD(ctypes.wintypes.MAX_PATH)
            ok = kernel32.QueryFullProcessImageNameW(handle, 0, buf, ctypes.byref(size))
            return buf.value.lower() if ok else ''
        finally:
            kernel32.CloseHandle(handle)
def find_windows(process_name: str=PROCESS_NAME) -> list:
    """Enumerates all game windows for this process name. When GATO_TARGET_PID
    is set (one real game client per account under 다클), this restricts the
    result to that single PID's window(s) -- without it, the first multi-
    instance run would have N bot processes all race to grab whichever
    window EnumWindows happens to return first, each controlling a RANDOM
    account's client instead of its own. Each bot process for 다클 must be
    launched with its own GATO_TARGET_PID pointing at the pid it actually
    logged into.

    A single Gersang.exe process owns several windows (the real game window
    titled exactly 'Gersang', plus helper windows like 'GDI+ Window
    (Gersang.exe)' and 'DIEmWin' that also have a non-empty title and so
    also pass the old filter). Observed live 2026-11-18: right after
    launch, EnumWindows can return the helper window before the real one,
    and the helper window stays at a degenerate 1x1 client rect forever --
    callers that grab the first match get a window that will never show
    real content. Sort results so an exact title match ('Gersang') comes
    first, which is stable regardless of enumeration order.
    """
    target_pid = os.environ.get('GATO_TARGET_PID')
    target_pid = int(target_pid) if target_pid else None
    result = []
    def _enum(hwnd, _):
        if not win32gui.IsWindowVisible(hwnd):
            return
        else:
            if win32gui.GetWindowTextLength(hwnd) == 0:
                return
            else:
                _, pid = win32process.GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd)
                if target_pid is not None and pid != target_pid:
                    return
                if _get_process_exe(pid).lower().endswith('\\' + process_name.lower()):
                    result.append(hwnd)
    win32gui.EnumWindows(_enum, None)
    result.sort(key=lambda h: 0 if win32gui.GetWindowText(h) == 'Gersang' else 1)
    return result
def find_window(process_name: str=PROCESS_NAME) -> int:
    result = find_windows(process_name)
    return result[0] if result else 0
def grab_once(process_name: str=PROCESS_NAME, normalize: bool=True) -> 'np.ndarray | None':
    return grab_hwnd(find_window(process_name), normalize=normalize)
def grab_hwnd_after(hwnd: int, after: float, timeout: float=0.25, stop=None, normalize: bool=True) -> 'np.ndarray | None':
    if not hwnd or (stop is not None and stop()):
        return None
    else:
        bgr = wgc.grab_after(hwnd, after, timeout=timeout, stop=stop)
        if bgr is None:
            return
        else:
            h, w = bgr.shape[:2]
            if normalize and (w, h) != (REF_W, REF_H):
                    bgr = cv2.resize(bgr, (REF_W, REF_H), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
            return None if stop is not None and stop() else bgr
def grab_hwnd(hwnd: int, normalize: bool=True) -> 'np.ndarray | None':
    if not hwnd:
        return None
    bgr = wgc.grab(hwnd)
    if bgr is not None:
        h, w = bgr.shape[:2]
        if normalize and (w, h) != (REF_W, REF_H):
            bgr = cv2.resize(bgr, (REF_W, REF_H), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
        return bgr
    try:
        left, top, right, bottom = win32gui.GetClientRect(hwnd)
    except win32gui.error:
        return None
    w, h = (right - left, bottom - top)
    if w <= 0 or h <= 0:
        return None
    hwnd_dc = user32.GetDC(hwnd)
    if not hwnd_dc:
        return None
    mem_dc = hbmp = old = None
    try:
        mem_dc = gdi32.CreateCompatibleDC(hwnd_dc)
        hbmp = gdi32.CreateCompatibleBitmap(hwnd_dc, w, h)
        old = gdi32.SelectObject(mem_dc, hbmp)
        if not gdi32.BitBlt(mem_dc, 0, 0, w, h, hwnd_dc, 0, 0, win32con.SRCCOPY):
            return None
        buf = ctypes.create_string_buffer(w * h * 4)
        bmi = BITMAPINFO()
        bmi.bmiHeader.biSize = ctypes.sizeof(BITMAPINFOHEADER)
        bmi.bmiHeader.biWidth = w
        bmi.bmiHeader.biHeight = -h
        bmi.bmiHeader.biPlanes = 1
        bmi.bmiHeader.biBitCount = 32
        bmi.bmiHeader.biCompression = BI_RGB
        gdi32.SelectObject(mem_dc, old)
        lines = gdi32.GetDIBits(mem_dc, hbmp, 0, h, buf, ctypes.byref(bmi), DIB_RGB_COLORS)
        gdi32.SelectObject(mem_dc, hbmp)
        if lines != h:
            return None
        bgra = np.frombuffer(buf, np.uint8).reshape(h, w, 4)
        bgr = np.ascontiguousarray(bgra[:, :, :3])
        if normalize and (w, h) != (REF_W, REF_H):
            bgr = cv2.resize(bgr, (REF_W, REF_H), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
        return bgr
    finally:
        if mem_dc and old:
            gdi32.SelectObject(mem_dc, old)
        if hbmp:
            gdi32.DeleteObject(hbmp)
        if mem_dc:
            gdi32.DeleteDC(mem_dc)
        user32.ReleaseDC(hwnd, hwnd_dc)


def grab_hwnd_region(hwnd: int, x0: int, y0: int, x1: int, y1: int) -> 'np.ndarray | None':
    if not hwnd:
        return None
    try:
        left, top, right, bottom = win32gui.GetClientRect(hwnd)
    except win32gui.error:
        return None
    cw, ch = (right - left, bottom - top)
    if (cw, ch) != (REF_W, REF_H):
        return None
    w, h = (x1 - x0, y1 - y0)
    if w <= 0 or h <= 0 or x1 > cw or y1 > ch:
        return None
    f = wgc.grab(hwnd)
    if f is not None:
        if f.shape[:2] != (REF_H, REF_W):
            return None
        return np.ascontiguousarray(f[y0:y1, x0:x1])
    hwnd_dc = user32.GetDC(hwnd)
    if not hwnd_dc:
        return None
    mem_dc = hbmp = old = None
    try:
        mem_dc = gdi32.CreateCompatibleDC(hwnd_dc)
        hbmp = gdi32.CreateCompatibleBitmap(hwnd_dc, w, h)
        old = gdi32.SelectObject(mem_dc, hbmp)
        if not gdi32.BitBlt(mem_dc, 0, 0, w, h, hwnd_dc, x0, y0, win32con.SRCCOPY):
            return None
        buf = ctypes.create_string_buffer(w * h * 4)
        bmi = BITMAPINFO()
        bmi.bmiHeader.biSize = ctypes.sizeof(BITMAPINFOHEADER)
        bmi.bmiHeader.biWidth = w
        bmi.bmiHeader.biHeight = -h
        bmi.bmiHeader.biPlanes = 1
        bmi.bmiHeader.biBitCount = 32
        bmi.bmiHeader.biCompression = BI_RGB
        gdi32.SelectObject(mem_dc, old)
        lines = gdi32.GetDIBits(mem_dc, hbmp, 0, h, buf, ctypes.byref(bmi), DIB_RGB_COLORS)
        gdi32.SelectObject(mem_dc, hbmp)
        if lines != h:
            return None
        bgra = np.frombuffer(buf, np.uint8).reshape(h, w, 4)
        return np.ascontiguousarray(bgra[:, :, :3])
    finally:
        if mem_dc and old:
            gdi32.SelectObject(mem_dc, old)
        if hbmp:
            gdi32.DeleteObject(hbmp)
        if mem_dc:
            gdi32.DeleteDC(mem_dc)
        user32.ReleaseDC(hwnd, hwnd_dc)


class CaptureEngine:
    REFIND_INTERVAL = 1.0
    def __init__(self, fps: float=60.0, process_name: str=PROCESS_NAME):
        self.fps = fps
        self.process_name = process_name
        self._hwnd = 0
        self._pid = 0
        self._last_find = 0.0
        self._mem_dc = None
        self._hbmp = None
        self._old_bmp = None
        self._buf = None
        self._bgra = None
        self._bmi = None
        self._size = (0, 0)
        self._frame_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._bgr = None
        self._bgr_ref = None
        self._frame_out = None
        self._frame_id = 0
        self._backend = ''
        self._last_ok = 0.0
        self._times = deque(maxlen=32)
        self._cap_ms = 0.0
        self._frames = 0
        self._thread = None
        self._stop = threading.Event()
        self._subscribers = []
    def subscribe(self, fn) -> None:
        self._subscribers.append(fn)
    def start(self):
        if self._thread and self._thread.is_alive():
                return
        self._stop.clear()
        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._loop, daemon=True, name='prism-capture')
        self._thread.start()
    def stop(self):
        self._stop.set()
        if self._thread:
            self._thread.join(timeout=2.0)
            self._thread = None
        self._hwnd = 0
    @property
    def running(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self._thread and self._thread.is_alive())
    @property
    def hwnd(self) -> int:
        return self._hwnd
    STALE_SEC = 1.0
    def snapshot(self) -> np.ndarray | None:
        with self._frame_lock:
            if self._frame_out is None or time.time() - self._last_ok > self.STALE_SEC:
                return
            else:
                return self._frame_out.copy()
    def jpeg(self, quality: int=70) -> bytes | None:
        with self._frame_lock:
            if self._frame_out is None:
                return
            else:
                ok, enc = cv2.imencode('.jpg', self._frame_out, [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, quality])
        return enc.tobytes() if ok else None
    def wait_new(self, last_id: int, timeout: float = 0.03) -> int:
        """Block (1 ms steps) until a frame newer than last_id was published; returns the current id."""
        end = time.perf_counter() + timeout
        while self._frame_id == last_id and time.perf_counter() < end:
            time.sleep(0.001)
        return self._frame_id
    def stats(self) -> dict:
        now = time.perf_counter()
        times = [t for t in self._times if now - t <= 3.0]
        fps = 0.0
        if len(times) >= 2:
            fps = (len(times) - 1) / (times[(-1)] - times[0])
        w, h = self._size
        return {'running': self.running, 'window_found': bool(self._hwnd), 'width': w, 'height': h, 'fps': round(fps, 1), 'cap_ms': round(self._cap_ms, 1), 'frames': self._frames, 'frame_id': self._frame_id, 'backend': self._backend}
    def _loop(self):
        interval = 1.0 / self.fps
        next_t = time.perf_counter()
        try:
            while not self._stop.is_set():
                t0 = time.perf_counter()
                if self._capture_once():
                    dt = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0
                    self._cap_ms = dt if self._cap_ms == 0 else self._cap_ms * 0.9 + dt * 0.1
                    self._times.append(time.perf_counter())
                    self._frames += 1
                next_t += interval
                now = time.perf_counter()
                if next_t <= now:
                    next_t = now + interval
                self._stop.wait(next_t - now)
        finally:
            self._free_resources()

    def _ensure_hwnd(self) -> int:
        if self._hwnd and win32gui.IsWindow(self._hwnd):
            return self._hwnd
        else:
            self._hwnd = 0
            now = time.perf_counter()
            if now - self._last_find >= self.REFIND_INTERVAL:
                self._last_find = now
                self._hwnd = self._find_sticky()
            return self._hwnd
    def _find_sticky(self) -> int:
        wins = find_windows(self.process_name)
        wgc.prune(wins)
        if not wins:
            return 0
        else:
            if self._pid:
                for h in wins:
                    _, pid = win32process.GetWindowThreadProcessId(h)
                    if pid == self._pid:
                        return h
            h = wins[0]
            _, self._pid = win32process.GetWindowThreadProcessId(h)
            return h
    def _capture_once(self) -> bool:
        hwnd = self._ensure_hwnd()
        if not hwnd:
            return False
        else:
            src = wgc.grab(hwnd)
            if src is not None:
                self._backend = 'wgc'
                return self._publish(src)
            else:
                if not self._blit_gdi(hwnd):
                    return False
                else:
                    self._backend = 'gdi'
                    with self._frame_lock:
                        w, h = self._size
                        if self._bgr is None or self._bgr.shape[:2] != (h, w):
                            self._bgr = np.empty((h, w, 3), np.uint8)
                        cv2.cvtColor(self._bgra, cv2.COLOR_BGRA2BGR, dst=self._bgr)
                    return self._publish(self._bgr)
    def _blit_gdi(self, hwnd: int) -> bool:
        try:
            left, top, right, bottom = win32gui.GetClientRect(hwnd)
        except win32gui.error:
            self._hwnd = 0
            return False
        w, h = (right - left, bottom - top)
        if w <= 0 or h <= 0:
            return False
        hwnd_dc = user32.GetDC(hwnd)
        if not hwnd_dc:
            return False
        try:
            if (w, h) != self._size or self._mem_dc is None:
                self._create_resources(hwnd_dc, w, h)
            if not gdi32.BitBlt(self._mem_dc, 0, 0, w, h, hwnd_dc, 0, 0, win32con.SRCCOPY):
                return False
            gdi32.SelectObject(self._mem_dc, self._old_bmp)
            lines = gdi32.GetDIBits(self._mem_dc, self._hbmp, 0, h, self._buf, ctypes.byref(self._bmi), DIB_RGB_COLORS)
            gdi32.SelectObject(self._mem_dc, self._hbmp)
            if lines != h:
                return False
            return True
        finally:
            user32.ReleaseDC(hwnd, hwnd_dc)

    def _publish(self, bgr) -> bool:
        h, w = bgr.shape[:2]
        self._size = (w, h)
        with self._frame_lock:
            self._bgr = bgr
            if (w, h) == (REF_W, REF_H):
                self._frame_out = self._bgr
            else:
                if self._bgr_ref is None:
                    self._bgr_ref = np.empty((REF_H, REF_W, 3), np.uint8)
                cv2.resize(self._bgr, (REF_W, REF_H), dst=self._bgr_ref, interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
                self._frame_out = self._bgr_ref
            self._frame_id += 1
            self._last_ok = time.time()
            frame, fid = (self._frame_out, self._frame_id)
        for fn in self._subscribers:
            try:
                fn(frame, fid)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return True
    def _create_resources(self, hwnd_dc: int, w: int, h: int):
        self._free_resources()
        self._mem_dc = gdi32.CreateCompatibleDC(hwnd_dc)
        self._hbmp = gdi32.CreateCompatibleBitmap(hwnd_dc, w, h)
        self._old_bmp = gdi32.SelectObject(self._mem_dc, self._hbmp)
        self._buf = ctypes.create_string_buffer(w * h * 4)
        self._bgra = np.frombuffer(self._buf, np.uint8).reshape(h, w, 4)
        bmi = BITMAPINFO()
        bmi.bmiHeader.biSize = ctypes.sizeof(BITMAPINFOHEADER)
        bmi.bmiHeader.biWidth = w
        bmi.bmiHeader.biHeight = -h
        bmi.bmiHeader.biPlanes = 1
        bmi.bmiHeader.biBitCount = 32
        bmi.bmiHeader.biCompression = BI_RGB
        self._bmi = bmi
        self._size = (w, h)
    def _free_resources(self):
        if self._mem_dc:
            if self._old_bmp:
                gdi32.SelectObject(self._mem_dc, self._old_bmp)
            gdi32.DeleteDC(self._mem_dc)
        if self._hbmp:
            gdi32.DeleteObject(self._hbmp)
        self._mem_dc = self._hbmp = self._old_bmp = None
        self._buf = self._bgra = self._bmi = None
        self._size = (0, 0)
if __name__ == '__main__':
    eng = CaptureEngine()
    eng.start()
    time.sleep(2.0)
    print(eng.stats())
    frame = eng.snapshot()
    print('frame:', None if frame is None else frame.shape)

def is_admin() -> bool:

    except Exception:

def ensure_admin() -> bool:
    if is_admin():
        return True
    else:
        if os.environ.get('PRISM_NO_ELEVATE') == '1':
            return False
        else:
            script = os.path.abspath(sys.argv[0])
            pyw = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(sys.executable), 'pythonw.exe')
            launcher = pyw if os.path.exists(pyw) else sys.executable
            ret = ctypes.windll.shell32.ShellExecuteW(None, 'runas', launcher, f'\"{script}\"', None, 1)
            if ret > 32:
                sys.exit(0)
            return False
_ULONG_PTR = ctypes.wintypes.WPARAM
class _KEYBDINPUT(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [('wVk', ctypes.c_ushort), ('wScan', ctypes.c_ushort), ('dwFlags', ctypes.c_ulong), ('time', ctypes.c_ulong), ('dwExtraInfo', _ULONG_PTR)]
class _MOUSEINPUT(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [('dx', ctypes.c_long), ('dy', ctypes.c_long), ('mouseData', ctypes.c_ulong), ('dwFlags', ctypes.c_ulong), ('time', ctypes.c_ulong), ('dwExtraInfo', _ULONG_PTR)]
class _INPUT(ctypes.Structure):
    class _U(ctypes.Union):
        _fields_ = [('ki', _KEYBDINPUT), ('mi', _MOUSEINPUT)]
    _anonymous_ = ('u',)
    _fields_ = [('type', ctypes.c_ulong), ('u', _U)]
_INPUT_MOUSE = 0
_INPUT_KEYBOARD = 1
_KEYUP = 2
_SCANCODE = 8
_EXTENDED = 1
_M_LEFTDOWN, _M_LEFTUP = (2, 4)
_M_RIGHTDOWN, _M_RIGHTUP = (8, 16)
_M_MOVE = 1
_M_ABSOLUTE = 32768
_M_VIRTUALDESK = 16384
def _send(inp: _INPUT) -> None:
    user32.SendInput(1, ctypes.byref(inp), ctypes.sizeof(_INPUT))
def _abs_xy(sx: int, sy: int):
    vx = user32.GetSystemMetrics(76)
    vy = user32.GetSystemMetrics(77)
    vw = user32.GetSystemMetrics(78)
    vh = user32.GetSystemMetrics(79)
    nx = int((sx - vx) * 65535 / max(1, vw - 1))
    ny = int((sy - vy) * 65535 / max(1, vh - 1))
    return (nx, ny)
def _key_event(scan: int, flags: int) -> None:
    inp = _INPUT(type=_INPUT_KEYBOARD)
    inp.ki = _KEYBDINPUT(0, scan, flags, 0, 0)
    _send(inp)
def _vk_event(vk: int, flags: int) -> None:
    inp = _INPUT(type=_INPUT_KEYBOARD)
    inp.ki = _KEYBDINPUT(vk, 0, flags, 0, 0)
    _send(inp)
def _mouse_event(flags: int, dx: int=0, dy: int=0) -> None:
    inp = _INPUT(type=_INPUT_MOUSE)
    inp.mi = _MOUSEINPUT(dx, dy, 0, flags, 0, 0)
    _send(inp)
VK = {'enter': 13, 'esc': 27, 'space': 32, 'tab': 9, 'backspace': 8, 'shift': 16, 'ctrl': 17, 'alt': 18, 'up': 38, 'down': 40, 'left': 37, 'right': 39, 'insert': 45, 'delete': 46, 'home': 36, 'end': 35, 'pageup': 33, 'pagedown': 34}
for _i in range(1, 13):
    VK[f'f{_i}'] = 112 + _i - 1
for _c in '0123456789':
    VK[_c] = ord(_c)
for _c in 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz':
    VK[_c] = ord(_c.upper())
_EXT_KEYS = {'left', 'up', 'pageup', 'pagedown', 'insert', 'end', 'down', 'delete', 'home', 'right'}
VK_HANGUL = 21
class Hands:
    FG_SETTLE = 0.3
    def __init__(self, hwnd_get=None, log=None):
        self._hwnd_get = hwnd_get or (lambda: 0)
        self._log = log or (lambda s: None)
        self._q = queue.Queue()
        self._mouse_held = None
        self._fg_at = 0.0
        self._pin = None             # (screen_x, screen_y, expires_monotonic): where the cursor must stay
        self.guard_enabled = False   # set by Brain while the macro runs
        self.guard_fixes = 0         # times the guard pulled a drifting cursor back
        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._worker, daemon=True, name='prism-hands')
        self._thread.start()
        self._guard_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._guard_loop, daemon=True, name='prism-cursor-guard')
        self._guard_thread.start()
    def _worker(self):
        # ***<module>.Hands._worker: Failure: Different control flow
        while True:
            try:
                fn, done, box = self._q.get()
                try:
                    box[0] = fn()
                except Exception as e:
                    box[0] = False
                    self._log(f'입력 오류: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')
            finally:
                if done is not None:
                    done.set()
    PIN_TTL = 2.5

    def _set_pin(self, sx: int, sy: int) -> None:
        self._pin = (sx, sy, time.monotonic() + self.PIN_TTL)

    def _touch_pin(self) -> None:
        pin = self._pin
        if pin is not None:
            self._pin = (pin[0], pin[1], time.monotonic() + self.PIN_TTL)

    def _guard_loop(self) -> None:
        """Keeps the cursor on the last commanded point while the macro is acting. If the real
        mouse (or anything else) moves it, it is put back within ~5 ms, so a cast or click never
        lands where the cursor drifted to."""
        pt = ctypes.wintypes.POINT()
        while True:
            time.sleep(0.004)
            pin = self._pin
            if pin is None or not self.guard_enabled:
                continue
            if time.monotonic() > pin[2]:
                self._pin = None
                continue
            user32.GetCursorPos(ctypes.byref(pt))
            if pt.x != pin[0] or pt.y != pin[1]:
                user32.SetCursorPos(pin[0], pin[1])
                self.guard_fixes += 1

    def _do(self, fn, wait: bool):
        if not wait:
            self._q.put((fn, None, [None]))
            return
        else:
            done = threading.Event()
            box = [None]
            self._q.put((fn, done, box))
            done.wait(timeout=10.0)
            return box[0]
    def _hwnd(self) -> int:
        hwnd = self._hwnd_get()
        if hwnd and win32gui.IsWindow(hwnd):
            return hwnd
        else:
            return find_window()
    def _foreground(self) -> int:
        hwnd = self._hwnd()
        if not hwnd:
            self._log('입력 스킵 — 거상 창 없음')
            return 0
        else:
            if win32gui.GetForegroundWindow() != hwnd:
                cur_tid = win32api.GetCurrentThreadId()
                fg = win32gui.GetForegroundWindow()
                fg_tid = win32process.GetWindowThreadProcessId(fg)[0] if fg else 0
                attached = False
                if fg_tid and fg_tid != cur_tid:
                        attached = bool(user32.AttachThreadInput(cur_tid, fg_tid, True))
                try:
                    win32gui.ShowWindow(hwnd, win32con.SW_SHOW)
                    try:
                        win32gui.SetForegroundWindow(hwnd)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                finally:
                    if attached:
                        user32.AttachThreadInput(cur_tid, fg_tid, False)
                self._fg_at = time.monotonic()
                time.sleep(0.15)
                if win32gui.GetForegroundWindow() != hwnd:
                    self._log('입력 스킵 — 거상 창 전면 전환 실패')
                    return 0
                else:
                    self._log('전면 전환')
            if self._fg_at:
                remain = self.FG_SETTLE - (time.monotonic() - self._fg_at)
                if remain > 0:
                    time.sleep(remain)
            return hwnd
    def focus_game(self) -> bool:
        try:
            return bool(self._foreground())
        except Exception as e:
            self._log(f'전면 전환 오류: {e}')
            return False
    @staticmethod
    def _to_screen(hwnd: int, x: int, y: int):
        try:
            _, _, cw, ch = win32gui.GetClientRect(hwnd)
            if cw and ch and ((cw, ch) != (REF_W, REF_H)):
                        x = x * cw / REF_W
                        y = y * ch / REF_H
        except Exception:
            pass
        return win32gui.ClientToScreen(hwnd, (int(round(x)), int(round(y))))
    @staticmethod
    def _press_key(name: str, hold: float):
        name = name.lower()
        scan = user32.MapVirtualKeyW(VK[name], 0)
        flags = _SCANCODE | (_EXTENDED if name in _EXT_KEYS else 0)
        _key_event(scan, flags)
        time.sleep(hold)
        _key_event(scan, flags | _KEYUP)
    def move(self, x, y, wait=False):
        def _fn():
            hwnd = self._foreground()
            if not hwnd:
                return False
            else:
                sx, sy = self._to_screen(hwnd, x, y)
                self._pin = None
                ok = self._land(sx, sy)
                if ok:
                    self._set_pin(sx, sy)
                return ok
        return self._do(_fn, wait)
    def move_nofg(self, x, y, wait=False):
        def _fn():
            hwnd = self._hwnd()
            if not hwnd:
                return False
            else:
                self._pin = None     # explicit park: the cursor guard must not drag it back to the last click point
                try:
                    user32.SetCursorPos(*self._to_screen(hwnd, x, y))
                except Exception:
                    return False
                return True
        return self._do(_fn, wait)
    @staticmethod
    def _land(sx: int, sy: int) -> bool:
        """SetCursorPos, then read the cursor back; retry so a click never fires off-target."""
        pt = ctypes.wintypes.POINT()
        for _ in range(4):
            user32.SetCursorPos(sx, sy)
            user32.GetCursorPos(ctypes.byref(pt))
            if pt.x == sx and pt.y == sy:
                return True
            time.sleep(0.004)
        return False
    def click(self, x, y, button='left', down_ms=0.08, wait=False):
        def _fn():
            hwnd = self._foreground()
            if not hwnd:
                return False
            else:
                sx, sy = self._to_screen(hwnd, x, y)
                nx, ny = _abs_xy(sx, sy)
                absf = _M_ABSOLUTE | _M_VIRTUALDESK
                self._pin = None
                if not self._land(sx, sy):
                    self._log(f'클릭 스킵 — 커서 안착 실패 ({sx},{sy})')
                    return False
                self._set_pin(sx, sy)            # guard holds the cursor here through the click
                _mouse_event(_M_MOVE | absf, nx, ny)
                time.sleep(0.012)
                if not self._land(sx, sy):
                    self._log(f'클릭 스킵 — 커서 이탈 ({sx},{sy})')
                    return False
                d, u = (_M_LEFTDOWN, _M_LEFTUP) if button == 'left' else (_M_RIGHTDOWN, _M_RIGHTUP)
                try:
                    _mouse_event(d | absf, nx, ny)
                    time.sleep(down_ms)
                finally:
                    _mouse_event(u | absf, nx, ny)
                return True
        return self._do(_fn, wait)
    def mouse_hold(self, x, y, button='right', wait=False):
        def _fn():
            hwnd = self._foreground()
            if not hwnd:
                return False
            else:
                sx, sy = self._to_screen(hwnd, x, y)
                nx, ny = _abs_xy(sx, sy)
                absf = _M_ABSOLUTE | _M_VIRTUALDESK
                if not self._land(sx, sy):
                    self._log(f'홀드 스킵 — 커서 안착 실패 ({sx},{sy})')
                    return False
                _mouse_event(_M_MOVE | absf, nx, ny)
                time.sleep(0.012)
                _mouse_event((_M_LEFTDOWN if button == 'left' else _M_RIGHTDOWN) | absf, nx, ny)
                self._mouse_held = button
                return True
        return self._do(_fn, wait)
    def mouse_release(self, button=None, wait=False):
        def _fn():
            b = button or self._mouse_held
            if b:
                _mouse_event(_M_LEFTUP if b == 'left' else _M_RIGHTUP)
                self._mouse_held = None
            return True
        return self._do(_fn, wait)
    def press(self, key, hold=0.05, wait=False):
        def _fn():
            self._touch_pin()
            if not self._foreground():
                return False
            else:
                self._press_key(key, hold)
                return True
        return self._do(_fn, wait)
    def key_hold(self, key, wait=False):
        def _fn():
            self._touch_pin()
            if not self._foreground():
                return False
            else:
                n = key.lower()
                flags = _SCANCODE | (_EXTENDED if n in _EXT_KEYS else 0)
                _key_event(user32.MapVirtualKeyW(VK[n], 0), flags)
                return True
        return self._do(_fn, wait)
    def key_release(self, key, wait=False):
        def _fn():
            n = key.lower()
            flags = _SCANCODE | (_EXTENDED if n in _EXT_KEYS else 0) | _KEYUP
            _key_event(user32.MapVirtualKeyW(VK[n], 0), flags)
            return True
        return self._do(_fn, wait)
    def combo(self, *keys, hold=0.05, wait=False):
        def _fn():
            if not self._foreground():
                return False
            else:
                mods = [k.lower() for k in keys[:(-1)]]
                for m in mods:
                    _key_event(user32.MapVirtualKeyW(VK[m], 0), _SCANCODE)
                time.sleep(0.02)
                self._press_key(keys[(-1)], hold)
                for m in reversed(mods):
                    _key_event(user32.MapVirtualKeyW(VK[m], 0), _SCANCODE | _KEYUP)
                return True
        return self._do(_fn, wait)
    def keys_together(self, *keys, hold=0.05, wait=False):
        def _fn():
            if not self._foreground():
                return False
            else:
                names = [k.lower() for k in keys]
                for n in names:
                    flags = _SCANCODE | (_EXTENDED if n in _EXT_KEYS else 0)
                    _key_event(user32.MapVirtualKeyW(VK[n], 0), flags)
                time.sleep(hold)
                for n in reversed(names):
                    flags = _SCANCODE | (_EXTENDED if n in _EXT_KEYS else 0)
                    _key_event(user32.MapVirtualKeyW(VK[n], 0), flags | _KEYUP)
                return True
        return self._do(_fn, wait)
    def press_vk(self, vk, shift=False, wait=False):
        def _fn():
            if not self._foreground():
                return False
            else:
                if shift:
                    _key_event(user32.MapVirtualKeyW(VK['shift'], 0), _SCANCODE)
                _vk_event(int(vk), 0)
                time.sleep(0.03)
                _vk_event(int(vk), _KEYUP)
                if shift:
                    _key_event(user32.MapVirtualKeyW(VK['shift'], 0), _SCANCODE | _KEYUP)
                return True
        return self._do(_fn, wait)
    def toggle_hangul(self, wait=False):
        def _fn():
            if not self._foreground():
                return False
            else:
                _vk_event(VK_HANGUL, 0)
                time.sleep(0.03)
                _vk_event(VK_HANGUL, _KEYUP)
                return True
        return self._do(_fn, wait)
    def clear_pending(self):
        try:
            while True:
                fn, done, box = self._q.get_nowait()
                box[0] = False
                if done is not None:
                    done.set()
        except queue.Empty:
            return None
    def release_all(self):
        self._pin = None
        self.clear_pending()
        if self._mouse_held:
            self.mouse_release(wait=True)
===== FILE: ./second_pw.py (    7628 chars) =====
"""2nd-password (2차 비밀번호) keypad solver.

Why this exists (manager, 2026-11-18): 거상's secondary-password screen is a
real anti-bot control, not a cosmetic quirk -- the digit buttons sit at 12
FIXED screen positions, but which digit is drawn at which position is
reshuffled every time the screen appears. 20 wrong entries destroy the
account's secondary-password data (per the in-game warning text), so this
module is built to refuse rather than guess whenever it isn't sure.

How it works: 12 button positions are fixed (measured live, see
KEYPAD_POS). For each position we crop a small patch and match it against
the 12 known digit templates (0-9, M=지우기/clear, X=취소/cancel) saved in
assets/pin_templates/. If every position matches a template with high
confidence AND all 12 labels are distinct (no duplicate reads, which would
mean a template is miscalibrated), we trust the mapping; otherwise we log
exactly what's ambiguous and do nothing.

The actual PIN digits never pass through here as a hardcoded value -- they
come from config.second_password(), which the user types directly into the
macro's own local settings (see config.py / prism_settings.json on this PC),
never through chat or any network call.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import time

try:
    import cv2
    import numpy as np
except Exception:
    cv2 = None
    np = None

ASSET_DIR = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), 'assets', 'pin_templates')

# The whole dialog can spawn at a DIFFERENT screen position each time (seen
# live 2026-11-18: one instance top-left at ~(17,288), another at ~(49,108)
# -- a pure translation, box width/height and every internal offset were
# pixel-identical between the two, so this is NOT a rescale, just a moving
# anchor). Everything below is defined RELATIVE to the password-display
# box's own top-left corner, which we locate fresh every time via
# find_anchor() -- no fixed absolute coordinate is trusted.
#
# Offsets measured 2026-11-18 from two real captures (both agreed exactly):
KEY_OFFSETS = {
    'r0c0': (48, 47),  'r0c1': (91, 47),  'r0c2': (134, 47),
    'r1c0': (48, 84),  'r1c1': (91, 84),  'r1c2': (134, 84),
    'r2c0': (48, 120), 'r2c1': (91, 120), 'r2c2': (134, 120),
    'r3c0': (48, 156), 'r3c1': (91, 156), 'r3c2': (134, 156),
}
CONFIRM_OFFSET = (62, 187)   # 확인
CANCEL_OFFSET = (134, 187)   # 취소
HALF_W, HALF_H = 16, 12
SEARCH_MARGIN = 3  # small tolerance only; the anchor itself should now be precise
MATCH_THRESHOLD = 0.85  # normalized correlation; below this we refuse, not guess

# Password box detection: template-matched against the box's own appearance
# (far more robust than a 1D darkness threshold, which was noisy by a few
# px between two real captures and threw off every offset downstream).
ANCHOR_TEMPLATE_PATH = os.path.join(ASSET_DIR, 'anchor_box.png')
ANCHOR_SEARCH_Y_MAX = 650
ANCHOR_MATCH_THRESHOLD = 0.9


def find_anchor(frame, log=print):
    """Returns (x, y) of the password box's top-left corner via template
    match, or None if not found confidently (ambiguous -> refuse)."""
    if cv2 is None or np is None:
        return None
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY) if frame.ndim == 3 else frame
    tmpl = cv2.imread(ANCHOR_TEMPLATE_PATH, cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
    if tmpl is None:
        log('[2차비번] anchor_box.png 템플릿 없음 — 중단')
        return None
    search = gray[:ANCHOR_SEARCH_Y_MAX, :300]
    res = cv2.matchTemplate(search, tmpl, cv2.TM_CCOEFF_NORMED)
    _, max_val, _, max_loc = cv2.minMaxLoc(res)
    if max_val < ANCHOR_MATCH_THRESHOLD:
        log(f'[2차비번] 비밀번호 입력창 위치를 찾지 못함 (일치율 {max_val:.2f}) — 중단')
        return None
    return max_loc  # (x, y) of top-left corner


def _load_templates() -> dict:
    out = {}
    if cv2 is None:
        return out
    for fname in os.listdir(ASSET_DIR):
        if fname.startswith('key_') and fname.endswith('.png'):
            label = fname[len('key_'):-len('.png')]
            img = cv2.imread(os.path.join(ASSET_DIR, fname), cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
            if img is not None:
                out[label] = img
    return out


def _best_match_windowed(gray, x, y, templates: dict):
    """Crops a search window larger than the template around (x,y) and lets
    matchTemplate find the best-aligned position within it, instead of
    demanding the template land pixel-perfectly at (x,y). Returns
    (label, score) for the best label across all shift positions."""
    win = gray[y - HALF_H - SEARCH_MARGIN:y + HALF_H + SEARCH_MARGIN,
               x - HALF_W - SEARCH_MARGIN:x + HALF_W + SEARCH_MARGIN]
    best_label, best_score = None, -1.0
    for label, tmpl in templates.items():
        th, tw = tmpl.shape
        if win.shape[0] < th or win.shape[1] < tw:
            continue
        res = cv2.matchTemplate(win, tmpl, cv2.TM_CCOEFF_NORMED)
        score = float(res.max())
        if score > best_score:
            best_label, best_score = label, score
    return best_label, best_score


def read_keypad(frame, log=print) -> dict | None:
    """Returns {label: (x, y)} for all 12 keys if the anchor was found and
    every position matched confidently and uniquely, else None (and logs
    exactly why)."""
    if cv2 is None or np is None:
        log('[2차비번] cv2/numpy 없음 — 이 PC에서 실행해야 함')
        return None
    anchor = find_anchor(frame, log)
    if anchor is None:
        return None
    ax, ay = anchor
    templates = _load_templates()
    if len(templates) < 12:
        log(f'[2차비번] 템플릿 {len(templates)}/12개만 로드됨 — assets/pin_templates 확인')
        return None
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY) if frame.ndim == 3 else frame
    mapping = {}
    seen_labels = {}
    for slot, (dx, dy) in KEY_OFFSETS.items():
        x, y = ax + dx, ay + dy
        label, score = _best_match_windowed(gray, x, y, templates)
        if label is None or score < MATCH_THRESHOLD:
            log(f'[2차비번] {slot} 위치 글자 인식 실패 (최고 일치율 {score:.2f}) — 중단')
            return None
        if label in seen_labels:
            log(f'[2차비번] "{label}"가 {seen_labels[label]}와 {slot} 양쪽에서 인식됨 — '
                '템플릿이 서로 구별되지 않음, 중단 (추측하지 않음)')
            return None
        seen_labels[label] = slot
        mapping[label] = (x, y)
    digits_found = sorted(k for k in mapping if k.isdigit())
    if digits_found != [str(d) for d in range(10)]:
        log(f'[2차비번] 숫자 0-9가 모두 인식되지 않음 (인식됨: {digits_found}) — 중단')
        return None
    mapping['_confirm'] = (ax + CONFIRM_OFFSET[0], ay + CONFIRM_OFFSET[1])
    mapping['_cancel'] = (ax + CANCEL_OFFSET[0], ay + CANCEL_OFFSET[1])
    return mapping


def enter_second_password(hands, frame, pin: str, log=print) -> bool:
    """pin comes from config.second_password() -- plain digits, user-entered
    locally. Never logs the pin itself."""
    if not pin or not pin.isdigit():
        log('[2차비번] 설정에 2차 비밀번호가 등록되어 있지 않음 — 직접 입력하세요')
        return False
    mapping = read_keypad(frame, log)
    if mapping is None:
        return False
    log(f'[2차비번] 키패드 인식 완료 ({len(pin)}자리 입력 시작)')
    for ch in pin:
        x, y = mapping[ch]
        hands.click(x, y)
        time.sleep(0.25)
    hands.click(*mapping['_confirm'])
    log('[2차비번] 확인 클릭 완료')
    return True

===== FILE: ./dev/tools/jochul_replay.py (    5019 chars) =====
"""Replay recorded 조철 rounds (debug/jochul_rounds/round_*/ : NNNNN.jpg + meta.json) through an offline tracker and
score the answer against the labels the macro stored (start_slot / reveal_slot / first / answer).

    python dev/tools/jochul_replay.py path/to/jochul_rounds            (all rounds)
    python dev/tools/jochul_replay.py path/to/jochul_rounds/round_X     (one round)

Pipeline: background from the scatter phase (median) -> foreground blobs (any colour) -> velocity-predicted
Hungarian matching with coasting while blobs merge -> where the track that started on the marked slot ends.
Needs numpy, opencv, scipy. Pure analysis: never touches the game.
"""
import json
import os
import sys

import cv2
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import linear_sum_assignment

SLOT_X = np.array([65, 145, 225, 305, 385], float)      # board-crop coordinates of the 5 slots
ROW_Y = 140.0


def load_round(d):
    meta = json.load(open(os.path.join(d, 'meta.json'), encoding='utf-8'))
    frames = [cv2.imread(os.path.join(d, '%05d.jpg' % i)) for i in range(meta['frames'])]
    return meta, np.array(meta['times'], float), frames


def blobs(mask, amin=350, amax=5200):
    m = cv2.morphologyEx(mask, cv2.MORPH_OPEN, np.ones((3, 3), np.uint8))
    m = cv2.morphologyEx(m, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, np.ones((7, 7), np.uint8))
    n, _, st, cen = cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(m, 8)
    return [np.array(cen[i], float) for i in range(1, n) if amin <= st[i, 4] <= amax and st[i, 2] < 110 and st[i, 3] < 110]


def foreground(frames, times, bg_from=0.35, bg_to=0.80, thr=34):
    T = times[-1]
    idx = [i for i, t in enumerate(times) if bg_from * T <= t <= bg_to * T] or list(range(len(frames)))
    med = np.median(np.stack([frames[i] for i in idx]), axis=0).astype(np.int16)
    return [(np.abs(f.astype(np.int16) - med).max(axis=2) > thr).astype(np.uint8) * 255 for f in frames]


def in_row(pts):
    return len(pts) >= 5 and np.std([p[1] for p in pts]) < 14 and abs(np.median([p[1] for p in pts]) - ROW_Y) < 40


def track(times, dets, start_slot, gate=150.0, decay=0.8):
    """dets: per frame list of (x, y). Returns (final slot of the tracked character, info)."""
    s = next((i for i, d in enumerate(dets) if in_row(d)), None)
    e = next((i for i in range(len(dets) - 1, -1, -1) if in_row(dets[i])), None)
    if s is None or e is None or e <= s:
        return None, 'no start/end row found'
    pos = np.array(sorted(dets[s], key=lambda p: p[0])[:5] if len(dets[s]) == 5 else sorted(dets[s], key=lambda p: abs(p[1] - ROW_Y))[:5])
    pos = pos[np.argsort(pos[:, 0])]
    vel = np.zeros((5, 2))
    last = times[s]
    coast = 0
    for i in range(s + 1, e + 1):
        dt = max(1e-3, times[i] - last)
        last = times[i]
        pred = pos + vel * min(dt, 0.25)
        D = np.array(dets[i]).reshape(-1, 2)
        if len(D):
            C = np.linalg.norm(pred[:, None, :] - D[None, :, :], axis=2)
            C = np.where(C > gate, 1e4, C)
            r, c = linear_sum_assignment(C)
            matched = set()
            for a, b in zip(r, c):
                if C[a, b] < 1e4:
                    vel[a] = 0.35 * vel[a] + 0.65 * np.clip((D[b] - pos[a]) / dt, -400, 400)
                    pos[a] = D[b]
                    matched.add(a)
            for a in range(5):
                if a not in matched:
                    pos[a] = pred[a]
                    vel[a] *= decay
                    coast += 1
        else:
            pos = pred
            vel *= decay
            coast += 5
    fin = np.array(sorted(dets[e], key=lambda p: abs(p[1] - ROW_Y))[:5])
    fin = fin[np.argsort(fin[:, 0])]
    C = np.linalg.norm(pos[:, None, :] - fin[None, :, :], axis=2)
    r, c = linear_sum_assignment(C)
    m = {int(a): int(b) + 1 for a, b in zip(r, c)}
    return m.get(start_slot - 1), 'ok (coasted %d track-frames)' % coast


def solve_round(d):
    meta, times, frames = load_round(d)
    masks = foreground(frames, times)
    dets = [blobs(m) for m in masks]
    start = meta.get('start_slot') or meta.get('first')
    ans, info = track(times, dets, int(start)) if start else (None, 'no start label')
    return meta, ans, info


def main(path):
    dirs = [path] if os.path.exists(os.path.join(path, 'meta.json')) else sorted(os.path.join(path, x) for x in os.listdir(path) if x.startswith('round_'))
    ok = tot = 0
    for d in dirs:
        meta, ans, info = solve_round(d)
        truth = meta.get('reveal_slot') or meta.get('truth')
        mark = ''
        if truth:
            tot += 1
            ok += (ans == truth)
            mark = 'OK' if ans == truth else 'WRONG'
        print('%s: %s fps, start=%s, macro answered=%s, tracker=%s, reveal=%s %s [%s]' % (
            os.path.basename(d), meta.get('effective_fps'), meta.get('start_slot') or meta.get('first'), meta.get('answer'), ans, truth, mark, info))
    if tot:
        print('tracker %d/%d against reveal labels' % (ok, tot))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else '.')

"""Runs the real Brain loop on a Mac/Linux with fake capture, hands and detector (Windows modules stubbed).
   python dev/mock/run_dummy_scenarios.py"""
import sys, time, threading, types
import os
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, HERE); sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(HERE)))
import mac_stub
import numpy as np, cv2
import brain, config, detector, vision
base = cv2.imread(os.path.join(HERE, 'main_scene.png'))
assert base is not None and base.shape[:2] == (768, 1024)

class FakeEngine:
    hwnd = 1; _frame_id = 0
    def __init__(self): self.mode = 'main'
    def snapshot(self):
        FakeEngine._frame_id += 1
        return base.copy()
    def wait_new(self, last, timeout=0.03): time.sleep(0.016); return self._frame_id
class FakeHands:
    guard_enabled = False
    def __init__(self): self.log = []
    def click(self, x, y, button='left', **k): self.log.append(('click', time.time(), x, y, button)); return True
    def press(self, *a, **k): self.log.append(('press', time.time(), a)); return True
    def combo(self, *a, **k): self.log.append(('combo', time.time(), a)); return True
    def move(self, *a, **k): return True
    def __getattr__(self, n): return lambda *a, **k: True
class FakeDet:
    ready = True
    def __init__(self, pts): self.pts = pts
    def detect(self, img, **k): return np.array(self.pts, np.float32).reshape(-1, 2)

class FakeVision:
    """Scene always 'main', no triggers: the field before the click is answered."""
    def snapshot(self): return {'scene': 'main', 'triggers': []}
    def note(self, *a, **k): pass
    def reset(self): pass

def make(pts):
    b = brain.Brain.__new__(brain.Brain)
    brain.Brain._build_detectors = lambda self: None
    brain.Brain._preload_async = lambda self: None
    return b

def run_scenario(title, seconds, pts, scene_fn=None):
    def _bd(self):
        self.fire_roi = config.mob_fire_roi(self.monster); self.fire_rect = config.mob_fire_rect(self.monster)
        self.trust_avoid = config.mob_trust_avoid(self.monster); self.ignore_dummy = config.mob_ignore_dummy(self.monster)
        self._stop_triggers = brain.STOP_TRIGGERS; self.detector = None; self.box_click = False
    brain.Brain._build_detectors = _bd
    brain.Brain._preload_async = lambda self: None
    notes = []
    eng, hands, vis = FakeEngine(), FakeHands(), FakeVision()
    if scene_fn: vis.snapshot = scene_fn
    vis.note = lambda *a, **k: notes.append((time.time(), a[-1]))
    b = brain.Brain(eng, vis, hands, note=lambda m: notes.append((time.time(), m)))
    b.detector = FakeDet(pts)
    b.detector.ready = True
    b._save_count = lambda: None
    b._grab_main = lambda normalize=True: base.copy()
    b.start()
    time.sleep(seconds)
    alive = b.running
    b.stop()
    return b, hands, notes, alive

"""Runs Brain._handle_jochul against fake capture/hands/solver (Windows modules stubbed).
   python dev/mock/run_jochul_flow.py"""
import os, sys, time, types
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import numpy as np
import mock_loop as m
import brain, jochul, config

def make(tile_after, marker_slot=None, track_answer=4):
    notes, t0 = [], time.time()
    hands = m.FakeHands()
    parks = []
    hands.move_nofg = lambda *a, **k: parks.append(a) or True
    eng = m.FakeEngine(); eng.hwnd = 1
    brain.find_window = lambda: 1
    b = brain.Brain(eng, m.FakeVision(), hands, note=lambda s: notes.append(s))
    b.running = True
    solved = {}
    class Solver:
        ready = True
        def solve_tracked(self, frames, times, first):
            solved.update(n=len(frames), first=first)
            return track_answer, 'ok (mock tracker)'
        def _detect_missing(self, frames):
            return [None] * len(frames), [None] * len(frames)
    b.jochul_solver = Solver()
    # the live code now captures with jochul.record_all (wgc/capture internals, not brain.grab_hwnd),
    # so it is stubbed directly here with a plausible native-fps frame/time series
    n_fake = 300
    def _fake_record_all(hwnd, duration=jochul.DURATION_S, stop=None):
        time.sleep(1.2)  # gives the parallel RoundRecorder a realistic window to gather frames
        return [np.zeros((4, 4, 3), np.uint8)] * n_fake, [round(i * duration / n_fake, 4) for i in range(n_fake)]
    jochul.record_all = _fake_record_all
    def cap():
        f = np.full((768, 1024, 3), (40, 100, 40), np.uint8)    # plain grass: no accidental pixel patterns
        if marker_slot and int((time.time() - t0) * 25) % 5 < 2:      # medallion bobs: visible ~40% of frames
            import cv2
            cx = jochul.CLICK_X[marker_slot] - jochul.REGION[0]
            cv2.circle(f, (jochul.REGION[0] + cx, jochul.REGION[1] + 105), 9, (40, 190, 235), -1)   # BGR gold
        if tile_after < 900 and time.time() - t0 >= tile_after:
            x, y = 130, 100          # inside REGION; pattern the real first tile has
            ox, oy = jochul.REGION[0], jochul.REGION[1]
            for (dx, dy), c in zip(((0, 0), (1, 0), (0, 1), (1, 1)), jochul._PAT1):
                f[oy + y + dy, ox + x + dx] = c
        return f
    b._cap = cap
    brain.grab_hwnd = lambda h, normalize=True: cap()    # every grab (solver + recorder) goes through the fake
    return b, hands, notes, parks, solved

check('tile visible: first tile found', any('첫 마패: 2번' in n for n in notes), notes[:4])

    check('frame files readable', len([f for f in os.listdir(os.path.join(rd, dirs[0])) if f.endswith('.jpg')]) == meta['frames'])

# swap-decoder fallback this used to click measured 43% real-world accuracy on 7 logged failures -

"""Runs Brain._handle_jamun (자동방지문자) against fake capture/hands/reader and checks the typed keys, the clicks and
the evidence record.   python dev/mock/run_jamun_flow.py"""
import json, os, sys, tempfile, time

def check(name, cond, extra=''):
    print(('PASS ' if cond else 'FAIL ') + name + ('' if cond else ' -- ' + str(extra)))
    if not cond: fails.append(name)

def make(reads, wrong_dialog=False):
    eng = m.FakeEngine(); eng.hwnd = 0
    brain.find_window = lambda: 0
    hands = m.FakeHands()
    b = brain.Brain(eng, m.FakeVision(), hands, note=lambda s: None)
    b.running = True
    class Reader:
        ready = True; last_step_conf = 0.0
        def __init__(self): self.i = 0
        def read(self, crop):
            t, c = reads[min(self.i, len(reads) - 1)]; self.i += 1; self.last_step_conf = c; return t, c
    b.text_reader = Reader()
    def frame():
        f = np.full((768, 1024, 3), (210, 215, 220), np.uint8)
        if wrong_dialog:
            f[426, 589] = (230, 230, 230); f[426, 590] = (230, 230, 230)
        return f
    b._cap = frame; b._cap_native = frame
    b._stop_now = lambda why='': setattr(b, 'running', False) or b.__dict__.setdefault('stops', []).append(why)
    return b, hands
