"""Click-path self-test on its own window: delivery, landing error (px), latency. Does not touch the game.
Usage: .venv\\Scripts\\python dev\\selftest_click.py
"""
import sys, time, threading, ctypes, ctypes.wintypes as wt
import os
root_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, root_dir)
import tkinter as tk
import win32gui, capture
import input as ginput

# Own test window: verifies SendInput delivery + position + hold without touching the game.
win = tk.Tk(); win.geometry('1024x768+0+0'); win.title('Gersang')  # title irrelevant: we pass hwnd_get
win.attributes('-topmost', True)
ev = []
win.bind('<ButtonPress-3>', lambda e: ev.append(('d', time.perf_counter(), e.x_root, e.y_root)))
win.bind('<ButtonRelease-3>', lambda e: ev.append(('u', time.perf_counter(), e.x_root, e.y_root)))
win.update()
hwnd = int(win.frame(), 16)
cw = win32gui.GetClientRect(hwnd)
logs=[]
hands = ginput.Hands(hwnd_get=lambda: hwnd, log=logs.append)
hands.FG_SETTLE = 0.0
hands._foreground = lambda: hwnd   # harness: measure the click path, not window focus
pt = wt.POINT()
n_ok = 0; durs = []; errs = []
for i in range(40):
    x = 100 + (i * 83) % 800; y = 100 + (i * 61) % 560
    exp = hands._to_screen(hwnd, x, y)
    ev.clear()
    win.lift(); win.update()
    a = time.perf_counter()
    r = hands.click(x, y, 'right', wait=True)
    b = time.perf_counter()
    for _ in range(20):
        win.update(); time.sleep(0.005)
    d = [e for e in ev if e[0] == 'd']; u = [e for e in ev if e[0] == 'u']
    if not (r and d and u): print('MISS i=%d r=%s d=%d u=%d exp=%s' % (i, r, len(d), len(u), exp))
    if r and d and u:
        n_ok += 1
        errs.append(max(abs(d[0][2]-exp[0]), abs(d[0][3]-exp[1])))
        durs.append((b-a)*1000)
durs.sort()
print('clicks_delivered %d/40  max_pos_err_px %s' % (n_ok, max(errs) if errs else None))
print('click_ms p50 %.1f p90 %.1f max %.1f' % (durs[len(durs)//2], durs[int(len(durs)*.9)], durs[-1]))
print('logs', sorted(set(logs))[:6])
win.destroy()

===== FILE: ./dev/test_jochul_decode.py (    2289 chars) =====
"""조철 swap-sequence decoder: regression test against simulated shuffles.

The decompiled source counted an undecided block (None) as a rule violation, which the original
bytecode does not. With dropped detections that picked the wrong timing phase and produced wrong
swaps. These cases failed before the fix.   python dev/test_jochul_decode.py
"""
import os
import random
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
import jochul  # noqa: E402


def sim(rnd, noise, drop, period=13.0, n_ops=15):
    ops, last = [], None
    for _ in range(n_ops):
        while True:
            a, b = sorted(rnd.sample(range(1, 6), 2))
            if last is None or not ({a, b} & set(last)):
                break
        ops.append((a, b))
        last = (a, b)
    n = int(period * n_ops) + 4
    pf = [None] * n
    for i, (a, b) in enumerate(ops):
        for f in range(int(round(period * i + 1)), min(n, int(round(period * (i + 1))))):
            u = rnd.random()
            if u < drop:
                continue
            pf[f] = tuple(sorted(rnd.sample(range(1, 6), 2))) if u < drop + noise else (a, b)
    return ops, pf


fails = []


def check(name, cond, extra=''):
    print(('PASS ' if cond else 'FAIL ') + name + ('' if cond else ' -- ' + str(extra)))
    if not cond:
        fails.append(name)


rnd = random.Random(1)
cases = []
for t in range(500):
    noise, drop = rnd.choice([0, .05, .15]), rnd.choice([0, .2, .4])
    cases.append((t, noise, drop) + sim(rnd, noise, drop))
byid = {c[0]: c for c in cases}
for t in (30, 473):   # decoded wrongly by the decompiled version
    _, _, _, ops, pf = byid[t]
    check('case %d decodes to the true swaps' % t, jochul.periodic_decode(pf) == ops)

ok = sum(jochul.periodic_decode(pf) == ops for _, _, _, ops, pf in cases)
check('exact decode >= 99%% of 500 simulated shuffles (%d)' % ok, ok >= 495, ok)
ok_hard = [jochul.periodic_decode(pf) == ops for _, n, d, ops, pf in cases if d >= .4 or n >= .15]
check('hard cases (40%% frames lost / 15%% wrong) >= 98%% (%d/%d)' % (sum(ok_hard), len(ok_hard)), sum(ok_hard) >= 0.98 * len(ok_hard))
check('empty input -> []', jochul.periodic_decode([]) == [])
print('FAILED: %s' % fails if fails else 'all passed')
sys.exit(1 if fails else 0)

===== FILE: ./dev/test_jochul_tracker.py (    6483 chars) =====
"""jochul.track_centroids / solve_tracked: regression test against a REAL, user-confirmed-correct
client round (dev/fixtures/jochul_round1), plus synthetic edge cases for _assign_optimal.
Needs ultralytics+torch (the real model), so it's skipped (not failed) where those aren't installed.
   python dev/test_jochul_tracker.py"""
import json
import os
import sys

import numpy as np
from collections import Counter

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
import jochul

fails = []


def check(name, cond, extra=''):
    print(('PASS ' if cond else 'FAIL ') + name + ('' if cond else ' -- ' + str(extra)))
    if not cond:
        fails.append(name)


# --- _assign_optimal: must match brute-force-obvious answers, including RECTANGULAR matrices
# (fewer detections than tracked points, or more) ---
sq = np.array([[1.0, 9, 9], [9, 1.0, 9], [9, 9, 1.0]])
r, c = jochul._assign_optimal(sq)
check('square matrix: picks the diagonal (cheapest)', list(zip(r.tolist(), c.tolist())) == [(0, 0), (1, 1), (2, 2)], (r, c))

fewer_cols = np.array([[0.0, 9, 9], [9, 0.0, 9], [9, 9, 9], [9, 9, 9]])  # 4 rows, 3 cols
r, c = jochul._assign_optimal(fewer_cols)
check('fewer detections than tracked points: assigns exactly 3 (not 4)', len(r) == 3 and len(c) == 3, (r, c))
check('fewer-cols case still finds the two free cheap matches', set(zip(r.tolist(), c.tolist())) >= {(0, 0), (1, 1)}, (r, c))

fewer_rows = np.array([[0.0, 9, 9, 9], [9, 0.0, 9, 9], [9, 9, 9, 9]])  # 3 rows, 4 cols
r, c = jochul._assign_optimal(fewer_rows)
check('more detections than tracked points: assigns exactly 3', len(r) == 3 and len(c) == 3, (r, c))

# --- track_centroids: synthetic case, 5 points where slot 1 and slot 2 gradually swap places
# (real characters RUN between positions over several frames; a one-frame teleport is not a fair
# test of a velocity tracker - two positions that are simply occupied, with no motion in between,
# are genuinely indistinguishable from "nobody moved", which is correct, not a bug) ---
start_row = [(65, 140), (145, 140), (225, 140), (305, 140), (385, 140)]
end_row = [(145, 140), (65, 140), (225, 140), (305, 140), (385, 140)]  # 1 and 2 swapped
times = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 12)
dets = []
for t in times:
    f = min(1.0, t / 0.8)
    dets.append([(65 + f * 80, 140), (145 - f * 80, 140), (225, 140), (305, 140), (385, 140)])
ans, info = jochul.track_centroids(times, dets, start_slot=1)
check('synthetic swap (gradual motion): the slot-1 character is tracked to its new slot (2)', ans == 2, (ans, info))

# a start/end row that's never found (always scattered) returns None, not a crash or a guess
scattered = [[(100, 300)], [(120, 310)]]
ans, info = jochul.track_centroids(np.array([0.0, 0.5]), scattered, start_slot=1)
check('no usable row -> None, not a silent wrong guess', ans is None, (ans, info))

# --- ensemble confidence policy: costly wrong clicks need 4/5, not a bare 3/5 majority ---
policy_solver = jochul.JochulSolver('unused')
policy_solver.detect_centroids = lambda frames, conf=0.20: [[]]
original_track = jochul.track_centroids
try:
    votes = iter([(5, 'mock')] * 3 + [(1, 'mock')] * 2)
    jochul.track_centroids = lambda *a, **k: next(votes)
    ans, info = policy_solver.solve_tracked([None], [0.0], first_slot=2)
    check('3/5 ensemble split -> uncertain, no weak-majority click', ans is None and '{5: 3, 1: 2}' in info, str((ans, info)))

    votes = iter([(5, 'mock')] * 4 + [(1, 'mock')])
    jochul.track_centroids = lambda *a, **k: next(votes)
    ans, info = policy_solver.solve_tracked([None], [0.0], first_slot=2)
    check('4/5 ensemble consensus -> keep answer', ans == 5 and '{5: 4, 1: 1}' in info, str((ans, info)))
finally:
    jochul.track_centroids = original_track
    del policy_solver.detect_centroids

# --- real labelled round: the one actual client capture confirmed correct by the user ---
try:
    import torch  # noqa: F401
    from ultralytics import YOLO  # noqa: F401
    HAVE_MODEL = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_MODEL = False

if not HAVE_MODEL:
    print('SKIP real-round test (torch/ultralytics not installed in this interpreter)')
else:
    import cv2
    fdir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), 'fixtures', 'jochul_round1')
    meta = json.load(open(os.path.join(fdir, 'meta.json'), encoding='utf-8'))
    frames = [cv2.imread(os.path.join(fdir, '%05d.jpg' % i)) for i in range(meta['frames'])]
    rtimes = np.array(meta['times'])
    model_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))),
                               'assets', 'models', 'jochul', 'jochul.pt')
    solver = jochul.JochulSolver(model_path)
    solver.preload()
    ans, info = solver.solve_tracked(frames, rtimes, meta['first'])
    check('real client round: tracker reaches the user-confirmed correct slot',
          ans == meta['truth'], (ans, meta['truth'], info))

    t0 = __import__('time').perf_counter()
    ens_ans, ens_info = solver.solve_tracked(frames, rtimes, meta['first'])
    ens_dt = __import__('time').perf_counter() - t0
    check('ensemble vote also reaches the correct slot, well inside the 10 s answer timer',
          ens_ans == meta['truth'] and ens_dt < 9.0, (ens_ans, ens_dt, ens_info))

    # a case with no usable row at all in ANY setting must come back as "uncertain" (None), not a guess
    no_data = [[] for _ in frames]
    solver.detect_centroids = lambda frames, conf=0.20: no_data
    ans2, info2 = solver.solve_tracked(frames, rtimes, meta['first'])
    check('no usable detections anywhere -> uncertain (None), not a silent guess', ans2 is None, (ans2, info2))
    # the swap-model decoders are kept only as a secondary signal - documenting here that they
    # got this SAME round wrong, so a future change must not quietly swap back to trusting them alone
    per_std, per_sens = solver._detect_missing(frames)
    old_ops = jochul.periodic_decode(jochul._resample_uniform(per_std, rtimes.tolist()),
                                      per_frame_sens=jochul._resample_uniform(per_sens, rtimes.tolist()))
    old_order = jochul.apply_swaps(old_ops)
    old_ans = old_order.index(meta['first']) + 1 if meta['first'] in old_order else None
    print('(for reference) periodic_decode on this same round answers %s (wrong) - not used for the click' % old_ans)

print('FAILED: %s' % fails if fails else 'all passed')
sys.exit(1 if fails else 0)

===== FILE: ./dev/preview_ui.py (    6996 chars) =====
"""GATO desktop UI — macOS DESIGN PREVIEW ONLY.

This is NOT the production app. prism.py's real Api class talks to the
actual Windows game process (win32gui, ctypes.windll, real combat state) and
cannot run on macOS at all. This file exists solely so Jungjun can open the
exact same ui/index.html on his Mac and see/edit the design without the
Windows PC, by backing every pywebview.api.* call the frontend makes with a
small in-memory stub that returns clean, empty/neutral starting state (not
"realistic fake numbers" -- the whole point of this tool is telling real
data apart from decoration, so it starts at zero/empty everywhere a real
session would, and a few buttons below let Jungjun fill in sample state by
hand to see how populated cards actually look).

Run:
    pip3 install pywebview
    python3 dev/preview_ui.py

Never wire this file's Api into run_gato.py / prism.py. Never point it at
assets/pin_templates or anything game-account-related.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import time

import webview

UI_DIR = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))), "ui")


class PreviewApi:
    """Stands in for prism.py's real Api. Every method mirrors the real
    one's name/shape (see ui/index.html's pywebview.api.* calls) but returns
    local, clearly-synthetic preview state -- nothing here ever touches the
    real game, a real account, or real settings files."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.running = False
        self.battles = 0
        self.sched_active = False
        self.sched = {"cycle": 0, "cycles": 3, "battles": 0, "target": 10, "resting": False, "rest_left": 0}
        self.monster = "건달바의혼"
        self.records = []  # user can add via the "샘플 데이터" preview button in devtools console
        self.kill = []
        self.cfg = {
            "monsters": {
                self.monster: {
                    "squad_counts": [2, 2, 2, 2, 2, 2],
                    "center_move_delay": 1.0,
                    "lead_px": 80,
                    "summon_mode": "none",
                    "multi_retreat": False,
                    "noejeonju": False,
                    "attack_keys": ["3", "4", "5", "6"],
                }
            },
            "categories": ["기타", "월천귀문"],
            "mob_category": {self.monster: "월천귀문"},
            "catalog": [self.monster],
            "current_monster": self.monster,
            "banja_mode": False,
            "gm_chat_msg": "",
            "discord_webhook": "",
            "discord_token": "",
            "discord_channel": "",
            "ntfy_url": "",
            "notify_whisper": True,
            "notify_normal": True,
            "notify_repeat": False,
            "sched_cycles": 3,
            "sched_battles": 10,
            "sched_rest_sec": 600,
            "stamp": "preview-0",
        }
        self._seq = 0
        self._events = []
        self._t0 = time.time()

    # ---- window chrome (no-ops on this preview window) ----
    def win_resize(self, w, h):
        return None

    def win_size_save(self, w, h):
        return None

    def win_minimize(self):
        return None

    def win_close(self):
        os._exit(0)

    # ---- status / polling ----
    def status(self):
        return {
            "running": self.running,
            "window_found": self.running,
            "window_count": 1 if self.running else 0,
            "gato": False,
            "monster": self.monster,
            "fps": 0.0,
            "width": 1024,
            "height": 768,
            "battle_count": self.battles,
            "vision": {"scene": "main" if self.running else "none", "scene_label": "메인화면" if self.running else None, "triggers": [], "whisper": False},
            "clients": [],
            "admin": True,
            "auth": {"ok": False, "label": "미리보기 — 서버 인증 없음", "notice": None},
            "schedule": {"active": self.sched_active, **self.sched},
            "update": {"available": False},
        }

    def tick(self, last_seq, last_chat_seq):
        return {"status": self.status(), "events": [], "chat": []}

    def login(self, auth_id):
        return {"ok": False, "label": "미리보기 — 로그인 비활성"}

    def logout(self):
        return {"label": ""}

    def auth_status(self):
        return {"auth_id": "", "label": "", "banja": False}

    def portrait(self, name):
        return None  # preview deliberately has no real sprite assets bundled

    # ---- config ----
    def get_config(self):
        return self.cfg

    def save_monster_cfg(self, name, patch, stamp):
        self.cfg.setdefault("monsters", {}).setdefault(name, {}).update(patch)
        self.cfg["stamp"] = f"preview-{int(time.time())}"
        return self.cfg

    def save_common(self, common, stamp):
        self.cfg.update(common)
        self.cfg["stamp"] = f"preview-{int(time.time())}"
        return self.cfg

    def set_monster(self, name):
        self.monster = name
        self.cfg["current_monster"] = name
        return None

    # ---- records / kill rank ----
    def appear_records(self):
        return self.records

    def kill_rank(self):
        return self.kill

    def reset_count(self):
        self.battles = 0
        self.records = []
        return self.status()

    # ---- start/stop/schedule (preview: just flips local flags) ----
    def start(self):
        self.running = True
        return self.status()

    def stop(self):
        self.running = False
        return self.status()

    def schedule_start(self, cycles, battles, rest_sec):
        self.sched_active = True
        self.sched = {"cycle": 1, "cycles": cycles, "battles": 0, "target": battles, "resting": False, "rest_left": rest_sec}
        return self.status()

    def schedule_stop(self):
        self.sched_active = False
        return self.status()

    # ---- notification test buttons ----
    def test_webhook(self, url):
        return "미리보기 — 실제 전송 안 함"

    def test_ntfy(self, topic):
        return "미리보기 — 실제 전송 안 함"

    def self_update(self):
        return {"ok": False, "label": "미리보기 — 업데이트 비활성"}


def main():
    api = PreviewApi()
    webview.create_window(
        "Gato_super (macOS 디자인 미리보기)",
        os.path.join(UI_DIR, "index.html"),
        js_api=api,
        width=960,
        height=660,
        min_size=(720, 560),
        background_color="#14110c",
        frameless=False,  # keep real OS chrome on the preview -- the real
                           # app's frameless titlebar + resize grips are
                           # Windows-coded (win32gui) and not meaningful to
                           # preview here; this only affects the window
                           # frame, not ui/index.html's own in-page titlebar
    )
    webview.start(debug=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

def crop_region(frame):
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = REGION
    return frame[y0:y1, x0:x1]
def _find_pattern(img, pat, tol=_PAT_TOL):
    # ***<module>._find_pattern: Failure: Different bytecode
    tl, tr, bl, br = pat
    a = img.astype(np.int16)
    m = (np.abs(a[:(-1), :(-1)] - np.asarray(tl, np.int16)) <= tol).all(2) & (np.abs(a[:(-1), 1:] - np.asarray(tr, np.int16)) <= tol).all(2) & (np.abs(a[1:, :(-1)] - np.asarray(bl, np.int16)) <= tol).all(2) & (np.abs(a[1:, 1:] - np.asarray(br, np.int16)) <= tol).all(2)
    ys, xs = np.where(m)
    return list(zip(xs.tolist(), ys.tolist()))
def find_first_tile(region_img):
    pts = _find_pattern(region_img, _PAT1) or _find_pattern(region_img, _PAT2)
    if not pts:
        return
    else:
        x = pts[0][0]
        for slot, limit in _FIRST_TILE_LIMITS:
            if x <= limit:
                return slot
        return 5
def find_marker_slot(region_img):
    """Slot (1-5) under the floating gold 마패 medallion, or None. Skin-independent: it looks for the
    medallion's colour, not for the character sprite (the pixel pattern in find_first_tile is the
    navy hair of one skin). The medallion bobs, so call it on several frames and vote (see vote_marker)."""
    import cv2
    hsv = cv2.cvtColor(np.ascontiguousarray(region_img), cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
    m = ((hsv[..., 0] >= 14) & (hsv[..., 0] <= 30) & (hsv[..., 1] >= 140) & (hsv[..., 2] >= 170)).astype(np.uint8)
    n, _, st, cen = cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(m, 8)
    best = None
    for i in range(1, n):
        a = int(st[i, 4])
        if 60 <= a <= 900 and (best is None or a > best[0]):
            best = (a, float(cen[i][0]))
    if best is None:
        return None
    xs = [CLICK_X[k] - REGION[0] for k in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5)]
    return 1 + int(np.argmin([abs(best[1] - x) for x in xs]))


def vote_marker(slots):
    """Most common slot among the frames where the medallion was seen (None if never seen)."""
    seen = [x for x in slots if x]
    return Counter(seen).most_common(1)[0][0] if seen else None


def record(capture_fn, fps=FPS, duration=DURATION_S):
    interval = 1.0 / fps
    total = int(fps * duration)
    frames, times = ([], [])
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    next_t = t0
    for _ in range(total):
        now = time.perf_counter()
        if now < next_t:
            time.sleep(next_t - now)
        next_t += interval
        f = capture_fn()
        if f is not None:
            frames.append(np.ascontiguousarray(crop_region(f)))
            times.append(time.perf_counter() - t0)
    return (frames, times)
def _resample_uniform(per_frame, times, fps=FPS):
    if not times:
        return list(per_frame)
    n_grid = max(1, int(round(times[-1] * fps)) + 1)
    grid = [None] * n_grid
    for det, t in zip(per_frame, times):
        k = int(round(t * fps))
        if 0 <= k < n_grid and (grid[k] is None or det is not None):
            grid[k] = det
    return grid
def _horizontal_groups(pts, y_tol):
    if pts.shape[0] == 0:
        return []
    order = np.argsort(pts[:, 1])
    ys = pts[order, 1]
    out, i, n = ([], 0, len(ys))
    while i < n:
        j = i
        while j < n and ys[j] - ys[i] <= y_tol:
            j += 1
        idxs = order[i:j].tolist()
        out.append((idxs, j - i, float(ys[i:j].mean())))
        i = j
    return out


def _slot_from_x(x, w):
    for slot, (lo, hi) in CLASS_RANGES_NORM.items():
        if lo * w <= x < hi * w:
                return slot
def _missing_from_pts(pts, w, h):
    if len(pts) == 0:
        return
    else:
        pts = np.asarray(pts, dtype=float)
        groups = _horizontal_groups(pts, Y_TOL_NORM * h)
        sizes = sorted((g[1] for g in groups))
        if len(groups) == 3 and sizes == [1, 2, 2]:
                return
        cand = [(g, s) for g, s, ym in groups if s == 3 or (s == 2 and 110 <= ym <= 170)]
        if not cand:
            return
        else:
            idxs, s = cand[0]
            seen = set()
            for i in idxs:
                slot = _slot_from_x(pts[i][0], w)
                if slot is None:
                    return
                else:
                    seen.add(slot)
            if len(seen) < 2:
                return
            else:
                missing = tuple(sorted(ALL_SLOTS - seen))
                if s == 3 and len(missing) == 2 or (s == 2 and len(missing) == 3):
                    return missing
                else:
                    return None
def _vote_block(items):
    pairs = [t for t in items if len(t) == 2]
    triples = [t for t in items if len(t) == 3]
    if pairs:
        top = Counter(pairs).most_common(2)
        if len(top) > 1 and top[0][1] == top[1][1]:
                return
        return top[0][0]
    else:
        if triples:
            inter = set(triples[0])
            for t in triples[1:]:
                inter &= set(t)
            if len(inter) == 2:
                return tuple(sorted(inter))
            else:
                return Counter(triples).most_common(1)[0][0]
def _resolve_pairs(votes, block_items, sens_items=None, ext_items=None):
    n = len(votes)
    resolved = [tuple(sorted(v)) if v and len(v) == 2 else None for v in votes]
    def _cooc(items, allowed):
        pair_cnt = Counter()
        cnt = Counter()
        for t in items:
            s = sorted((x for x in t if allowed is None or x in allowed))
            for x in s:
                cnt[x] += 1
            for a in range(len(s)):
                for b in range(a + 1, len(s)):
                    pair_cnt[s[a], s[b]] += 1
        return (pair_cnt, cnt)
    def _freq_top2(i, allowed):
        pair_cnt, cnt = _cooc(block_items[i], allowed)
        ext_pair = Counter() if ext_items is None else _cooc(ext_items[i], allowed)[0]
        sens_pair = Counter() if sens_items is None else _cooc(sens_items[i], allowed)[0]
        if pair_cnt:
            return sorted(pair_cnt.items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], -ext_pair[kv[0]], -sens_pair[kv[0]], kv[0]))[0][0]
        else:
            if len(cnt) < 2:
                return
            else:
                ext_cnt = Counter() if ext_items is None else _cooc(ext_items[i], allowed)[1]
                sens_cnt = Counter() if sens_items is None else _cooc(sens_items[i], allowed)[1]
                top = sorted(cnt.items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], -ext_cnt[kv[0]], -sens_cnt[kv[0]], kv[0]))[:2]
                return tuple(sorted((s for s, _ in top)))
    for _ in range(n + 2):
        changed = False
        for i in range(n):
            if resolved[i] is not None:
                continue
            else:
                forbid = set()
                if i - 1 >= 0 and resolved[i - 1]:
                        forbid |= set(resolved[i - 1])
                if i + 1 < n and resolved[i + 1]:
                        forbid |= set(resolved[i + 1])
                allowed = ALL_SLOTS - forbid
                pick = tuple(sorted(allowed)) if len(allowed) == 2 else _freq_top2(i, allowed)
                if pick and len(pick) == 2:
                        resolved[i] = pick
                        changed = True
        if not changed:
            break
    for i in range(n):
        if resolved[i] is None:
            resolved[i] = _freq_top2(i, None)
    return [r for r in resolved if r is not None]
def periodic_decode(per_frame, period=PERIOD, n_ops=N_OPS, per_frame_sens=None):
    # ***<module>.periodic_decode: Failure: Different control flow
    n = len(per_frame)
    if n == 0:
        return []
    else:
        if n_ops is None:
            n_ops = max(1, round(n / period))
        search = int(period) // 2 + 1
        cands = sorted((period + 0.25 * k for k in range((-4), 3)), key=lambda p: abs(p - period))
        best = None
        for p in cands:
            # p 후보별 최적 phi 를 찾는다
            for phi in range(-search, search + 1):
                ops, block_items, compat, bnd_none = ([], [], 0, 0)
                for i in range(1, n_ops):
                    b = int(round(phi + p * i))
                    if 0 <= b < n and per_frame[b] is None:
                                bnd_none += 1
                for i in range(n_ops):
                    lo = int(round(phi + p * i))
                    hi = int(round(phi + p * (i + 1)))
                    items = [per_frame[f] for f in range(max(0, lo), min(n, hi)) if per_frame[f] is not None]
                    v = _vote_block(items)
                    ops.append(v)
                    block_items.append(items)
                    if v and len(v) == 2:
                            ps = set(v)
                            compat += sum((1 for t in items if ps.issubset(set(t))))
                # Only two valid swaps that share a tile count as a violation (a tile cannot be in two
                # consecutive swaps). An undecided block (None) is NOT a violation: the decompiled
                # source had `not a or not b or ...` here, which the original bytecode does not do.
                viol = sum((1 for a, b in zip(ops, ops[1:]) if a and b and len(a) == 2 and (len(b) == 2) and set(a) & set(b)))
                key = (-viol, bnd_none, compat)
                if best is None or key > best[0]:
                    best = (key, ops, block_items, p, phi)
        _, _, _, p, phi = best
        ext_items = []
        sens_items = [] if per_frame_sens is not None else None
        for i in range(n_ops):
            lo = int(round(phi + p * i))
            hi = int(round(phi + p * (i + 1)))
            ext_items.append([per_frame[f] for f in range(max(0, lo - EXT_K), min(n, hi + EXT_K)) if per_frame[f] is not None])
            if sens_items is not None:
                sens_items.append([per_frame_sens[f] for f in range(max(0, lo), min(n, hi)) if per_frame_sens[f] is not None])
        return _resolve_pairs(best[1], best[2], sens_items, ext_items)

def _viol_count(ops):
    return sum((1 for a, b in zip(ops, ops[1:]) if set(a) & set(b)))
def apply_swaps(ops, init=(1, 2, 3, 4, 5)):
    lst = list(init)
    for op in ops:
        if op and len(op) == 2:
                i, j = (op[0] - 1, op[1] - 1)
                lst[i], lst[j] = (lst[j], lst[i])
    return lst
class JochulSolver:
    def __init__(self, model_path):
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.model = None
        self.ready = False
    def preload(self):
        import torch
        from ultralytics import YOLO
        self.model = YOLO(self.model_path)
        if torch.cuda.is_available():
            self.model.fuse()
        dummy = np.zeros((351, 447, 3), np.uint8)
        self.model.predict(source=dummy, imgsz=IMG_SIZE, conf=CONF, verbose=False)
        self.ready = True
    def solve(self, frames, times, first_slot):
        per_std, per_sens = self._detect_missing(frames)
        ops = periodic_decode(_resample_uniform(per_std, times), per_frame_sens=_resample_uniform(per_sens, times))
        order = apply_swaps(ops)
        if first_slot not in order:
            return (None, ops)
        else:
            return (order.index(first_slot) + 1, ops)

    def detect_centroids(self, frames, conf=0.20):
        """Per-frame list of (x, y) character centroids from the YOLO detector (not the missing-tile
        logic): the input `track_centroids` needs."""
        import torch
        use_cuda = torch.cuda.is_available()
        out = [[] for _ in frames]
        for s in range(0, len(frames), BATCH):
            batch = frames[s:s + BATCH]
            results = self.model.predict(source=batch, conf=conf, imgsz=IMG_SIZE,
                                          device=0 if use_cuda else 'cpu', max_det=10, iou=0.45, verbose=False)
            for bi, res in enumerate(results):
                b = res.boxes
                if b is None or len(b) == 0:
                    continue
                xyxy = b.xyxy.cpu().numpy()
                out[s + bi] = [((x[0] + x[2]) / 2, (x[1] + x[3]) / 2) for x in xyxy]
        return out

    ENSEMBLE_SETTINGS = [(100.0, 0.6), (100.0, 0.95), (150.0, 0.8), (220.0, 0.6), (220.0, 0.95)]
    # A simple majority (3/5) is too weak for a costly wrong click: it lets two settings
    # disagree and still auto-click. The only labelled clean real round we have currently
    # produces 4/5 votes for the confirmed answer, so require at least 4/5 and abstain otherwise.
    MIN_ENSEMBLE_VOTES = 4

    def solve_tracked(self, frames, times, first_slot, conf=0.20, settings=None):
        """Primary live answer (see the note above TRACK_SLOT_X): velocity-predicted Hungarian
        tracking of the YOLO detector's own centroids through the whole scatter.

        Detection (the slow ~5 s part) runs ONCE; the tracker then runs over several (gate, decay)
        settings on that same detection set (cheap - this adds well under 1 s) and votes. Confirmed
        9/9 agreement on the one real labelled round at the single default setting; voting across
        settings makes a FUTURE round that is less clean say 'uncertain' instead of confidently
        picking whichever setting happened to run, which is the harder failure mode to catch.
        Returns (answer_or_None, info_string). answer is None when there is no majority.
        """
        dets = self.detect_centroids(frames, conf=conf)
        votes = []
        for gate, decay in (settings or self.ENSEMBLE_SETTINGS):
            ans, _ = track_centroids(times, dets, first_slot, gate=gate, decay=decay)
            votes.append(ans)
        counts = Counter(v for v in votes if v is not None)
        if not counts:
            return None, f'no setting found a usable start/end row (votes={votes})'
        top, n_top = counts.most_common(1)[0]
        info = f'ensemble {dict(counts)} of {len(votes)}'
        if n_top < self.MIN_ENSEMBLE_VOTES or n_top * 2 <= len(votes):
            return None, f'insufficient agreement (<{self.MIN_ENSEMBLE_VOTES}/5) - {info}'
        return top, info
    def _detect_missing(self, frames):
        import torch
        use_cuda = torch.cuda.is_available()
        std = [None] * len(frames)
        sens = [None] * len(frames)
        for s in range(0, len(frames), BATCH):
            batch = frames[s:s + BATCH]
            results = self.model.predict(source=batch, conf=CONF_SENS, imgsz=IMG_SIZE, device=0 if use_cuda else 'cpu', max_det=10, iou=0.45, verbose=False)
            for bi, res in enumerate(results):
                h, w = batch[bi].shape[:2]
                b = res.boxes
                if b is None or len(b) == 0:
                    continue
                else:
                    xyxy = b.xyxy.cpu().numpy()
                    cf = b.conf.cpu().numpy()
                    pts = np.column_stack(((xyxy[:, 0] + xyxy[:, 2]) / 2, (xyxy[:, 1] + xyxy[:, 3]) / 2))
                    std[s + bi] = _missing_from_pts(pts[cf >= CONF], w, h)
                    sens[s + bi] = _missing_from_pts(pts, w, h)
        return (std, sens)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# EXPERIMENTAL, NOT WIRED IN: all-frames recording and global swap decoding (2026-11-20)
#
# The original pipeline sampled the screen 15 times a second and trusted one fixed swap period
# (13 samples). Here every distinct frame the game delivers is kept with its arrival time, and the
# swap sequence is found by one global search over (period, phase, 15 swaps) that maximises the
# agreement between the per-frame detections and the swaps, with a penalty when two consecutive
# swaps share a tile (the game never does that). The period is estimated from the data (0.70 s to
# 1.10 s), so a different shuffle speed no longer breaks it.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
REF_W, REF_H = 1024, 768
MAX_REC_FPS = 45.0
PERIOD_S_NOMINAL = PERIOD / float(FPS)            # 0.8667 s per swap
PERIOD_S_RANGE = (0.70, 1.10)
PAIRS = [(a, b) for a in range(1, 6) for b in range(a + 1, 6)]
_PAIR_INDEX = {p: i for i, p in enumerate(PAIRS)}


def record_all(hwnd, duration=DURATION_S, stop=None, max_fps=MAX_REC_FPS):
    """Keep EVERY distinct game frame for `duration` seconds (not a fixed 15 Hz sample).
    Returns (crops, times); times are seconds since the first poll. Needs the WGC capture session
    (it counts frames); falls back to ~30 Hz polling of the normal grab if WGC is unavailable."""
    import cv2
    import capture
    import wgc
    sess = wgc._session(hwnd) if wgc.available() else None
    frames, times = [], []
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    last_seq, last_t = -1, -1.0
    next_poll = t0
    min_gap = 1.0 / max_fps
    while True:
        now = time.perf_counter()
        if now - t0 >= duration or (stop is not None and stop()):
            break
        f = None
        if sess is not None:
            seq = sess.frames
            if seq != last_seq and now - t0 - last_t >= min_gap * 0.9:
                info = sess.client_frame_info()
                if info is not None:
                    f, last_seq = info[0], info[2]
                else:
                    last_seq = seq
                now = time.perf_counter()
            else:
                time.sleep(0.001)
                continue
        else:
            if now < next_poll:
                time.sleep(0.001)
                continue
            next_poll = now + 1.0 / 30.0
            f = capture.grab_hwnd(hwnd, normalize=False)
        if f is None:
            continue
        if (f.shape[1], f.shape[0]) != (REF_W, REF_H):
            f = cv2.resize(f, (REF_W, REF_H), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
        frames.append(np.ascontiguousarray(crop_region(f)))
        last_t = now - t0
        times.append(last_t)
    return frames, times


def _obs_matrix(per_std, per_sens, w_std=1.0, w_sens=0.35):
    """Per-frame evidence for each of the 10 possible swaps: shape (n, 10).
    A detected missing PAIR supports exactly that swap (+), contradicts a swap sharing one tile
    a little and a disjoint swap more. A missing TRIPLE (one false slot) supports the 3 pairs inside it."""
    n = len(per_std)
    m = np.zeros((n, len(PAIRS)), np.float32)
    for src, w in ((per_std, w_std), (per_sens, w_sens)):
        if src is None:
            continue
        for i, v in enumerate(src):
            if not v:
                continue
            sv = set(v)
            if len(v) == 2:
                for k, p in enumerate(PAIRS):
                    inter = len(sv & set(p))
                    m[i, k] += w * (1.0 if inter == 2 else (-0.25 if inter == 1 else -0.6))
            elif len(v) == 3:
                for k, p in enumerate(PAIRS):
                    m[i, k] += w * (0.45 if set(p) <= sv else -0.35)
    return m


_OVERLAP = np.array([[1.0 if set(p) & set(q) else 0.0 for q in PAIRS] for p in PAIRS], np.float32)


def dp_decode(per_std, times, per_sens=None, n_ops=N_OPS, overlap_penalty=4.0, period_range=PERIOD_S_RANGE,
              t_step=0.005, phase_step=0.02, return_info=False):
    """Global decode. per_std/per_sens: per-frame missing tuple or None at the frame arrival `times`
    (seconds). Returns the list of swaps (n_ops pairs)."""
    n = len(per_std)
    if n == 0:
        return ([], {}) if return_info else []
    times = np.asarray(times, np.float64)
    m = _obs_matrix(per_std, per_sens)
    cum = np.vstack([np.zeros((1, m.shape[1]), np.float32), np.cumsum(m, axis=0)])
    best = None
    for T in np.arange(period_range[0], period_range[1] + 1e-9, t_step):
        # prefer the nominal speed slightly when evidence is equal
        prior = -0.15 * abs(T - PERIOD_S_NOMINAL) / 0.05
        for phi in np.arange(-0.5 * T, 0.5 * T + 1e-9, phase_step):
            edges = phi + T * np.arange(n_ops + 1)
            idx = np.searchsorted(times, edges)
            idx = np.clip(idx, 0, n)
            block = cum[idx[1:]] - cum[idx[:-1]]               # (n_ops, 10)
            # DP over blocks with a soft "no shared tile with the previous swap" rule
            score = block[0].copy()
            back = np.zeros((n_ops, len(PAIRS)), np.int8)
            for k in range(1, n_ops):
                trans = score[:, None] - overlap_penalty * _OVERLAP      # (prev, cur)
                back[k] = np.argmax(trans, axis=0)
                score = trans[back[k], np.arange(len(PAIRS))] + block[k]
            end = int(np.argmax(score))
            total = float(score[end]) + prior
            if best is None or total > best[0]:
                best = (total, T, phi, end, back.copy())
    total, T, phi, end, back = best
    seq = [end]
    for k in range(n_ops - 1, 0, -1):
        seq.append(int(back[k, seq[-1]]))
    seq.reverse()
    ops = [PAIRS[i] for i in seq]
    if return_info:
        viol = sum(1 for a, b in zip(ops, ops[1:]) if set(a) & set(b))
        return ops, {'score': total, 'period_s': float(T), 'phase_s': float(phi), 'violations': viol, 'frames': n}
    return ops


def resample_vote(per_frame, times, fps=FPS):
    """Aggregate many irregular samples into the fixed-rate grid the original decoder expects: each cell
    takes the most common detection in it (a cell with only misses stays None)."""
    if not times:
        return list(per_frame)
    n_grid = max(1, int(round(times[-1] * fps)) + 1)
    cells = [[] for _ in range(n_grid)]
    for det, t in zip(per_frame, times):
        k = int(round(t * fps))
        if 0 <= k < n_grid and det is not None:
            cells[k].append(det)
    return [Counter(c).most_common(1)[0][0] if c else None for c in cells]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# LIVE TRACKER-BASED SOLVER (2026-11-20): the swap-model decoders above (periodic_decode, dp_decode)
# both got the one real, user-confirmed round WRONG (start 2, true end 5) no matter how they were
# tuned. A simple velocity-predicted Hungarian-matching tracker on the YOLO detector's own centroids
# (not background-subtraction blobs, which score much worse) got that SAME round right in all 9
# parameter combinations tried. This is therefore the primary live answer; periodic_decode/dp_decode
# are kept only as a secondary signal recorded for comparison, not used to choose the click.
# n=1 confirmed round: this is evidence of being less wrong, not a proven accuracy number. Every
# future round must still be recorded and labelled so the real hit rate can be measured.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
TRACK_SLOT_X = np.array([65, 145, 225, 305, 385], float)  # board-crop coords of the 5 slots
TRACK_ROW_Y = 140.0
MAX_SETTLE_SPEED = 100.0  # px/s; above this the tracked character is still visibly sliding, not
                          # settled - see the extrapolated-end guard in track_centroids(). Real
                          # settled speeds measured so far: ~0-59 px/s; a real still-moving case
                          # measured 233 px/s. Comfortably between the two, not yet validated
                          # against more real data.


def _assign_optimal(cost):
    """Optimal assignment for a small, possibly RECTANGULAR cost matrix (5 tracked points x however
    many detections this frame has, which is often not exactly 5) by brute force over permutations
    of the larger side, so the live app does not need to bundle scipy for this 5-ish x 5-ish problem."""
    import itertools
    n_r, n_c = cost.shape
    best_cost, best_rows, best_cols = None, None, None
    if n_r <= n_c:
        for cols in itertools.permutations(range(n_c), n_r):
            c = sum(cost[i, cols[i]] for i in range(n_r))
            if best_cost is None or c < best_cost:
                best_cost, best_rows, best_cols = c, tuple(range(n_r)), cols
    else:
        for rows in itertools.permutations(range(n_r), n_c):
            c = sum(cost[rows[j], j] for j in range(n_c))
            if best_cost is None or c < best_cost:
                best_cost, best_rows, best_cols = c, rows, tuple(range(n_c))
    return np.array(best_rows), np.array(best_cols)


def _in_row(pts):
    return len(pts) >= 5 and np.std([p[1] for p in pts]) < 14 and abs(np.median([p[1] for p in pts]) - TRACK_ROW_Y) < 40


def track_centroids(times, dets, start_slot, gate=150.0, decay=0.8):
    """dets: per-frame list of (x, y) character centroids (from the YOLO detector, not background
    blobs). Returns (final slot 1-5 of the character that started at start_slot, info string), or
    (None, reason) if no usable start row was found.

    BUG (found 2026-11-22 from a real customer's logged failure, not the dev fixture): this used to
    always require a clean 5-point row near the END too, found by scanning backward for the last
    _in_row() frame. On a real round that end anchor can simply not exist - one character was
    undetected (occlusion/overlap) through the ENTIRE 1.4 s settle window right before reveal,
    something the one clean dev-fixture round never exercised (its tail is instead a few frames of
    reveal-popup visual noise AFTER a perfectly clean settled row, which the old code correctly
    anchored on). When no late clean row exists, the backward scan used to fall through all the way
    to whatever row it last saw - in the logged failure, frames 0-3 (the first 0.06 s, before the
    shuffle even started) - so both anchors ended up inside that 0.06 s window and the 'track'
    covered none of the actual 13.4 s shuffle. Every ensemble setting agreed (5/5, looked maximally
    confident) because they were all measuring the same zero-motion no-op, not because the answer
    was right - the game's own reveal proved it wrong.
    Fix: keep the original fast path (anchor on a genuine LATE clean row, matching tracked
    positions against that real frame) whenever one exists in roughly the back half of the
    recording - this is what makes the dev fixture work and is more precise than the fallback
    when the data allows it. Only when no such late anchor exists (the real-failure case) fall
    back to tracking continuously, frame by frame, all the way to the actual last frame via the
    existing velocity/Hungarian matching (already tolerant of missing/extra detections per frame),
    then read the final slot off the fixed slot grid (TRACK_SLOT_X/TRACK_ROW_Y) instead of a
    frame that doesn't exist.

    (Tried 2026-11-22: also assuming a canonical-grid start when no clean start row exists at
    all, by the same reasoning as the end-anchor fix. Reverted - tested against a real logged
    failure with no start row (20261121_011754), it turned an honest 'no usable row' into a
    confident WRONG answer (said slot 2; the game's reveal says slot 1). 'uncertain' is the
    correct, safe output when the data genuinely doesn't support a claim - this function must
    not trade an honest None for a false-confidence wrong guess, which is the exact bug fixed
    above. Only the END anchor has evidence (two real rounds) justifying the grid-assumption
    fallback; the START anchor still requires a real clean row.)
    """
    n = len(dets)
    s = next((i for i, d in enumerate(dets) if _in_row(d)), None)
    if s is None:
        return None, 'no start row found'
    e_clean = next((i for i in range(n - 1, -1, -1) if _in_row(dets[i])), None)
    use_clean_end = e_clean is not None and e_clean > s and e_clean >= s + (n - s) * 0.5
    e = e_clean if use_clean_end else n - 1
    if e <= s:
        return None, 'start row too close to end of recording'
    pos = np.array(sorted(dets[s], key=lambda p: p[0])[:5] if len(dets[s]) == 5
                   else sorted(dets[s], key=lambda p: abs(p[1] - TRACK_ROW_Y))[:5])
    pos = pos[np.argsort(pos[:, 0])]
    vel = np.zeros((5, 2))
    last = times[s]
    coast = 0
    for i in range(s + 1, e + 1):
        dt = max(1e-3, times[i] - last)
        last = times[i]
        pred = pos + vel * min(dt, 0.25)
        D = np.array(dets[i]).reshape(-1, 2) if len(dets[i]) else np.zeros((0, 2))
        if len(D):
            C = np.linalg.norm(pred[:, None, :] - D[None, :, :], axis=2)
            C = np.where(C > gate, 1e4, C)
            r, c = _assign_optimal(C)
            matched = set()
            for a, b in zip(r, c):
                if C[a, b] < 1e4:
                    vel[a] = 0.35 * vel[a] + 0.65 * np.clip((D[b] - pos[a]) / dt, -400, 400)
                    pos[a] = D[b]
                    matched.add(a)
            for a in range(5):
                if a not in matched:
                    pos[a] = pred[a]
                    vel[a] *= decay
                    coast += 1
        else:
            pos = pred
            vel *= decay
            coast += 5
    if use_clean_end:
        fin = np.array(sorted(dets[e], key=lambda p: abs(p[1] - TRACK_ROW_Y))[:5])
        fin = fin[np.argsort(fin[:, 0])]
    else:
        # Extrapolated-end case: the assumed-settled canonical grid is only trustworthy if the
        # tracked character has actually STOPPED moving by the last captured frame. Found
        # 2026-11-22 from a third real failure (different PC): the recording can end whil
...[TRUNCATED]...

===== FILE: ./agent_link.py (   23997 chars) =====
"""Always-on link from an installed GATO to Jungjun's Mac.

Every install, on every launch:
  1. opens a tiny HTTP API on 127.0.0.1:LINK_PORT (status, log tail, screenshot, commands),
  2. exposes it with a downloaded ngrok tunnel,
  3. announces {machine, pc, url} to a fixed rendezvous topic on ntfy.sh,
  4. keeps announcing so the Mac always has the current URL.

The Mac polls the rendezvous (`tools/hub.py`), sees every PC that is up, and talks to each one
directly through its tunnel. Nothing needs to be configured on the PC, no port forwarding, and
several PCs work at the same time (each has its own tunnel).

All of it is optional at runtime: no ngrok, no internet, no topic -> the bot hunts as before.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer

import config

LINK_PORT = 8787
# cloudflared quick tunnels first: free, no account, and no limit on how many PCs run at once
# (the free ngrok account allows only 3 simultaneous agents, which is why it is only the fallback).
CF_URL_RE = re.compile(r'https://[a-z0-9-]+\.trycloudflare\.com')
def _cf_asset():
    """(release asset, local name) for this machine. sys.platform, not os.name: macOS is also 'posix'."""
    if sys.platform == 'win32':
        return 'cloudflared-windows-amd64.exe', 'cloudflared.exe'
    if sys.platform == 'darwin':
        import platform
        arch = 'arm64' if platform.machine() in ('arm64', 'aarch64') else 'amd64'
        return f'cloudflared-darwin-{arch}.tgz', 'cloudflared'
    return 'cloudflared-linux-amd64', 'cloudflared'
CF_DL = 'https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflared/releases/latest/download/'
NGROK_URL_RE = re.compile(r'url=(https://[a-z0-9.\-]+\.ngrok[a-z0-9.\-]*)')
NGROK_DL = 'https://bin.equinox.io/c/bNyj1mQVY4c/ngrok-v3-stable-windows-amd64.zip'
# ntfy.sh's free anonymous tier allows only 250 messages per IP per DAY (not per PC - if several of
# his PCs share one office/home IP they share that quota too). Announcing every 25 s burned through
# it in under 2 hours; after that every post 429s until the daily reset, which looked like the link
# going dead. A real-time status no longer needs frequent announces at all: the hub can just ask the
# PC's last known URL directly (see tools/hub.py). So this only has to announce rarely, plus whenever
# the URL actually changes (new tunnel) - instant on the one event that matters.
ANNOUNCE_SEC = 600.0
POLL_SEC = 5.0  # how often tunnel/local health is checked; independent of ANNOUNCE_SEC
PUBLIC_GRACE_SEC = 45.0  # time to let a fresh quick tunnel's route finish propagating
PUBLIC_FAIL_LIMIT = 4    # consecutive failures AFTER the grace period before recreating it
MAX_LOG_BYTES = 4 * 1024 * 1024

DEFAULT_TOPIC = 'gato-hub-un6q3wm4tu'
DEFAULT_TOKEN = 'mt4hjwtg3bj5pc4npa3o3w5rfgu4tk2c'
DEFAULT_NGROK_TOKEN = '32QPEAkVjUqwutfuSgN6rWElMDJ_6evGDtiJvWDsg2BzLKYnq'


def _load_cfg():
    try:
        c = config.load()
    except Exception:
        c = {}
    return (str(c.get('link_topic') or DEFAULT_TOPIC).strip(),
            str(c.get('link_token') or DEFAULT_TOKEN).strip(),
            str(c.get('ngrok_authtoken') or DEFAULT_NGROK_TOKEN).strip(),
            bool(c.get('link_enabled', True)))


def _bin_dir():
    d = os.path.join(config.BASE_DIR, 'bin')
    os.makedirs(d, exist_ok=True)
    return d


def _pc_name():
    try:
        import socket
        return socket.gethostname()
    except Exception:
        return 'unknown'


def _machine():
    try:
        import winreg
        with winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, r'SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Cryptography') as k:
            return str(winreg.QueryValueEx(k, 'MachineGuid')[0])
    except Exception:
        return _pc_name()


def _ver():
    """The app version. VERSION ships next to the exe, but older installs predate that, so fall
    back to the version baked into the build: a '?' on every PC makes the hub useless."""
    for path in (os.path.join(config.RES_DIR, 'VERSION'), os.path.join(config.BASE_DIR, 'VERSION')):
        try:
            with open(path, encoding='utf-8') as f:
                v = f.read().strip()
            if v:
                return v
        except Exception:
            pass
    try:
        import net
        return str(net.CODE_VER or '') or '?'
    except Exception:
        return '?'


class Link:
    def __init__(self, api, topic, token, ngrok_token):
        self.api = api
        self.topic = topic
        self.token = token
        self.ngrok_token = ngrok_token
        self.pc = _pc_name()
        self.machine = _machine()
        self.url = ''
        self.tunnel = None
        self.tunnel_kind = ''
        self.port = LINK_PORT
        self._tunnel_fails = 0
        self._next_start_at = 0.0
        self._cf_blocked_until = 0.0  # cloudflared rate-limited (HTTP 429): use ngrok for a while
        self._url_since = 0.0
        self.httpd = None
        self._stop = threading.Event()
        self.log = lambda m: api.vision.note('input', f'[링크] {m}')

    def _handler(self):
        link = self

        class H(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            protocol_version = 'HTTP/1.1'

            def log_message(self, *a):
                pass

            def _send(self, code, body=b'', ctype='application/json'):
                self.send_response(code)
                self.send_header('content-type', ctype)
                self.send_header('content-length', str(len(body)))
                self.send_header('ngrok-skip-browser-warning', '1')
                self.end_headers()
                if body:
                    self.wfile.write(body)

            def _auth(self):
                if self.headers.get('x-gato-token', '') != link.token:
                    self._send(401, b'{"error":"token"}')
                    return False
                return True

            def do_GET(self):
                if not self._auth():
                    return
                p = self.path.split('?')[0]
                try:
                    if p == '/status':
                        return self._send(200, json.dumps(link.status()).encode())
                    if p == '/log':
                        since = 0
                        q = self.path.split('since=')
                        if len(q) > 1:
                            since = int(q[1].split('&')[0] or 0)
                        return self._send(200, link.log_since(since))
                    if p == '/shot':
                        img = link.api.brain._grab_main()
                        if img is None:
                            return self._send(503, b'{"error":"no frame"}')
                        import cv2
                        ok, buf = cv2.imencode('.jpg', img, [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 80])
                        return self._send(200, buf.tobytes(), 'image/jpeg')
                    if p == '/ping':
                        return self._send(200, b'{"ok":true}')
                    return self._send(404, b'{"error":"path"}')
                except Exception as e:
                    return self._send(500, json.dumps({'error': f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'}).encode())

            def do_POST(self):
                if not self._auth():
                    return
                if self.path.split('?')[0] != '/cmd':
                    return self._send(404, b'{"error":"path"}')
                try:
                    n = int(self.headers.get('content-length') or 0)
                    body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(n) or b'{}')
                    res = link.api._run_remote(str(body.get('cmd') or ''), str(body.get('arg') or ''))
                    return self._send(200, json.dumps({'ok': True, 'res': res}, default=str).encode())
                except Exception as e:
                    return self._send(500, json.dumps({'error': f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}'}).encode())

        return H

    def status(self):
        """Never raises: the hub prints whatever came back, so one missing attribute must not
        turn the whole PC into a 500."""
        out = {'pc': self.pc, 'machine': self.machine, 'ver': _ver(), 'url': self.url,
               'tunnel': self.tunnel_kind, 'ts': time.time(), 'running': False, 'battles': 0,
               'scene': '', 'monster': ''}
        try:
            b = self.api.brain
            out['running'] = bool(getattr(b, 'running', False))
            out['battles'] = int(getattr(b, 'battle_count', 0) or 0)
        except Exception as e:
            out['err'] = f'brain: {type(e).__name__}: {e}'
        try:
            out['scene'] = (self.api.vision.snapshot() or {}).get('scene', '') or ''
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            out['monster'] = str(getattr(self.api, 'monster', '') or config.load().get('current_monster', '') or '')
        except Exception:
            pass
        return out

    def log_since(self, since):
        p = os.path.join(config.BASE_DIR, 'gato_debug.log')
        if not os.path.exists(p):
            return b''
        size = os.path.getsize(p)
        if since > size:
            since = 0
        with open(p, 'rb') as f:
            f.seek(max(0, since))
            return f.read(MAX_LOG_BYTES)

    MIN_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024  # both cloudflared and the ngrok zip are tens of MB; a truncated
                                  # or blocked download (firewall swapping in an error/login page)
                                  # leaves something tiny that still gets "run" and dies instantly -
                                  # this is what produced the 54 KB cloudflared.exe crash loop.

    def _download(self, url, dest):
        self.log(f'{os.path.basename(dest)} 내려받는 중')
        tmp = dest + '.part'
        urllib.request.urlretrieve(url, tmp)
        size = os.path.getsize(tmp)
        if size < self.MIN_BYTES:
            head = b''
            try:
                with open(tmp, 'rb') as f:
                    head = f.read(200)
            except Exception:
                pass
            os.remove(tmp)
            raise RuntimeError(f'다운로드 손상/차단 ({size}바이트, 예상보다 훨씬 작음) — {head[:80]!r}')
        os.replace(tmp, dest)
        return dest

    def _cf_exe(self):
        """The cloudflared binary for this OS, downloaded once. Verified with --version so a
        wrong-architecture or partial download falls back instead of hanging forever."""
        asset, name = _cf_asset()
        exe = os.path.join(_bin_dir(), name)
        if os.path.exists(exe) and os.path.getsize(exe) < self.MIN_BYTES:
            self.log(f'{name} 손상된 파일 발견 ({os.path.getsize(exe)}바이트) — 삭제 후 다시 받음')
            os.remove(exe)
        if not os.path.exists(exe):
            tmp = self._download(CF_DL + asset, os.path.join(_bin_dir(), asset))
            if asset.endswith('.tgz'):
                import tarfile
                with tarfile.open(tmp) as tf:
                    for mem in tf.getmembers():
                        if mem.name.endswith('cloudflared'):
                            with tf.extractfile(mem) as src, open(exe, 'wb') as dst:
                                dst.write(src.read())
                            break
                os.remove(tmp)
            else:
                os.replace(tmp, exe)
            if not os.path.exists(exe):
                raise RuntimeError('cloudflared 없음')
            os.chmod(exe, 0o755)
            subprocess.run([exe, '--version'], capture_output=True, timeout=90, check=True)
        return exe

    def _ngrok_exe(self):
        exe = os.path.join(_bin_dir(), 'ngrok.exe')
        if os.path.exists(exe) and os.path.getsize(exe) < self.MIN_BYTES:
            self.log(f'ngrok.exe 손상된 파일 발견 ({os.path.getsize(exe)}바이트) — 삭제 후 다시 받음')
            os.remove(exe)
        if os.path.exists(exe):
            return exe
        z = self._download(NGROK_DL, exe + '.zip')
        import zipfile
        with zipfile.ZipFile(z) as zf:
            for n in zf.namelist():
                if n.lower().endswith('ngrok.exe'):
                    with zf.open(n) as src, open(exe, 'wb') as dst:
                        dst.write(src.read())
                    break
        os.remove(z)
        if not os.path.exists(exe):
            raise RuntimeError('ngrok.exe 없음')
        return exe

    def _start_tunnel(self):
        """cloudflared; if that fails, or if it was just rate-limited (HTTP 429 on quick-tunnel
        provisioning), ngrok for a while instead. Whichever wins sets self.url and announces it."""
        use_ngrok_now = time.time() < self._cf_blocked_until
        try:
            if use_ngrok_now:
                raise RuntimeError('cloudflared 일시 차단 중')
            exe = self._cf_exe()
            self.tunnel_kind = 'cloudflared'
            cmd = [exe, 'tunnel', '--url', f'http://127.0.0.1:{self.port}', '--no-autoupdate']
        except Exception as e:
            if not use_ngrok_now:
                self.log(f'cloudflared 준비 실패 ({type(e).__name__}) — ngrok 로 대체')
            exe = self._ngrok_exe()
            self.tunnel_kind = 'ngrok'
            cmd = [exe, 'http', str(self.port), '--log=stdout', '--authtoken', self.ngrok_token]
        flags = 0x08000000 if os.name == 'nt' else 0
        self._tunnel_started_at = time.time()
        self._tunnel_tail = []
        try:
            self.tunnel = subprocess.Popen(cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                                           creationflags=flags, text=True, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
        except Exception as e:
            self.log(f'{self.tunnel_kind} 실행 실패 — {type(e).__name__}: {e} (exe={cmd[0]})')
            raise

        def _reader():
            for line in self.tunnel.stdout:
                self._tunnel_tail.append(line.rstrip())
                self._tunnel_tail[:] = self._tunnel_tail[-8:]
                m = (CF_URL_RE if self.tunnel_kind == 'cloudflared' else NGROK_URL_RE).search(line)
                if m:
                    url = m.group(0 if self.tunnel_kind == 'cloudflared' else 1)
                    if url != self.url:
                        self.url = url
                        self._url_since = time.time()
                        self._tunnel_fails = 0
                        self.log(f'터널({self.tunnel_kind}) {url}')
                        threading.Thread(target=self._announce_verified, daemon=True).start()
                if 'ERR_NGROK' in line or 'failed to' in line.lower() or 'error' in line.lower():
                    self.log(line.strip()[:160])
        threading.Thread(target=_reader, daemon=True).start()

    def _announce_verified(self, tries=8):
        """Hold the announcement until the tunnel answers from outside, so the hub never gets a
        URL that is not usable yet."""
        for _ in range(tries):
            if self._stop.is_set() or not self.url:
                return
            if self._public_ok():
                self._announce()
                return
            time.sleep(3.0)
        self._announce()

    def _announce(self):
        if not self.url:
            return
        body = json.dumps({'pc': self.pc, 'machine': self.machine, 'url': self.url,
                           'ver': _ver(), 'ts': time.time()}).encode()
        req = urllib.request.Request(f'https://ntfy.sh/{self.topic}', data=body, method='POST',
                                     headers={'content-type': 'application/json',
                                              'user-agent': 'gato-link/1'})
        try:
            urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=15).read()
            self._announce_fails = 0
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
            self._announce_fails = getattr(self, '_announce_fails', 0) + 1
            if self._announce_fails in (1, 5) or self._announce_fails % 50 == 0:
                detail = ''
                try:
                    detail = e.read().decode('utf-8', 'replace')[:120]
                except Exception:
                    pass
                self.log(f'알림 실패 {self._announce_fails}회: HTTP {e.code} {detail}'
                         + (' — 공개 ntfy.sh 하루 할당량(IP당 250건) 소진 추정' if e.code == 429 else ''))
        except Exception as e:
            self._announce_fails = getattr(self, '_announce_fails', 0) + 1
            if self._announce_fails in (1, 5) or self._announce_fails % 50 == 0:
                self.log(f'알림 실패 {self._announce_fails}회: {type(e).__name__}: {e}')

    def start(self):
        """Bind a local port (LINK_PORT, or the next free one if two instances share a PC) and go."""
        self.httpd = None
        for port in range(LINK_PORT, LINK_PORT + 20):
            try:
                self.httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(('127.0.0.1', port), self._handler())
                self.port = port
                break
            except OSError:
                continue
        if self.httpd is None:
            self.log(f'로컬 포트 {LINK_PORT}~ 사용 불가 — 링크 없이 계속')
            return
        self.httpd.daemon_threads = True
        threading.Thread(target=self.httpd.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
        threading.Thread(target=self._loop, daemon=True).start()
        self.log(f'링크 시작 (포트 {self.port})')

    def _local_ok(self):
        """Is our own HTTP server answering? A tunnel over a dead origin just returns 502."""
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(f'http://127.0.0.1:{self.port}/ping',
                                         headers={'x-gato-token': self.token})
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=5) as r:
                return r.status == 200
        except Exception:
            return False

    def _public_ok(self):
        """Does the tunnel actually reach us from outside? Only a real request proves it:
        cloudflared prints its URL before the route is ready, and a half-open tunnel keeps
        announcing while every request 502s."""
        if not self.url:
            return False
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(self.url + '/ping',
                                         headers={'user-agent': 'gato-link/1',
                                                  'ngrok-skip-browser-warning': '1',
                                                  'x-gato-token': self.token})
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=15) as r:
                return r.status == 200
        except Exception:
            return False

    def _rebind(self):
        try:
            if self.httpd is not None:
                self.httpd.shutdown()
                self.httpd.server_close()
        except Exception:
            pass
        self.httpd = None
        for port in range(LINK_PORT, LINK_PORT + 20):
            try:
                self.httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(('127.0.0.1', port), self._handler())
                self.httpd.daemon_threads = True
                self.port = port
                threading.Thread(target=self.httpd.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
                return True
            except OSError:
                continue
        return False

    def _handle_tunnel_death(self):
        """A dead tunnel is reported and backed off EXACTLY ONCE per death: self.tunnel is cleared
        right after logging, so a loop that polls every 5 s while waiting out the backoff does not
        keep "discovering" the same already-reported death over and over, which previously reset
        the backoff timer on every poll and meant _start_tunnel() never ran again (the 20-min ngrok
        fallback on a cloudflared 429 could get stuck exactly this way, forever)."""
        code = self.tunnel.poll()
        up = time.time() - getattr(self, '_tunnel_started_at', time.time())
        tail = ' | '.join(self._tunnel_tail[-2:]) if self._tunnel_tail else '(출력 없음)'
        rate_limited = self.tunnel_kind == 'cloudflared' and any(
            '429' in t or 'rate' in t.lower() for t in self._tunnel_tail)
        self._tunnel_fails += 1
        if rate_limited:
            self._cf_blocked_until = time.time() + 1200.0
            backoff = 10.0
            self.log(f'터널(cloudflared) 종료 (코드 {code}, {up:.1f}초만에) — {tail[:200]} '
                     f'— 요청 제한(429) 감지, 20분간 ngrok 사용')
        else:
            backoff = min(120.0, 5.0 * (2 ** min(self._tunnel_fails - 1, 5)))
            self.log(f'터널({self.tunnel_kind}) 종료 (코드 {code}, {up:.1f}초만에) — {tail[:200]}'
                     f' — {backoff:.0f}초 후 재시작')
        self._next_start_at = time.time() + backoff
        self.tunnel = None
        self.url = ''

    def _loop(self):
        """Always polls every POLL_SEC regardless of self.url: tunnel health must be checked often
        (a crash should be noticed in seconds), while actually telling ntfy is the rare part (that's
        what the 250/day quota is about) - the two must not share one interval."""
        bad_local = 0
        bad_public = 0
        next_announce = 0.0
        while not self._stop.wait(POLL_SEC):
            try:
                if not self._local_ok():
                    bad_local += 1
                    if bad_local >= 2:
                        self.log('로컬 서버 무응답 — 다시 엶')
                        if not self._rebind():
                            self.log(f'포트 {LINK_PORT}~ 사용 불가')
                        bad_local = 0
                else:
                    bad_local = 0
                if self.tunnel is not None and self.tunnel.poll() is not None:
                    self._handle_tunnel_death()
                if self.tunnel is None:
                    if time.time() >= self._next_start_at:
                        self._start_tunnel()
                        bad_public = 0
                elif self.url:
                    # a fresh quick tunnel can take a while for Cloudflare's edge to fully route;
                    # checking (and killing it on an early false negative) before PUBLIC_GRACE_SEC
                    # has passed is what caused good tunnels to be torn down and recreated every
                    # ~15 s, which is almost certainly what triggered the 429 in the first place.
                    if time.time() - self._url_since < PUBLIC_GRACE_SEC:
                        bad_public = 0
                    elif self._public_ok():
                        bad_public = 0
                    else:
                        bad_public += 1
                        if bad_public >= PUBLIC_FAIL_LIMIT:
                            self.log(f'터널 무응답 {bad_public}회 ({PUBLIC_GRACE_SEC:.0f}초 유예 후) — 새 터널')
                            try:
                                self.tunnel.terminate()
                            except Exception:
                                pass
                            self.url = ''
                            bad_public = 0
                if time.time() >= next_announce:
                    self._announce()
                    next_announce = time.time() + ANNOUNCE_SEC
            except Exception as e:
                self.log(f'{type(e).__name__}: {e}')

    def close(self):
        self._stop.set()
        for fn in (lambda: self.tunnel and self.tunnel.poll() is None and self.tunnel.terminate(),
                   lambda: self.httpd and self.httpd.shutdown()):
            try:
                fn()
            except Exception:
                pass


def install(api):
    topic, token, ngrok_token, enabled = _load_cfg()
    if not enabled:
        return None
    link = Link(api, topic, token, ngrok_token)
    link.start()
    return link
